Now let me compile a comprehensive report of the conversation management system:

## Comprehensive Codebase Analysis: Conversation Management

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's the complete picture of how conversations are managed:

### **1. FRONTEND CONVERSATION ARCHITECTURE**

#### AgentContext.tsx (Frontend State Management)
**Location:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/contexts/AgentContext.tsx`

The `AgentProvider` component manages all conversation state using React hooks:

**Key State Variables:**
- `messages: ChatMessage[]` - All messages in the current conversation
- `isStreaming: boolean` - Whether agent is currently responding
- `sessionIdRef.current` - Agent session ID (updated after first response)
- `pendingHistoryRef.current` - Conversation history from deleted messages (used as fallback context)

**Important Ref Pattern:**
The component uses refs heavily to persist state across renders without triggering updates:
- `sessionIdRef` - Persists the Claude agent session ID
- `textBufferRef` - Buffers streamed text chunks before flushing
- `segmentsRef` - Accumulates content segments (thinking, tool calls, answers)
- `assistantIdRef` - Tracks which assistant message is currently streaming

**Critical Issue - Conversation History:**
Lines 78-84 build conversation history from current messages for each new message:
```typescript
const history = messages
  .filter((m) => m.role === 'user' || m.role === 'assistant')
  .map((m) => ({ role: m.role, content: m.content }));
```

This is **recreated on every sendMessage call** from the current in-memory `messages` state. When switching conversations, if messages aren't properly isolated per conversation, this could leak history between conversations.

**Session Handling:**
- `useSessionId()` provides the browser session ID (X-Session-ID header) - this is per-browser-tab
- `sessionIdRef.current` stores the Claude agent session ID - used for resuming multi-turn conversations
- When a message is deleted, `pendingHistoryRef.current` stores the remaining history to use as context for the next message

#### Message Structure (types.ts)
**Location:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/types.ts`

```typescript
interface ChatMessage {
  id: string;
  role: 'user' | 'assistant';
  content: string;
  toolCalls?: ToolCallResult[];
  segments?: ContentSegment[];
  isStreaming?: boolean;
  currentPhase?: 'thinking' | 'answer';
}
```

Messages are stored in flat array with segments containing thinking, tool calls, charts, and answers.

---

### **2. FRONTEND UI COMPONENTS**

**AgentPanel.tsx** - Displays all messages in a scrollable container
- Calls `useAgent()` to get `messages` and `clearMessages`
- Uses a scroll-to-bottom mechanism on new messages

**MessageBubble.tsx** - Renders individual messages
- Displays user questions and assistant responses
- Handles edit/delete operations on user messages
- Renders thinking blocks, tool results, charts inline

**ChatInput.tsx** - User message input
- Calls `sendMessage(text)` from AgentContext
- Handles slash commands for skills

**Flow:** ChatInput → sendMessage() → AgentProvider updates messages state → AgentPanel rerenders

---

### **3. BACKEND SESSION ARCHITECTURE**

#### Session Management (session_manager.py)
**Location:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/session_manager.py`

```python
class SessionManager:
    _sessions: dict[str, SessionEntry]  # session_id -> (DuckDB connection, last_seen)
    
    def get_or_create(session_id: str) -> Database:
        # Creates new DuckDB file: /tmp/duckdb-data-agent-{session_id}.duckdb
        # Returns Database connection per session
        
    def cleanup_stale(ttl_seconds=300):
        # Removes sessions inactive for 5 minutes
```

**Important:** The browser's `X-Session-ID` header (generated per tab) maps to a DuckDB instance per session. This provides data isolation.

#### Chat Endpoints (chat.py)
**Location:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/routes/chat.py`

Two main endpoints:
1. **POST /api/chat** - Normal message (has optional `session_id` for resuming Claude agent)
2. **POST /api/chat/edit** - Edit a message (always starts fresh, passes conversation history)

Both accept:
- `message: str` - User's message
- `session_id: str | None` - Claude agent session ID for resuming
- `conversation_history: list[dict]` - Previous messages for context
- `langfuse_session_id: str | None` - Tracing ID

**Stream Implementation:**
Returns `StreamingResponse` with SSE events (text chunks, tool calls, thinking, charts, etc.)

---

### **4. SSE STREAMING / AGENT SERVICE**

#### agentService.ts (Frontend)
**Location:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/agent/agentService.ts`

The `runAgentLoop()` function:
1. POSTs to `/api/chat` with user message + conversation history
2. Opens SSE connection and parses events
3. Calls callbacks for each event type:
   - `onTextChunk()` - streaming text
   - `onThinkingDone()` - end of thinking phase
   - `onToolCall()` - tool invocation
   - `onToolResult()` - tool result with data
   - `onSubagentStart/End()` - delegated SQL analyst work
   - `onDone()` - final session ID
   - `onError()` - error handling

**Key insight:** The frontend agentService purely streams SSE events - it doesn't manage conversation state. The AgentProvider wraps this to update messages state.

#### agent.py (Backend - SSE Stream)
**Location:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/agent.py` (lines 164-602)

The `stream_chat()` async generator:
1. Launches TypeScript sidecar container (reused per `backend_session_id`)
2. POSTs payload to sidecar with:
   - `message` - the user's message
   - `session_id` - Claude agent session ID (for resume)
   - `conversation_history` - previous messages (fallback if session expired)
   - `system_prompt` - current table schemas
3. Reads SSE events from sidecar, transforms them, and yields to frontend

**Critical logic (lines 268-274):**
```python
# Always pass conversation_history so the sidecar can use it as fallback
# if resume fails (e.g. container was recreated and session is gone).
if conversation_history:
    payload["original_message"] = message
payload["conversation_history"] = conversation_history or []
```

The backend **always sends conversation_history**, allowing the sidecar to resume even if the Claude agent session is gone.

---

### **5. SIDECAR CONTAINER LIFECYCLE**

**Location:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/container_manager.py`

The TypeScript sidecar (Docker container) runs the Claude Agent SDK:
- **Keyed by:** `backend_session_id` (the browser X-Session-ID)
- **Reused across:** Multiple API calls from the same browser tab
- **Idle timeout:** SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS (kills idle containers)
- **Cleanup:** Background loop removes expired containers

When a request comes in:
1. Backend calls `container_manager.create(stable_session, env)` 
2. `stable_session = backend_session_id or session_id or "default"`
3. If container already exists for that session, it's reused
4. Container is "touched" (idle timer reset) on each message

---

### **6. CONVERSATION HISTORY FLOW**

**New Message Scenario:**
```
Frontend:
  messages = [user1, assistant1, user2, assistant2]  (in-memory state)
  → sendMessage("user3")
  → builds history from messages array
  → POST /api/chat with:
    - message: "user3"
    - session_id: last Claude agent session ID
    - conversation_history: [{role: user, content: user1}, {role: assistant, ...}, {role: user, content: user2}, ...]

Backend:
  → stream_chat(message, session_id, conversation_history)
  → If session_id exists: use it (resume prior Claude agent)
  → If not or fails: sidecar uses conversation_history as fallback
  → Sidecar SDK runs with this context
  → Yields SSE events back to frontend
  
Frontend:
  → parseSSE events
  → update messages state
  → save new session_id from done event
```

**Edit Message Scenario:**
```
Frontend:
  messages = [user1, assistant1, user2, assistant2, user3]
  → editMessage(index=2, newContent)  // editing user2
  → builds history from messages[0:2] = [user1, assistant1]
  → POST /api/chat/edit with conversation_history
  
Backend:
  → stream_chat(message, session_id=null, conversation_history=[user1, assistant1])
  → Always starts fresh session (session_id=null)
  → But sidecar has [user1, assistant1] context
  → Creates new assistant message based on edited user message
```

**Delete Message Scenario:**
```
Frontend:
  → deleteMessage(index=2)  // delete user2
  → setMessages(messages.slice(0, 2))  // [user1, assistant1]
  → pendingHistoryRef.current = [{role: user, content: user1}, {role: assistant, ...}]
  → Next sendMessage() uses pendingHistoryRef as conversation_history fallback
  → Also creates new langfuse_session_id for fresh trace
```

---

### **7. POTENTIAL STATE MANAGEMENT ISSUES**

#### **Issue 1: No Multi-Conversation Support**
The AgentContext uses a single `messages` state for THE conversation. There's no:
- Conversation ID management
- Conversation list UI
- Ability to create/switch conversations
- Per-conversation message storage

Current commits indicate this feature is being worked on ("session-scoped conversations").

#### **Issue 2: Agent Session ID Persistence**
The `sessionIdRef.current` is in AgentProvider but is NOT tied to a conversation ID. If multiple conversations exist, they'd share the same agent session, causing message mixing.

**Current behavior:** Only one conversation at a time in memory, so this doesn't manifest. But if multi-conversation support is added without refactoring, this could cause cross-conversation leaks.

#### **Issue 3: Conversation History Ref Semantics**
The `pendingHistoryRef.current` is cleared after the next message is sent:
```typescript
const pendingHistory = pendingHistoryRef.current;
pendingHistoryRef.current = null;  // Line 84
```

If a user deletes a message and then navigates away before sending another message, the deleted history is lost. This might be intentional but is worth noting.

#### **Issue 4: Sidecar Container Reuse**
Containers are keyed by `backend_session_id` (X-Session-ID header), which is **browser-tab specific**. This means:
- Switching conversations within the same tab reuses the same container
- Container state (if any) persists across conversation switches
- The conversation_history passed to each request is what isolates conversations, not the container itself

---

### **8. STREAMING WITH MESSAGE STATE**

When streaming a response:

1. **User sends message** → New assistant message added with `isStreaming: true`
2. **SSE chunks arrive**:
   - `onTextChunk()` → appends to `textBufferRef.current`
   - Every 50ms: `flushText()` → updates message `content` via setMessages()
3. **Tool calls/results** → accumulated in `segmentsRef.current` → setMessages() updates
4. **Thinking phase ends** → `onThinkingDone()` → moves segment to segments array, switches to answer phase
5. **Stream ends** → `onDone()` → sets `isStreaming: false`, updates session ID

**Ref pattern ensures:**
- TextBuffer doesn't cause unnecessary re-renders during rapid text chunks
- Segments accumulate without losing state between callbacks
- Session ID from final done event persists in ref

---

|------|---------|----------|
| `/frontend/src/contexts/AgentContext.tsx` | Main conversation state, message management | Center of state mgmt - MODIFIED per git status |
| `/frontend/src/agent/agentService.ts` | SSE streaming, event parsing | Connects frontend to backend streaming |
| `/backend/app/agent.py:stream_chat()` | Backend SSE generator, event transformation | Orchestrates sidecar + event streaming |
| `/backend/app/routes/chat.py` | HTTP endpoints for chat/edit | Entry points for frontend messages |
| `/backend/app/session_manager.py` | Per-session DuckDB connections | Data isolation per browser tab |
| `/backend/app/container_manager.py` | TypeScript sidecar lifecycle | Manages Claude Agent SDK containers |
| `/frontend/src/components/AgentPanel.tsx` | Message list UI | Displays all messages |
| `/frontend/src/types.ts` | ChatMessage, ContentSegment types | Data structure definitions |

---

### **10. MODIFICATION INDICATED IN GIT STATUS**

The modified file `frontend/src/contexts/AgentContext.tsx` likely contains changes to support multi-conversation features. The current version uses refs for session management which may need refactoring to support per-conversation session tracking.

This analysis shows a robust streaming architecture with proper message buffering and state management, but explicit multi-conversation support (create, list, switch) is not yet implemented in the UI layer. The backend is designed to handle it via separate session IDs and conversation_history payloads.

   276→              prev.map((m) =>
   277→                m.id === assistantId
   278→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   279→                  : m
   280→              )
   281→            );
   282→            setIsStreaming(false);
   283→            abortRef.current = null;
   284→          },
   285→          onError: (error) => {
   286→            if (activeConversationIdRef.current !== conversationId) {
   287→              if (conversationId) messagesCacheRef.current.delete(conversationId);
   288→              return;
   289→            }
   290→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   291→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   292→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   293→            }
   294→            flushText();
   295→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   296→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   297→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   298→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   299→            }
   300→            segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'error', errorMessage: error });
   301→            setMessages((prev) =>
   302→              prev.map((m) =>
   303→                m.id === assistantId
   304→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   305→                  : m
   306→              )
   307→            );
   308→            setIsStreaming(false);
   309→            abortRef.current = null;
   310→          },
   311→          onUserQuestion: (data) => {
   312→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   313→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   314→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   315→            }
   316→            flushText();
   317→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   318→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   319→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   320→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   321→            }
   322→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   323→              type: 'user_question',
   324→              questionData: data,
   325→            });
   326→            setMessages((prev) =>
   327→              prev.map((m) =>
   328→                m.id === assistantId
   329→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   330→                  : m
   331→              )
   332→            );
   333→          },
   334→        },
   335→        controller.signal,
   336→        userSessionId,
   337→        skills,
   338→      );
   339→    },
   340→    [isStreaming, flushText, refreshTables, userSessionId]
   341→  );
   342→
   343→  const editMessage = useCallback(
   344→    async (messageIndex: number, newContent: string) => {
   345→      if (isStreaming) return;
   346→
   347→      // Build conversation history from messages before the edit point
   348→      const conversationHistory: { role: string; content: string }[] = [];
   349→      for (let i = 0; i < messageIndex; i++) {
   350→        const msg = messages[i];
   351→        if (msg.role === 'user' || msg.role === 'assistant') {
   352→          conversationHistory.push({ role: msg.role, content: msg.content });
   353→        }
   354→      }
   355→
   356→      const assistantId = generateId();
   357→      assistantIdRef.current = assistantId;
   358→
   359→      const userMsg: ChatMessage = {
   360→        id: generateId(),
   361→        role: 'user',
   362→        content: newContent,
   363→      };
   364→      const assistantMsg: ChatMessage = {
   365→        id: assistantId,
   366→        role: 'assistant',
   367→        content: '',
   368→        toolCalls: [],
   369→        isStreaming: true,
   370→      };
   371→
   372→      setMessages((prev) => [...prev.slice(0, messageIndex), userMsg, assistantMsg]);
   373→      setIsStreaming(true);
   374→      textBufferRef.current = '';
   375→      segmentsRef.current = [];
   376→      currentTextRef.current = '';
   377→      phaseRef.current = 'thinking';
   378→
   379→      const controller = new AbortController();
   380→      abortRef.current = controller;
   381→
   382→      await runAgentEditLoop(
   383→        newContent,
   384→        conversationHistory,
   385→        generateUUID(),
   386→        {
   387→          onTextChunk: (chunk) => {
   388→            textBufferRef.current += chunk;
   389→            if (!flushTimerRef.current) {
   390→              flushTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
   391→                flushText();
   392→                flushTimerRef.current = null;
   393→              }, 50);
   394→            }
   395→          },
   396→          onThinkingDone: () => {
   397→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   398→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   399→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   400→            }
   401→            flushText();
   402→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   403→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'thinking', text: currentTextRef.current });
   404→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   405→            }
   406→            phaseRef.current = 'answer';
   407→            setMessages((prev) =>
   408→              prev.map((m) =>
   409→                m.id === assistantId
   410→                  ? { ...m, currentPhase: 'answer', segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   411→                  : m
   412→              )
   413→            );
   414→          },
   415→          onToolCall: (pending: ToolCallResult) => {
   416→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   417→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   418→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   419→            }
   420→            flushText();
   421→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   422→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   423→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   424→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   425→            }
   426→            segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'tool', toolResult: pending });
   427→            setMessages((prev) =>
   428→              prev.map((m) =>
   429→                m.id === assistantId
   430→                  ? { ...m, currentPhase: 'answer', segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   431→                  : m
   432→              )
   433→            );
   434→          },
   435→          onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => {
   436→            const pendingIdx = segmentsRef.current.findIndex(
   437→              (s) => s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.toolCallId === result.toolCallId
   438→            );
   439→            if (pendingIdx !== -1) {
   440→              const pending = segmentsRef.current[pendingIdx].toolResult!;
   441→              segmentsRef.current[pendingIdx] = {
   442→                type: 'tool',
   443→                toolResult: {
   444→                  ...pending,
   445→                  ...result,
   446→                  sql: result.sql || pending.sql,
   447→                  toolName: result.toolName || pending.toolName,
   448→                  command: result.command || pending.command,
   449→                  toolInput: result.toolInput || pending.toolInput,
   450→                },
   451→              };
   452→            } else {
   453→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'tool', toolResult: result });
   454→            }
   455→            setMessages((prev) =>
   456→              prev.map((m) =>
   457→                m.id === assistantId
   458→                  ? { ...m, toolCalls: [...(m.toolCalls || []), result], segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   459→                  : m
   460→              )
   461→            );
   462→            refreshTables();
   463→          },
   464→          onSubagentStart: (data) => {
   465→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   466→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   467→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   468→            }
   469→            flushText();
   470→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   471→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   472→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   473→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   474→            }
   475→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   476→              type: 'subagent_start',
   477→              subagentId: data.id,
   478→              subagentName: data.name,
   479→              text: data.prompt,
   480→            });
   481→            setMessages((prev) =>
   482→              prev.map((m) =>
   483→                m.id === assistantId
   484→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   485→                  : m
   486→              )
   487→            );
   488→          },
   489→          onSubagentEnd: (data) => {
   490→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   491→              type: 'subagent_end',
   492→              subagentId: data.id,
   493→              subagentName: data.name,
   494→              chart_spec: data.chart_spec,
   495→              sqlResults: data.sql_results,
   496→              text: data.result,
   497→            });
   498→            setMessages((prev) =>
   499→              prev.map((m) =>
   500→                m.id === assistantId
   501→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   502→                  : m
   503→              )
   504→            );
   505→          },
   506→          onDone: (newSessionId) => {
   507→            // Guard: if refs were taken over by another stream, bail out
   508→            if (assistantIdRef.current !== assistantId) return;
   509→            if (newSessionId) sessionIdRef.current = newSessionId;
   510→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   511→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   512→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   513→            }
   514→            flushText();
   515→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   516→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'answer', text: currentTextRef.current });
   517→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   518→            }
   519→            setMessages((prev) =>
   520→              prev.map((m) =>
   521→                m.id === assistantId
   522→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   523→                  : m
   524→              )
   525→            );
   526→            setIsStreaming(false);
   527→            abortRef.current = null;
   528→          },
   529→          onError: (error) => {
   530→            if (assistantIdRef.current !== assistantId) return;
   531→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   532→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   533→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   534→            }
   535→            flushText();
   536→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   537→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   538→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   539→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   540→            }
   541→            segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'error', errorMessage: error });
   542→            setMessages((prev) =>
   543→              prev.map((m) =>
   544→                m.id === assistantId
   545→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   546→                  : m
   547→              )
   548→            );
   549→            setIsStreaming(false);
   550→            abortRef.current = null;
   551→          },
   552→          onUserQuestion: (data) => {
   553→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   554→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   555→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   556→            }
   557→            flushText();
   558→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   559→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   560→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   561→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   562→            }
   563→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   564→              type: 'user_question',
   565→              questionData: data,
   566→            });
   567→            setMessages((prev) =>
   568→              prev.map((m) =>
   569→                m.id === assistantId
   570→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   571→                  : m
   572→              )
   573→            );
   574→          },
   575→        },
   576→        controller.signal,
   577→        userSessionId,
   578→      );
   579→    },
   580→    [isStreaming, messages, flushText, refreshTables, userSessionId]
   581→  );
   582→
   583→  const deleteMessage = useCallback(
   584→    (messageIndex: number) => {
   585→      if (isStreaming) return;
   586→
   587→      // Store remaining messages as history for Langfuse context on next send
   588→      setMessages((prev) => {
   589→        const remaining = prev.slice(0, messageIndex);
   590→        pendingHistoryRef.current = remaining
   591→          .filter((m) => m.role === 'user' || m.role === 'assistant')
   592→          .map((m) => ({ role: m.role, content: m.content }));
   593→        return remaining;
   594→      });
   595→
   596→      // Clear session so next message starts fresh
   597→      sessionIdRef.current = null;
   598→    },
   599→    [isStreaming]
   600→  );
   601→
   602→  const respondToQuestion = useCallback(
   603→    async (questionId: string, answers: string[], freeText?: string) => {
   604→      // Update the segment to show the user's answer
   605→      const segIdx = segmentsRef.current.findIndex(
   606→        (s) => s.type === 'user_question' && s.questionData?.questionId === questionId
   607→      );
   608→      if (segIdx !== -1) {
   609→        segmentsRef.current[segIdx] = {
   610→          ...segmentsRef.current[segIdx],
   611→          userAnswer: answers,
   612→          userFreeText: freeText,
   613→        };
   614→        setMessages((prev) =>
   615→          prev.map((m) =>
   616→            m.id === assistantIdRef.current
   617→              ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   618→              : m
   619→          )
   620→        );
   621→      }
   622→
   623→      // POST to backend
   624→      try {
   625→        await fetch('/api/chat/respond', {
   626→          method: 'POST',
   627→          headers: {
   628→            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   629→            ...(userSessionId ? { 'X-Session-ID': userSessionId } : {}),
   630→          },
   631→          body: JSON.stringify({
   632→            question_id: questionId,
   633→            answers,
   634→            free_text: freeText || null,
   635→          }),
   636→        });
   637→      } catch (e) {
   638→        console.error('Failed to respond to question:', e);
   639→      }
   640→    },
   641→    [userSessionId]
   642→  );
   643→
   644→  const loadMessages = useCallback((msgs: ChatMessage[], outgoingConversationId?: string | null, incomingConversationId?: string | null) => {
   645→    // Fall back to the stream's conversation ID when React state is stale
   646→    // (e.g. user switches before re-render after createConversation).
   647→    const outgoing = outgoingConversationId || streamConversationIdRef.current;
   648→    activeConversationIdRef.current = incomingConversationId || null;
   649→
   650→    // Don't abort the SSE stream — let the backend agent continue running
   651→    // in the background.  The onDone/onError callbacks detect that the
   652→    // conversation has changed and handle cleanup (cache invalidation).
   653→    // Only clear the flush timer so it doesn't interfere with the new
   654→    // conversation's state.
   655→    if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   656→      clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   657→      flushTimerRef.current = null;
   658→    }
   659→
   660→    // Prefer cached messages when the cache is at least as complete as
   661→    // the backend response (cache keeps rich segments / streaming data).
   662→    let finalMsgs = msgs;
   663→    if (incomingConversationId) {
   664→      const cached = messagesCacheRef.current.get(incomingConversationId);
   665→      if (cached && cached.length > 0 && cached.length >= msgs.length) {
   666→        finalMsgs = cached;
   667→      }
   668→      // Clear used cache entry so stale data doesn't persist forever
   669→      messagesCacheRef.current.delete(incomingConversationId);
   670→    }
   671→
   672→    // Capture ref values NOW — React 18 batching may defer the updater
   673→    // function below until after the ref resets at the bottom of this
   674→    // function, so reading refs inside the updater would see empty values.
   675→    const capturedTextBuffer = textBufferRef.current;
   676→    const capturedAssistantId = assistantIdRef.current;
   677→    const capturedSegments = [...segmentsRef.current];
   678→    const capturedCurrentText = currentTextRef.current;
   679→    const capturedPhase = phaseRef.current;
   680→
   681→    // Save current messages to cache before replacing them.
   682→    setMessages(prev => {
   683→      if (outgoing && prev.length > 0) {
   684→        const pendingText = capturedTextBuffer;
   685→        const aId = capturedAssistantId;
   686→
   687→        // Build finalized segments including any unflushed text
   688→        let finalSegments = [...capturedSegments];
   689→        const pendingSegmentText = capturedCurrentText + pendingText;
   690→        if (pendingSegmentText.trim()) {
   691→          const segType = capturedPhase === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   692→          finalSegments.push({ type: segType as ContentSegment['type'], text: pendingSegmentText });
   693→        }
   694→
   695→        const cleaned = prev.map(m => {
   696→          if (m.id === aId && m.isStreaming) {
   697→            return {
   698→              ...m,
   699→              content: m.content + pendingText,
   700→              // Keep isStreaming: true — the SSE stream continues in
   701→              // the background so the UI should show a thinking indicator.
   702→              segments: finalSegments.length > 0 ? finalSegments : m.segments,
   703→            };
   704→          }
   705→          return m;
   706→        });
   707→        // Filter out completed-but-empty assistant messages. Streaming
   708→        // messages are always kept so the thinking indicator stays visible.
   709→        const toCache = cleaned.filter(m => {
   710→          if (m.role === 'assistant' && !m.isStreaming && !m.content?.trim() && (!m.segments || m.segments.length === 0)) {
   711→            return false;
   712→          }
   713→          return true;
   714→        });
   715→        messagesCacheRef.current.set(outgoing, toCache);
   716→      }
   717→      return finalMsgs;
   718→    });
   719→
   720→    setIsStreaming(false);
   721→    textBufferRef.current = '';
   722→    currentTextRef.current = '';
   723→    segmentsRef.current = [];
   724→    assistantIdRef.current = '';
   725→    phaseRef.current = 'thinking';
   726→    sessionIdRef.current = null;
   727→    pendingHistoryRef.current = null;
   728→  }, []);
   729→
   730→  const clearMessages = useCallback((outgoingConversationId?: string | null) => {
   731→    const outgoing = outgoingConversationId || streamConversationIdRef.current;
   732→    activeConversationIdRef.current = null;
   733→    if (abortRef.current) {
   734→      abortRef.current.abort();
   735→      abortRef.current = null;
   736→    }
   737→    if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   738→      clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   739→      flushTimerRef.current = null;
   740→    }
   741→
   742→    // Capture refs before setMessages (React 18 batching may defer the updater)
   743→    const capturedTextBuffer = textBufferRef.current;
   744→    const capturedAssistantId = assistantIdRef.current;
   745→    const capturedSegments = [...segmentsRef.current];
   746→    const capturedCurrentText = currentTextRef.current;
   747→    const capturedPhase = phaseRef.current;
   748→
   749→    setMessages(prev => {
   750→      if (outgoing && prev.length > 0) {
   751→        const pendingText = capturedTextBuffer;
   752→        const aId = capturedAssistantId;
   753→        let finalSegments = [...capturedSegments];
   754→        const pendingSegmentText = capturedCurrentText + pendingText;
   755→        if (pendingSegmentText.trim()) {
   756→          const segType = capturedPhase === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   757→          finalSegments.push({ type: segType as ContentSegment['type'], text: pendingSegmentText });
   758→        }
   759→        const cleaned = prev.map(m => {
   760→          if (m.id === aId && m.isStreaming) {
   761→            return {
   762→              ...m,
   763→              content: m.content + pendingText,
   764→              isStreaming: false,
   765→              currentPhase: undefined,
   766→              segments: finalSegments.length > 0 ? finalSegments : m.segments,
   767→            };
   768→          }
   769→          return m.isStreaming ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined } : m;
   770→        });
   771→        const toCache = cleaned.filter(m => {
   772→          if (m.role === 'assistant' && !m.content?.trim() && (!m.segments || m.segments.length === 0)) {
   773→            return false;
   774→          }
   775→          return true;
   776→        });
   777→        messagesCacheRef.current.set(outgoing, toCache);
   778→      }
   779→      return [];
   780→    });
   781→    setIsStreaming(false);
   782→    textBufferRef.current = '';
   783→    currentTextRef.current = '';
   784→    segmentsRef.current = [];
   785→    assistantIdRef.current = '';
   786→    phaseRef.current = 'thinking';
   787→    sessionIdRef.current = null;
   788→    pendingHistoryRef.current = null;
   789→  }, []);
   790→
   791→  return (
   792→    <AgentContext.Provider
   793→      value={{ messages, isStreaming, sendMessage, editMessage, deleteMessage, clearMessages, loadMessages, respondToQuestion }}
   794→    >
   795→      {children}
   796→    </AgentContext.Provider>
   797→  );
   798→}
   799→

     1→import { createContext, useState, useCallback, useContext, ReactNode } from 'react';
     2→import type { ChatMessage, ContentSegment } from '../types';
     3→
     4→interface Conversation {
     5→  id: string;
     6→  title: string | null;
     7→  created_at: string;
     8→  updated_at: string;
     9→  messages?: Array<{
    10→    id: string;
    11→    role: string;
    12→    content: string;
    13→    metadata: string | null;
    14→    sort_order: number;
    15→  }>;
    16→}
    17→
    18→interface ConversationContextType {
    19→  activeConversationId: string | null;
    20→  refreshTrigger: number;
    21→  createConversation: (firstMessage: string) => Promise<string>;
    22→  selectConversation: (id: string) => Promise<ChatMessage[]>;
    23→  deleteConversation: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
    24→  renameConversation: (id: string, title: string) => Promise<void>;
    25→  startNewConversation: () => void;
    26→  triggerRefresh: () => void;
    27→}
    28→
    29→const ConversationContext = createContext<ConversationContextType | null>(null);
    30→
    31→export function ConversationProvider({ children, sessionId }: { children: ReactNode; sessionId: string }) {
    32→  const [activeConversationId, setActiveConversationId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    33→  const [refreshTrigger, setRefreshTrigger] = useState(0);
    34→
    35→  const triggerRefresh = useCallback(() => {
    36→    setRefreshTrigger((n) => n + 1);
    37→  }, []);
    38→
    39→  const createConversation = useCallback(async (firstMessage: string): Promise<string> => {
    40→    const title = firstMessage.length > 50 ? firstMessage.slice(0, 50) + '...' : firstMessage;
    41→    const res = await fetch('/api/conversations', {
    42→      method: 'POST',
    43→      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-Session-ID': sessionId },
    44→      body: JSON.stringify({ title }),
    45→    });
    46→    const conv = await res.json();
    47→    setActiveConversationId(conv.id);
    48→    triggerRefresh();
    49→    return conv.id;
    50→  }, [triggerRefresh, sessionId]);
    51→
    52→  const selectConversation = useCallback(async (id: string): Promise<ChatMessage[]> => {
    53→    const res = await fetch(`/api/conversations/${id}`);
    54→    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load conversation');
    55→    const conv: Conversation = await res.json();
    56→    setActiveConversationId(id);
    57→
    58→    const chatMessages: ChatMessage[] = (conv.messages || []).map((msg) => {
    59→      if (msg.role !== 'assistant') {
    60→        return { id: msg.id, role: 'user' as const, content: msg.content };
    61→      }
    62→
    63→      // Try to restore segments from metadata
    64→      let segments: ContentSegment[] | undefined;
    65→      if (msg.metadata) {
    66→        try {
    67→          const meta = typeof msg.metadata === 'string' ? JSON.parse(msg.metadata) : msg.metadata;
    68→          if (Array.isArray(meta?.segments)) {
    69→            segments = (meta.segments as Array<Record<string, unknown>>).map((seg) => {
    70→              const s: ContentSegment = { type: seg.type as ContentSegment['type'] };
    71→              if (seg.text) s.text = seg.text as string;
    72→              if (seg.toolCallId || seg.toolName) {
    73→                s.toolResult = {
    74→                  toolCallId: (seg.toolCallId as string) || '',
    75→                  toolName: seg.toolName as string,
    76→                  sql: (seg.sql as string) || '',
    77→                  columns: [],
    78→                  rows: [],
    79→                  rowCount: (seg.rowCount as number) || 0,
    80→                  error: seg.error as string | undefined,
    81→                  chart_spec: seg.chart_spec as ContentSegment['chart_spec'],
    82→                };
    83→              }
    84→              if (seg.subagentId) s.subagentId = seg.subagentId as string;
    85→              if (seg.subagentName) s.subagentName = seg.subagentName as string;
    86→              if (seg.sqlResults) s.sqlResults = seg.sqlResults as ContentSegment['sqlResults'];
    87→              if (seg.chart_spec && seg.type === 'subagent_end') {
    88→                s.chart_spec = seg.chart_spec as ContentSegment['chart_spec'];
    89→              }
    90→              return s;
    91→            });
    92→          }
    93→        } catch {
    94→          // metadata parse failed, fall through to default
    95→        }
    96→      }
    97→
    98→      // Fallback: single answer segment from content
    99→      if (!segments) {
   100→        segments = [{ type: 'answer' as const, text: msg.content }];
   101→      }
   102→
   103→      return {
   104→        id: msg.id,
   105→        role: 'assistant' as const,
   106→        content: msg.content,
   107→        segments,
   108→      };
   109→    });
   110→    return chatMessages;
   111→  }, []);
   112→
   113→  const deleteConversation = useCallback(async (id: string) => {
   114→    await fetch(`/api/conversations/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
   115→    if (activeConversationId === id) {
   116→      setActiveConversationId(null);
   117→    }
   118→    triggerRefresh();
   119→  }, [activeConversationId, triggerRefresh]);
   120→
   121→  const renameConversation = useCallback(async (id: string, title: string) => {
   122→    await fetch(`/api/conversations/${id}`, {
   123→      method: 'PUT',
   124→      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
   125→      body: JSON.stringify({ title }),
   126→    });
   127→    triggerRefresh();
   128→  }, [triggerRefresh]);
   129→
   130→  const startNewConversation = useCallback(() => {
   131→    setActiveConversationId(null);
   132→  }, []);
   133→
   134→  return (
   135→    <ConversationContext.Provider
   136→      value={{
   137→        activeConversationId,
   138→        refreshTrigger,
   139→        createConversation,
   140→        selectConversation,
   141→        deleteConversation,
   142→        renameConversation,
   143→        startNewConversation,
   144→        triggerRefresh,
   145→      }}
   146→    >
   147→      {children}
   148→    </ConversationContext.Provider>
   149→  );
   150→}
   151→
   152→export function useConversation() {
   153→  const ctx = useContext(ConversationContext);
   154→  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useConversation must be used within ConversationProvider');
   155→  return ctx;
   156→}
   157→

     1→import { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
     2→import { useTranslation } from '../hooks/useTranslation';
     3→import './ConversationHistory.css';
     4→
     5→interface Conversation {
     6→  id: string;
     7→  title: string | null;
     8→  created_at: string;
     9→  updated_at: string;
    10→}
    11→
    12→interface ConversationHistoryProps {
    13→  activeConversationId: string | null;
    14→  onSelect: (conversationId: string) => void;
    15→  onNew: () => void;
    16→  onDelete: (conversationId: string) => void;
    17→  onRename: (conversationId: string, title: string) => void;
    18→  refreshTrigger: number;
    19→  sessionId: string;
    20→}
    21→
    22→function timeAgo(dateStr: string, t: (key: string, params?: Record<string, string | number>) => string): string {
    23→  const date = new Date(dateStr);
    24→  const now = new Date();
    25→  const seconds = Math.floor((now.getTime() - date.getTime()) / 1000);
    26→  if (seconds < 60) return t('timeJustNow');
    27→  const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
    28→  if (minutes < 60) return t('timeMinutesAgo', { count: minutes });
    29→  const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);
    30→  if (hours < 24) return t('timeHoursAgo', { count: hours });
    31→  const days = Math.floor(hours / 24);
    32→  if (days < 30) return t('timeDaysAgo', { count: days });
    33→  return date.toLocaleDateString();
    34→}
    35→
    36→export function ConversationHistory({
    37→  activeConversationId,
    38→  onSelect,
    39→  onNew,
    40→  onDelete,
    41→  onRename,
    42→  refreshTrigger,
    43→  sessionId,
    44→}: ConversationHistoryProps) {
    45→  const { t } = useTranslation();
    46→  const [conversations, setConversations] = useState<Conversation[]>([]);
    47→  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    48→  const [editTitle, setEditTitle] = useState('');
    49→  const editInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    50→
    51→  // Tick state to force re-render of relative timestamps every 60s
    52→  const [, setTick] = useState(0);
    53→  useEffect(() => {
    54→    const interval = setInterval(() => setTick((t) => t + 1), 60_000);
    55→    return () => clearInterval(interval);
    56→  }, []);
    57→
    58→  const fetchConversations = useCallback(async () => {
    59→    try {
    60→      const res = await fetch('/api/conversations', {
    61→        headers: { 'X-Session-ID': sessionId },
    62→      });
    63→      if (res.ok) {
    64→        setConversations(await res.json());
    65→      }
    66→    } catch {
    67→      // Sidebar fetch failure: show empty list
    68→    }
    69→  }, [sessionId]);
    70→
    71→  useEffect(() => {
    72→    fetchConversations();
    73→  }, [fetchConversations, refreshTrigger]);
    74→
    75→  useEffect(() => {
    76→    if (editingId && editInputRef.current) {
    77→      editInputRef.current.focus();
    78→      editInputRef.current.select();
    79→    }
    80→  }, [editingId]);
    81→
    82→  const handleRenameSubmit = (id: string) => {
    83→    const trimmed = editTitle.trim();
    84→    if (trimmed) {
    85→      onRename(id, trimmed);
    86→    }
    87→    setEditingId(null);
    88→  };
    89→
    90→  const handleDelete = (e: React.MouseEvent, id: string) => {
    91→    e.stopPropagation();
    92→    if (confirm(t('deleteConversationConfirm'))) {
    93→      onDelete(id);
    94→    }
    95→  };
    96→
    97→  const handleStartRename = (e: React.MouseEvent, id: string, currentTitle: string) => {
    98→    e.stopPropagation();
    99→    setEditingId(id);
   100→    setEditTitle(currentTitle || '');
   101→  };
   102→
   103→  return (
   104→    <div className="conv-history">
   105→      <div className="conv-history__header">
   106→        <span className="conv-history__title">{t('conversations')}</span>
   107→        <button className="conv-history__new-btn" onClick={onNew} title={t('newConversation')}>
   108→          <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round">
   109→            <line x1="12" y1="5" x2="12" y2="19" />
   110→            <line x1="5" y1="12" x2="19" y2="12" />
   111→          </svg>
   112→        </button>
   113→      </div>
   114→      <div className="conv-history__list">
   115→        {conversations.length === 0 ? (
   116→          <div className="conv-history__empty">{t('noConversationsYet')}</div>
   117→        ) : (
   118→          conversations.map((conv) => (
   119→            <div
   120→              key={conv.id}
   121→              className={`conv-history__item ${conv.id === activeConversationId ? 'conv-history__item--active' : ''}`}
   122→              onClick={() => onSelect(conv.id)}
   123→            >
   124→              {editingId === conv.id ? (
   125→                <input
   126→                  ref={editInputRef}
   127→                  className="conv-history__edit-input"
   128→                  value={editTitle}
   129→                  onChange={(e) => setEditTitle(e.target.value)}
   130→                  onBlur={() => handleRenameSubmit(conv.id)}
   131→                  onKeyDown={(e) => {
   132→                    if (e.key === 'Enter') handleRenameSubmit(conv.id);
   133→                    if (e.key === 'Escape') setEditingId(null);
   134→                  }}
   135→                  onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   136→                />
   137→              ) : (
   138→                <>
   139→                  <div className="conv-history__item-title">
   140→                    {conv.title || t('untitled')}
   141→                  </div>
   142→                  <div className="conv-history__item-meta">
   143→                    <span className="conv-history__item-time">{timeAgo(conv.updated_at, t)}</span>
   144→                    <span className="conv-history__item-actions">
   145→                      <button
   146→                        className="conv-history__action-btn"
   147→                        onClick={(e) => handleStartRename(e, conv.id, conv.title || '')}
   148→                        title={t('rename')}
   149→                      >
   150→                        <svg width="12" height="12" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2">
   151→                          <path d="M11 4H4a2 2 0 0 0-2 2v14a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h14a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-7" />
   152→                          <path d="M18.5 2.5a2.121 2.121 0 0 1 3 3L12 15l-4 1 1-4 9.5-9.5z" />
   153→                        </svg>
   154→                      </button>
   155→                      <button
   156→                        className="conv-history__action-btn conv-history__action-btn--danger"
   157→                        onClick={(e) => handleDelete(e, conv.id)}
   158→                        title={t('deleteConversation')}
   159→                      >
   160→                        <svg width="12" height="12" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2">

   163→                        </svg>
   164→                      </button>
   165→                    </span>
   166→                  </div>
   167→                </>
   168→              )}
   169→            </div>

   171→        )}

     1→from fastapi import APIRouter, Header, Query
     2→from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
     3→from pydantic import BaseModel
     4→
     5→from app.memory_store import memory_store
     6→
     7→router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/conversations", tags=["conversations"])
     8→
     9→
    10→class CreateConversationRequest(BaseModel):
    11→    user_id: str = "default"
    12→    title: str | None = None
    13→
    14→
    15→class UpdateConversationRequest(BaseModel):
    16→    title: str
    17→
    18→
    19→@router.get("")
    20→async def list_conversations(
    21→    user_id: str = Query("default"),
    22→    limit: int = Query(50),
    23→    offset: int = Query(0),
    24→    x_session_id: str = Header(""),
    25→):
    26→    return memory_store.list_conversations(
    27→        user_id=user_id, limit=limit, offset=offset, session_id=x_session_id,
    28→    )
    29→
    30→
    31→@router.post("")
    32→async def create_conversation(
    33→    request: CreateConversationRequest,
    34→    x_session_id: str = Header(""),
    35→):
    36→    return memory_store.create_conversation(
    37→        user_id=request.user_id, title=request.title, session_id=x_session_id,
    38→    )
    39→
    40→
    41→@router.get("/{conversation_id}")
    42→async def get_conversation(conversation_id: str):
    43→    conv = memory_store.get_conversation(conversation_id)
    44→    if conv is None:
    45→        return JSONResponse(status_code=404, content={"error": "Conversation not found"})
    46→    return conv
    47→
    48→
    49→@router.put("/{conversation_id}")
    50→async def update_conversation(conversation_id: str, request: UpdateConversationRequest):
    51→    if not memory_store.update_conversation(conversation_id, request.title):
    52→        return JSONResponse(status_code=404, content={"error": "Conversation not found"})
    53→    return {"status": "ok"}
    54→
    55→
    56→@router.delete("/{conversation_id}")
    57→async def delete_conversation(conversation_id: str):
    58→    if not memory_store.delete_conversation(conversation_id):
    59→        return JSONResponse(status_code=404, content={"error": "Conversation not found"})
    60→    return {"status": "ok"}
    61→

     1→import { useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';
     2→import { useTranslation } from '../hooks/useTranslation';
     3→import { useAgent } from '../hooks/useAgent';
     4→import { ChatInput } from './ChatInput';
     5→import { MessageBubble } from './MessageBubble';
     6→import { FileUpload } from './FileUpload';
     7→import type { TableInfo } from '../types';
     8→import './AgentPanel.css';
     9→import { exportConversation } from '../utils/exportConversation';
    10→import { useConversation } from '../contexts/ConversationContext';
    11→
    12→interface AgentPanelProps {
    13→  tables: TableInfo[];
    14→  onUpload: (files: File[]) => Promise<void>;
    15→  onLoadSample: () => Promise<void>;
    16→  pendingSkillCommand?: string | null;
    17→  onSkillCommandConsumed?: () => void;
    18→}
    19→
    20→export function AgentPanel({ tables, onUpload, onLoadSample, pendingSkillCommand, onSkillCommandConsumed }: AgentPanelProps) {
    21→  const { t } = useTranslation();
    22→  const { messages, clearMessages } = useAgent();
    23→  const { activeConversationId, startNewConversation } = useConversation();
    24→  const bottomRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    25→  const scrollContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    26→  const userScrolledUp = useRef(false);
    27→
    28→  const isNearBottom = useCallback(() => {
    29→    const el = scrollContainerRef.current;
    30→    if (!el) return true;
    31→    return el.scrollHeight - el.scrollTop - el.clientHeight < 80;
    32→  }, []);
    33→
    34→  const handleScroll = useCallback(() => {
    35→    userScrolledUp.current = !isNearBottom();
    36→  }, [isNearBottom]);
    37→
    38→  useEffect(() => {
    39→    if (!userScrolledUp.current) {
    40→      bottomRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
    41→    }
    42→  }, [messages]);
    43→
    44→  return (
    45→    <div className="agent-panel">
    46→      <div className="agent-panel__header">
    47→        <span className="agent-panel__title">{t('agentMode')}</span>
    48→        <div className="agent-panel__actions">
    49→          {messages.length > 0 && (
    50→            <button className="agent-panel__clear" onClick={() => exportConversation()}>
    51→              {t('export')}
    52→            </button>
    53→          )}
    54→          {messages.length > 0 && (
    55→            <button className="agent-panel__clear" onClick={() => { if (confirm(t('clearConfirm'))) { clearMessages(activeConversationId); startNewConversation(); } }}>
    56→              {t('clear')}
    57→            </button>
    58→          )}
    59→        </div>
    60→      </div>
    61→      <div className="agent-panel__messages" ref={scrollContainerRef} onScroll={handleScroll}>
    62→        {messages.length === 0 && (
    63→          <div className="agent-panel__empty">
    64→            {tables.length === 0 ? (
    65→              <FileUpload onUpload={onUpload} onLoadSample={onLoadSample} />
    66→            ) : (
    67→              t('agentEmptyState')
    68→            )}
    69→          </div>
    70→        )}
    71→        {messages.map((msg, index) => (
    72→          <MessageBubble key={msg.id} message={msg} messageIndex={index} />
    73→        ))}
    74→        <div ref={bottomRef} />
    75→      </div>
    76→      <ChatInput pendingSkillCommand={pendingSkillCommand} onSkillCommandConsumed={onSkillCommandConsumed} />
    77→    </div>
    78→  );
    79→}
    80→

     1→import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
     2→import { useTranslation } from '../hooks/useTranslation';
     3→import { useAgent } from '../hooks/useAgent';
     4→import { useConversation } from '../contexts/ConversationContext';
     5→import { SlashCommandMenu } from './SlashCommandMenu';
     6→import { fetchSkills } from '../services/skillsService';
     7→import type { SkillInfo } from '../types';
     8→import './ChatInput.css';
     9→
    10→interface ChatInputProps {
    11→  pendingSkillCommand?: string | null;
    12→  onSkillCommandConsumed?: () => void;
    13→}
    14→
    15→export function ChatInput({ pendingSkillCommand, onSkillCommandConsumed }: ChatInputProps) {
    16→  const { t } = useTranslation();
    17→  const { sendMessage, isStreaming } = useAgent();
    18→  const { activeConversationId, createConversation, triggerRefresh } = useConversation();
    19→  const [text, setText] = useState('');
    20→  const textareaRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);
    21→
    22→  const [skills, setSkills] = useState<SkillInfo[]>([]);
    23→  const [showSlashMenu, setShowSlashMenu] = useState(false);
    24→  const [slashFilter, setSlashFilter] = useState('');
    25→
    26→  // Load skills on mount and refresh when toggled/updated
    27→  useEffect(() => {
    28→    fetchSkills().then(setSkills).catch(() => {});
    29→    const handler = () => { fetchSkills().then(setSkills).catch(() => {}); };
    30→    window.addEventListener('skills-updated', handler);
    31→    return () => window.removeEventListener('skills-updated', handler);
    32→  }, []);
    33→
    34→  // Consume pending skill command from sidebar "Use" button
    35→  useEffect(() => {
    36→    if (pendingSkillCommand) {
    37→      setText(prev => {
    38→        const trimmed = prev.trim();
    39→        const command = `${t('useSkillPlaceholder')} /${pendingSkillCommand}`;
    40→        return trimmed ? `${trimmed} ${command}` : command;
    41→      });
    42→      onSkillCommandConsumed?.();
    43→      textareaRef.current?.focus();
    44→    }
    45→  }, [pendingSkillCommand, onSkillCommandConsumed]);
    46→
    47→  const handleSend = async () => {
    48→    const trimmed = text.trim();
    49→    if (!trimmed || isStreaming) return;
    50→    setText('');
    51→    setShowSlashMenu(false);
    52→    if (textareaRef.current) {
    53→      textareaRef.current.style.height = 'auto';
    54→    }
    55→
    56→    let convId = activeConversationId;
    57→    if (!convId) {
    58→      convId = await createConversation(trimmed);
    59→    }
    60→    await sendMessage(trimmed, convId);
    61→    triggerRefresh();
    62→  };
    63→
    64→  const handleKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    65→    // When slash menu is open, let it handle arrow keys and Enter
    66→    if (showSlashMenu) {
    67→      if (['ArrowDown', 'ArrowUp', 'Enter', 'Escape'].includes(e.key)) {
    68→        return; // SlashCommandMenu handles these via document listener
    69→      }
    70→    }
    71→
    72→    if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey && !e.nativeEvent.isComposing) {
    73→      e.preventDefault();
    74→      handleSend();
    75→    }
    76→  };
    77→
    78→  const slashStartRef = useRef<number>(-1);
    79→
    80→  const handleInput = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLTextAreaElement>) => {
    81→    const val = e.target.value;
    82→    const cursorPos = e.target.selectionStart;
    83→    setText(val);
    84→
    85→    // Find the slash token at or before the cursor
    86→    const beforeCursor = val.slice(0, cursorPos);
    87→    const slashIdx = beforeCursor.lastIndexOf('/');
    88→    if (slashIdx !== -1) {
    89→      const token = beforeCursor.slice(slashIdx + 1);
    90→      // Valid slash command: no spaces in the token
    91→      if (!token.includes(' ')) {
    92→        slashStartRef.current = slashIdx;
    93→        setShowSlashMenu(true);
    94→        setSlashFilter(token);
    95→      } else {
    96→        setShowSlashMenu(false);
    97→      }
    98→    } else {
    99→      setShowSlashMenu(false);
   100→    }
   101→
   102→    const el = e.target;
   103→    el.style.height = 'auto';
   104→    el.style.height = Math.min(el.scrollHeight, 150) + 'px';
   105→  };
   106→
   107→  const handleSlashSelect = (skillName: string) => {
   108→    const start = slashStartRef.current;
   109→    const cursorPos = textareaRef.current?.selectionStart ?? text.length;
   110→    const before = text.slice(0, start);
   111→    const after = text.slice(cursorPos);
   112→    setText(`${before}/${skillName} ${after}`);
   113→    setShowSlashMenu(false);
   114→    slashStartRef.current = -1;
   115→    // Set cursor after the inserted command
   116→    const newCursorPos = start + skillName.length + 2; // "/" + name + " "
   117→    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   118→      textareaRef.current?.focus();
   119→      textareaRef.current?.setSelectionRange(newCursorPos, newCursorPos);
   120→    });
   121→  };
   122→
   123→  return (
   124→    <div className="chat-input">
   125→      <div className="chat-input__textarea-wrapper">
   126→        {showSlashMenu && skills.length > 0 && (
   127→          <SlashCommandMenu
   128→            skills={skills}
   129→            filter={slashFilter}
   130→            onSelect={handleSlashSelect}
   131→            onClose={() => setShowSlashMenu(false)}
   132→          />
   133→        )}
   134→        <textarea
   135→          ref={textareaRef}
   136→          className="chat-input__textarea"
   137→          value={text}
   138→          onChange={handleInput}
   139→          onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
   140→          placeholder={isStreaming ? '' : t('chatPlaceholder')}
   141→          disabled={isStreaming}
   142→          rows={1}
   143→        />
   144→        {isStreaming && (
   145→          <span className="chat-input__shimmer">{t('chatPlaceholderWaiting')}</span>
   146→        )}
   147→      </div>
   148→      <button
   149→        className="chat-input__send"
   150→        onClick={handleSend}
   151→        disabled={isStreaming || !text.trim()}
   152→      >
   153→        {t('send')}
   154→      </button>
   155→    </div>
   156→  );
   157→}
   158→

     1→import type { ToolCallResult, UserQuestionData, UserQuestionOption } from '../types';
     2→
     3→interface AgentCallbacks {
     4→  onTextChunk: (text: string) => void;
     5→  onThinkingDone: () => void;
     6→  onToolCall: (pending: ToolCallResult) => void;
     7→  onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => void;
     8→  onSubagentStart?: (data: { id: string; name: string; prompt: string }) => void;
     9→  onSubagentEnd?: (data: { id: string; name: string; result?: string; sql_results?: Array<{ sql: string; columns?: string[]; rows?: Record<string, unknown>[]; rowCount?: number; error?: string }>; chart_spec?: { data: unknown[]; layout?: Record<string, unknown> } }) => void;
    10→  onDone: (sessionId: string | null) => void;
    11→  onError: (error: string) => void;
    12→  onUserQuestion?: (data: UserQuestionData) => void;
    13→}
    14→
    15→export type { AgentCallbacks };
    16→
    17→async function streamSSE(
    18→  response: Response,
    19→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
    20→  signal?: AbortSignal,
    21→): Promise<void> {
    22→  const reader = response.body?.getReader();
    23→  if (!reader) {
    24→    callbacks.onError('No response stream');
    25→    return;
    26→  }
    27→
    28→  let doneReceived = false;
    29→  const wrappedCallbacks: AgentCallbacks = {
    30→    ...callbacks,
    31→    onDone: (sessionId) => {
    32→      doneReceived = true;
    33→      callbacks.onDone(sessionId);
    34→    },
    35→    onError: (error) => {
    36→      doneReceived = true;
    37→      callbacks.onError(error);
    38→    },
    39→  };
    40→
    41→  try {
    42→    const decoder = new TextDecoder();
    43→    let buffer = '';
    44→    let eventType = '';
    45→
    46→    while (true) {
    47→      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
    48→      if (done) break;
    49→
    50→      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
    51→      const lines = buffer.split('\n');
    52→      buffer = lines.pop() ?? '';
    53→      for (const line of lines) {
    54→        if (line.startsWith('event: ')) {
    55→          eventType = line.slice(7).trim();
    56→        } else if (line.startsWith('data: ')) {
    57→          const data = line.slice(6);
    58→          try {
    59→            const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
    60→            handleSSEEvent(eventType, parsed, wrappedCallbacks);
    61→          } catch {
    62→            // Skip malformed JSON
    63→          }
    64→          eventType = '';
    65→        }
    66→      }
    67→    }
    68→
    69→    // Process any remaining data left in buffer after stream ends
    70→    if (buffer.trim()) {
    71→      const remainingLines = buffer.split('\n');
    72→      for (const line of remainingLines) {
    73→        if (line.startsWith('event: ')) {
    74→          eventType = line.slice(7).trim();
    75→        } else if (line.startsWith('data: ')) {
    76→          const data = line.slice(6);
    77→          try {
    78→            const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
    79→            handleSSEEvent(eventType, parsed, wrappedCallbacks);
    80→          } catch {
    81→            // Skip malformed JSON
    82→          }
    83→          eventType = '';
    84→        }
    85→      }
    86→    }
    87→
    88→    // Safety net: if stream ended without done/error, force done
    89→    if (!doneReceived) {
    90→      callbacks.onDone(null);
    91→    }
    92→  } catch (e: unknown) {
    93→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
    94→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
    95→    callbacks.onError(msg);
    96→  }
    97→}
    98→
    99→export async function runAgentLoop(
   100→  message: string,
   101→  agentSessionId: string | null,
   102→  langfuseSessionId: string | null,
   103→  conversationHistory: { role: string; content: string }[] | null,
   104→  conversationId?: string | null,
   105→  callbacks?: AgentCallbacks,
   106→  signal?: AbortSignal,
   107→  userSessionId?: string,
   108→  skills?: string[],
   109→): Promise<void> {
   110→  if (!callbacks) return;
   111→  try {
   112→    const response = await fetch('/api/chat', {
   113→      method: 'POST',
   114→      headers: {
   115→        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   116→        ...(userSessionId ? { 'X-Session-ID': userSessionId } : {}),
   117→      },
   118→      body: JSON.stringify({
   119→        message,
   120→        session_id: agentSessionId,
   121→        conversation_id: conversationId,
   122→        langfuse_session_id: langfuseSessionId,
   123→        conversation_history: conversationHistory ?? [],
   124→        ...(skills?.length ? { skills } : {}),
   125→      }),
   126→      signal,
   127→    });
   128→
   129→    if (!response.ok) {
   130→      const errorText = await response.text();
   131→      callbacks.onError(`Server error: ${errorText}`);
   132→      return;
   133→    }
   134→
   135→    await streamSSE(response, callbacks, signal);
   136→  } catch (e: unknown) {
   137→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
   138→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
   139→    callbacks.onError(msg);
   140→  }
   141→}
   142→
   143→export async function runAgentEditLoop(
   144→  newMessage: string,
   145→  conversationHistory: { role: string; content: string }[],
   146→  langfuseSessionId: string | null,
   147→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
   148→  signal?: AbortSignal,
   149→  userSessionId?: string,
   150→): Promise<void> {
   151→  try {
   152→    const response = await fetch('/api/chat/edit', {
   153→      method: 'POST',
   154→      headers: {
   155→        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   156→        ...(userSessionId ? { 'X-Session-ID': userSessionId } : {}),
   157→      },
   158→      body: JSON.stringify({
   159→        new_message: newMessage,
   160→        conversation_history: conversationHistory,
   161→        langfuse_session_id: langfuseSessionId,
   162→      }),
   163→      signal,
   164→    });
   165→
   166→    if (!response.ok) {
   167→      const errorText = await response.text();
   168→      callbacks.onError(`Server error: ${errorText}`);
   169→      return;
   170→    }
   171→
   172→    await streamSSE(response, callbacks, signal);
   173→  } catch (e: unknown) {
   174→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
   175→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
   176→    callbacks.onError(msg);
   177→  }
   178→}
   179→
   180→function handleSSEEvent(
   181→  eventType: string,
   182→  data: Record<string, unknown>,
   183→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
   184→) {
   185→  switch (eventType) {
   186→    case 'thinking':
   187→    case 'answer':
   188→      callbacks.onTextChunk(data.text as string);
   189→      break;
   190→    case 'thinking_done':
   191→      callbacks.onThinkingDone();
   192→      break;
   193→    case 'tool_call':
   194→      callbacks.onToolCall({
   195→        toolCallId: (data.id as string) ?? '',
   196→        toolName: (data.name as string) ?? undefined,
   197→        sql: (data.sql as string) ?? '',
   198→        command: (data.command as string) ?? undefined,
   199→        toolInput: (data.input as Record<string, unknown>) ?? undefined,
   200→        columns: [],
   201→        rows: [],
   202→        rowCount: 0,
   203→      });
   204→      break;
   205→    case 'tool_result': {
   206→      const result: ToolCallResult = {
   207→        toolCallId: (data.id as string) ?? '',
   208→        toolName: (data.name as string) ?? undefined,
   209→        sql: (data.sql as string) ?? '',
   210→        columns: (data.columns as string[]) ?? [],
   211→        rows: (data.rows as Record<string, unknown>[]) ?? [],
   212→        rowCount: (data.rowCount as number) ?? 0,
   213→        error: (data.error as string) ?? undefined,
   214→        output: (data.output as string) ?? undefined,
   215→        rawContent: (data.content as string) ?? undefined,
   216→        chart_spec: (data.chart_spec as { data: unknown[]; layout?: Record<string, unknown> }) ?? undefined,
   217→      };
   218→      callbacks.onToolResult(result);
   219→      break;
   220→    }
   221→    case 'subagent_start':
   222→      callbacks.onSubagentStart?.({
   223→        id: data.id as string,
   224→        name: data.name as string,
   225→        prompt: (data.prompt as string) ?? '',
   226→      });
   227→      break;
   228→    case 'subagent_end': {
   229→      type ChartSpec = { data: unknown[]; layout?: Record<string, unknown>; frames?: unknown[] };
   230→      // Backend sends chart_specs (array) for multi-chart support.
   231→      // Fall back to legacy chart_spec (single object) for backwards compat.
   232→      const rawSpecs = data.chart_specs as ChartSpec[] | undefined;
   233→      const legacySpec = data.chart_spec as ChartSpec | undefined;
   234→      const chartSpecs: ChartSpec[] = rawSpecs ?? (legacySpec ? [legacySpec] : []);
   235→
   236→      if (chartSpecs.length > 0) {
   237→        // Emit one callback per chart so each gets its own segment
   238→        for (const spec of chartSpecs) {
   239→          callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
   240→            id: data.id as string,
   241→            name: data.name as string,
   242→            chart_spec: spec,
   243→          });
   244→        }
   245→        // Emit text result separately (if any) so it doesn't get lost
   246→        if (data.result) {
   247→          callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
   248→            id: data.id as string,
   249→            name: data.name as string,
   250→            result: data.result as string,
   251→          });
   252→        }
   253→      } else {
   254→        callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
   255→          id: data.id as string,
   256→          name: data.name as string,
   257→          result: (data.result as string) ?? undefined,
   258→          sql_results: (data.sql_results as Array<{ sql: string; columns?: string[]; rows?: Record<string, unknown>[]; rowCount?: number; error?: string }>) ?? undefined,
   259→        });
   260→      }
   261→      break;
   262→    }
   263→    case 'user_question': {
   264→      // Normalize options: the agent may send plain strings or {label, description} objects
   265→      const rawOptions = (data.options as unknown[]) ?? [];
   266→      const normalizedOptions: UserQuestionOption[] = (Array.isArray(rawOptions) ? rawOptions : []).map(
   267→        (opt: unknown) => {
   268→          if (typeof opt === 'string') return { label: opt };
   269→          if (typeof opt === 'object' && opt !== null && 'label' in opt) return opt as UserQuestionOption;
   270→          return { label: String(opt) };
   271→        }
   272→      );
   273→      callbacks.onUserQuestion?.({
   274→        questionId: data.question_id as string,
   275→        question: data.question as string,
   276→        options: normalizedOptions,
   277→        multiSelect: (data.multi_select as boolean) ?? false,
   278→      });
   279→      break;
   280→    }
   281→    case 'done':
   282→      callbacks.onDone((data.session_id as string) ?? null);
   283→      break;
   284→    case 'error':
   285→      callbacks.onError((data.message as string) ?? 'Unknown error');
   286→      break;
   287→  }
   288→}
   289→

     1→import { createContext, useContext } from 'react';
     2→import type { ChatMessage } from '../types';

     4→interface AgentContextValue {
     5→  messages: ChatMessage[];
     6→  isStreaming: boolean;

    10→  clearMessages: (outgoingConversationId?: string | null) => void;
    11→  loadMessages: (msgs: ChatMessage[], outgoingConversationId?: string | null, incomingConversationId?: string | null) => void;
    12→  respondToQuestion: (questionId: string, answers: string[], freeText?: string) => void;
    13→}
    14→
    15→export const AgentContext = createContext<AgentContextValue>({

    18→  sendMessage: () => {},
    19→  editMessage: () => {},
    20→  deleteMessage: () => {},
    21→  clearMessages: () => {},
    22→  loadMessages: () => {},
    23→  respondToQuestion: () => {},
    24→});
    25→

     1→from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Header
     2→from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse, StreamingResponse
     3→from pydantic import BaseModel
     4→
     5→from app.agent import stream_chat
     6→from app.database import Database
     7→from app.dependencies import get_session_db
     8→from app.pending_questions import pending_question_store
     9→
    10→router = APIRouter(prefix="/api", tags=["chat"])
    11→
    12→
    13→class ChatRequest(BaseModel):
    14→    message: str
    15→    session_id: str | None = None
    16→    langfuse_session_id: str | None = None
    17→    conversation_history: list[dict] = []
    18→    skills: list[str] | None = None
    19→    conversation_id: str | None = None
    20→
    21→
    22→class ChatEditRequest(BaseModel):
    23→    new_message: str
    24→    conversation_history: list[dict] = []
    25→    langfuse_session_id: str | None = None
    26→
    27→
    28→class QuestionResponseRequest(BaseModel):
    29→    question_id: str
    30→    answers: list[str] = []
    31→    free_text: str | None = None
    32→
    33→
    34→@router.post("/chat")
    35→async def chat(
    36→    request: ChatRequest,
    37→    db: Database = Depends(get_session_db),
    38→    x_session_id: str = Header(...),
    39→):
    40→    return StreamingResponse(
    41→        stream_chat(
    42→            request.message,
    43→            request.session_id,
    44→            db,
    45→            conversation_history=request.conversation_history or None,
    46→            langfuse_session_id=request.langfuse_session_id,
    47→            backend_session_id=x_session_id,
    48→            skills=request.skills,
    49→            conversation_id=request.conversation_id,
    50→        ),
    51→        media_type="text/event-stream",
    52→        headers={
    53→            "Cache-Control": "no-cache",
    54→            "Connection": "keep-alive",
    55→            "X-Accel-Buffering": "no",
    56→        },
    57→    )
    58→
    59→
    60→@router.post("/chat/edit")
    61→async def chat_edit(
    62→    request: ChatEditRequest,
    63→    db: Database = Depends(get_session_db),
    64→    x_session_id: str = Header(...),
    65→):
    66→    """Edit a message: start a fresh session with conversation history as context."""
    67→    return StreamingResponse(
    68→        stream_chat(
    69→            request.new_message,
    70→            session_id=None,
    71→            db=db,
    72→            conversation_history=request.conversation_history,
    73→            langfuse_session_id=request.langfuse_session_id,
    74→            backend_session_id=x_session_id,
    75→        ),
    76→        media_type="text/event-stream",
    77→        headers={
    78→            "Cache-Control": "no-cache",
    79→            "Connection": "keep-alive",
    80→            "X-Accel-Buffering": "no",
    81→        },
    82→    )
    83→
    84→
    85→@router.post("/chat/respond")
    86→async def respond_to_question(
    87→    request: QuestionResponseRequest,
    88→    x_session_id: str = Header(...),
    89→):
    90→    """Respond to a pending user question from the agent."""
    91→    pending = pending_question_store.get_pending(x_session_id)
    92→    if pending is None or pending["question_id"] != request.question_id:
    93→        return JSONResponse(status_code=404, content={"error": "No pending question found"})
    94→
    95→    answer = {"answers": request.answers}
    96→    if request.free_text:
    97→        answer["free_text"] = request.free_text
    98→    pending_question_store.respond(x_session_id, request.question_id, answer)
    99→    return {"status": "ok"}
   100→
