    13→import { ResultMarkdown } from './components/ResultMarkdown';
    14→import { Sidebar } from './components/Sidebar';
    15→import { ErrorMessage } from './components/ErrorMessage';
    16→import { AgentPanel } from './components/AgentPanel';
    17→import { useAgent } from './hooks/useAgent';
    18→import type { TableInfo, QueryResult } from './types';
    19→import './App.css';
    20→
    21→function findDuplicateFileNames(files: File[]): string[] {
    22→  const seen = new Set<string>();
    23→  const duplicates = new Set<string>();
    24→  for (const file of files) {
    25→    if (seen.has(file.name)) {
    26→      duplicates.add(file.name);
    27→    }
    28→    seen.add(file.name);
    29→  }
    30→  return Array.from(duplicates);
    31→}
    32→
    33→function AppContent({
    34→  tables,
    35→  refreshTables,
    36→  sessionId,
    37→}: {
    38→  tables: TableInfo[];
    39→  refreshTables: () => Promise<void>;
    40→  sessionId: string;
    41→}) {
    42→  const { theme, toggleTheme } = useTheme();
    43→  const { language, setLanguage, t } = useTranslation();
    44→  const [queryResult, setQueryResult] = useState<QueryResult | null>(null);
    45→  const [error, setError] = useState<string | { key: string; params: Record<string, string> } | null>(null);
    46→  const [editorQuery, setEditorQuery] = useState<string | undefined>(
    47→    () => tables.length > 0 ? `SELECT * FROM "${tables[0].name}" LIMIT 100` : undefined
    48→  );
    49→  const [sidebarCollapsed, setSidebarCollapsed] = useState(() => window.innerWidth < 768);
    50→  const [agentOpen, setAgentOpen] = useState(true);
    51→  const [pendingSkillCommand, setPendingSkillCommand] = useState<string | null>(null);
    52→
    53→  const conversation = useConversation();
    54→  const { clearMessages, loadMessages } = useAgent();
    55→
    56→  const handleConversationSelect = useCallback(async (id: string) => {
    57→    const outgoingId = conversation.activeConversationId;
    58→    const messages = await conversation.selectConversation(id);
    59→    loadMessages(messages, outgoingId, id);
    60→  }, [conversation, loadMessages]);
    61→
    62→  const handleNewConversation = useCallback(() => {
    63→    const outgoingId = conversation.activeConversationId;
    64→    conversation.startNewConversation();
    65→    clearMessages(outgoingId);
    66→  }, [conversation, clearMessages]);
    67→
    68→  const handleConversationDelete = useCallback(async (id: string) => {
    69→    await conversation.deleteConversation(id);
    70→    if (conversation.activeConversationId === id) {
    71→      clearMessages();
    72→    }
    73→  }, [conversation, clearMessages]);
    74→
    75→  const handleConversationRename = useCallback(async (id: string, title: string) => {
    76→    await conversation.renameConversation(id, title);
    77→  }, [conversation]);
    78→
    79→  useEffect(() => {
    80→    document.title = t('appTitle');
    81→  }, [t]);
    82→
    83→  useEffect(() => {
    84→    if (tables.length === 0) {
    85→      setQueryResult(null);
    86→      setError(null);
    87→      setEditorQuery(undefined);
    88→    }
    89→  }, [tables]);
    90→
    91→  const errorMessage = error
    92→    ? (typeof error === 'string' ? error : t(error.key, error.params))
    93→    : null;
    94→
    95→  const handleAgentToggle = () => {
    96→    setAgentOpen((prev) => !prev);
    97→  };
    98→
    99→  const handleLoadSample = useCallback(async () => {
   100→    setError(null);
   101→    try {
   102→      const response = await fetch('/api/upload/sample', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'X-Session-ID': sessionId } });
   103→      if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load sample dataset');
   104→      await refreshTables();
   105→      setEditorQuery('SELECT * FROM "titanic" LIMIT 100');
   106→    } catch (e) {
   107→      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Failed to load sample dataset');
   108→    }
   109→  }, [refreshTables, sessionId]);
   110→
   111→  const handleFileUpload = useCallback(
   112→    async (files: File[]) => {
   113→      setError(null);
   114→
   115→      // Check for duplicate filenames within the batch
   116→      const batchDuplicates = findDuplicateFileNames(files);
   117→      if (batchDuplicates.length > 0) {
   118→        setError({ key: 'duplicateFilesInBatch', params: { names: batchDuplicates.join(', ') } });
   119→        return;
   120→      }
   121→
   122→      // Check for filenames that match already-uploaded table names
   123→      const existingNames = new Set(tables.map((tbl) => tbl.name));
   124→      const alreadyUploaded = files.filter((f) => existingNames.has(f.name)).map((f) => f.name);
   125→      if (alreadyUploaded.length > 0) {
   126→        setError({ key: 'duplicateFilesExist', params: { names: alreadyUploaded.join(', ') } });
   127→        return;
   128→      }
   129→
   130→      let lastName = '';
   131→      try {
   132→        for (const file of files) {
   133→          const formData = new FormData();
   134→          formData.append('file', file);
   135→          const response = await fetch('/api/upload', {
   136→            method: 'POST',
   137→            headers: { 'X-Session-ID': sessionId },
   138→            body: formData,
   139→          });
   140→          if (!response.ok) {
   141→            const errorData = await response.json();
   142→            throw new Error(errorData.detail || 'Failed to upload file');
   143→          }
   144→          const result = await response.json();
   145→          // Handle both single table and array of tables (Excel with multiple sheets)
   146→          if (Array.isArray(result)) {
   147→            lastName = result[result.length - 1]?.name || '';
   148→          } else {
   149→            lastName = result.name;
   150→          }
   151→        }
   152→        await refreshTables();
   153→        if (lastName) {
   154→          setEditorQuery(`SELECT * FROM "${lastName}" LIMIT 100`);
   155→        }
   156→      } catch (e) {
   157→        setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Failed to upload file');
   158→      }
   159→    },
   160→    [refreshTables, sessionId, tables]
   161→  );
   162→
   163→  const handleQueryExecute = useCallback(
   164→    async (sql: string) => {
   165→      setError(null);
   166→      setQueryResult(null);
   167→      try {
   168→        const start = performance.now();
   169→        const response = await fetch('/api/query', {
   170→          method: 'POST',
   171→          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-Session-ID': sessionId },
   172→          body: JSON.stringify({ sql }),
   173→        });
   174→        const elapsed = performance.now() - start;
   175→
   176→        if (!response.ok) {
   177→          const errorData = await response.json();
   178→          throw new Error(errorData.detail || 'Query execution failed');
   179→        }
   180→
   181→        const result = await response.json();
   182→        setQueryResult({
   183→          columns: result.columns,
   184→          rows: result.rows,
   185→          rowCount: result.rowCount,
   186→          executionTimeMs: elapsed,
   187→          resultType: result.resultType,
   188→        });
   189→        await refreshTables();
   190→      } catch (e) {
   191→        setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Query execution failed');
   192→      }
   193→    },
   194→    [refreshTables, sessionId]
   195→  );
   196→
   197→  const handleTableClick = useCallback((tableName: string) => {
   198→    setEditorQuery(`SELECT * FROM "${tableName}" LIMIT 100`);
   199→  }, []);
   200→
   201→  const handleTableDelete = useCallback(async (tableName: string) => {
   202→    if (!confirm(t('deleteTableConfirm', { name: tableName }))) return;
   203→    try {
   204→      const response = await fetch(`/api/tables/${encodeURIComponent(tableName)}`, {
   205→        method: 'DELETE',
   206→        headers: { 'X-Session-ID': sessionId },
   207→      });
   208→      if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to delete table');
   209→      await refreshTables();
   210→    } catch (e) {
   211→      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Failed to delete table');
   212→    }
   213→  }, [refreshTables, t, sessionId]);
   214→
   215→  const handleDeleteAll = useCallback(async () => {
   216→    if (tables.length === 0) return;
   217→    if (!confirm(t('deleteAllTablesConfirm'))) return;
   218→    try {
   219→      for (const table of tables) {
   220→        const response = await fetch(`/api/tables/${encodeURIComponent(table.name)}`, {
   221→          method: 'DELETE',
   222→          headers: { 'X-Session-ID': sessionId },
   223→        });
   224→        if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to delete table');
   225→      }
   226→      await refreshTables();
   227→    } catch (e) {
   228→      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Failed to delete tables');
   229→    }
   230→  }, [tables, refreshTables, t, sessionId]);
   231→
   232→  const appClass = [
   233→    'app',
   234→    sidebarCollapsed ? 'app--sidebar-collapsed' : '',
   235→  ].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
   236→
   237→  const themeIcon = theme === 'dark' ? (
   238→    <svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round">
   239→      <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="5" />
   240→      <line x1="12" y1="1" x2="12" y2="3" />
   241→      <line x1="12" y1="21" x2="12" y2="23" />
   242→      <line x1="4.22" y1="4.22" x2="5.64" y2="5.64" />
   243→      <line x1="18.36" y1="18.36" x2="19.78" y2="19.78" />
   244→      <line x1="1" y1="12" x2="3" y2="12" />
   245→      <line x1="21" y1="12" x2="23" y2="12" />
   246→      <line x1="4.22" y1="19.78" x2="5.64" y2="18.36" />
   247→      <line x1="18.36" y1="5.64" x2="19.78" y2="4.22" />
   248→    </svg>
   249→  ) : (
   250→    <svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round">
   251→      <path d="M21 12.79A9 9 0 1 1 11.21 3 7 7 0 0 0 21 12.79z" />
   252→    </svg>
   253→  );
   254→
   255→  return (
   256→    <div className={appClass}>
   257→      <div className="app__sidebar-wrapper">
   258→        <Sidebar
   259→          tables={tables}
   260→          onTableClick={handleTableClick}
   261→          onTableDelete={handleTableDelete}
   262→          onUpload={handleFileUpload}
   263→          onDeleteAll={handleDeleteAll}
   264→          collapsed={sidebarCollapsed}
   265→          onToggle={() => setSidebarCollapsed((prev) => !prev)}
   266→          onUseSkill={(name) => setPendingSkillCommand(name)}
   267→          activeConversationId={conversation.activeConversationId}
   268→          onConversationSelect={handleConversationSelect}
   269→          onConversationNew={handleNewConversation}
   270→          onConversationDelete={handleConversationDelete}
   271→          onConversationRename={handleConversationRename}
   272→          conversationRefreshTrigger={conversation.refreshTrigger}
   273→          sessionId={sessionId}
   274→        />
   275→      </div>
   276→      {agentOpen ? (
   277→        <div className="app__agent-wrapper">
   278→          <div className="app__header">
   279→            <h1 className="app__title">{t('appTitle')}</h1>
   280→            <div className="app__header-actions">
   281→              <button
   282→                className="app__lang-toggle"
   283→                onClick={() => setLanguage(language === 'en' ? 'zh-TW' : 'en')}
   284→                aria-label={language === 'en' ? t('switchToZh') : t('switchToEn')}
   285→                title={language === 'en' ? t('switchToZh') : t('switchToEn')}
   286→              >
   287→                {language === 'en' ? 'EN' : '中'}
   288→              </button>
   289→              <button
   290→                className="app__theme-toggle"
   291→                onClick={toggleTheme}
   292→                aria-label={theme === 'dark' ? t('switchToLight') : t('switchToDark')}
   293→                title={theme === 'dark' ? t('switchToLight') : t('switchToDark')}
   294→              >
   295→                {themeIcon}
   296→              </button>
   297→              <button
   298→                className="app__agent-toggle app__agent-toggle--active"
   299→                onClick={handleAgentToggle}
   300→              >
   301→                {t('editorMode')}
   302→              </button>
   303→            </div>
   304→          </div>
   305→          {errorMessage && (
   306→            <ErrorMessage message={errorMessage} onDismiss={() => setError(null)} />
   307→          )}
   308→          <AgentPanel
   309→            tables={tables}
   310→            onUpload={handleFileUpload}
   311→            onLoadSample={handleLoadSample}
   312→            pendingSkillCommand={pendingSkillCommand}
   313→            onSkillCommandConsumed={() => setPendingSkillCommand(null)}
   314→          />
   315→        </div>
   316→      ) : (
   317→        <div className="app__editor-wrapper">
   318→          <div className="app__header">
   319→            <h1 className="app__title">{t('appTitle')}</h1>
   320→            <div className="app__header-actions">
   321→              <button
   322→                className="app__lang-toggle"
   323→                onClick={() => setLanguage(language === 'en' ? 'zh-TW' : 'en')}
   324→                aria-label={language === 'en' ? t('switchToZh') : t('switchToEn')}
   325→                title={language === 'en' ? t('switchToZh') : t('switchToEn')}
   326→              >
   327→                {language === 'en' ? 'EN' : '中'}
   328→              </button>
   329→              <button
   330→                className="app__theme-toggle"
   331→                onClick={toggleTheme}
   332→                aria-label={theme === 'dark' ? t('switchToLight') : t('switchToDark')}
   333→                title={theme === 'dark' ? t('switchToLight') : t('switchToDark')}
   334→              >
   335→                {themeIcon}
   336→              </button>
   337→              <button
   338→                className="app__agent-toggle"
   339→                onClick={handleAgentToggle}
   340→              >
   341→                {t('agentMode')}
   342→              </button>
   343→            </div>
   344→          </div>
   345→          <div className="app__mode-header">
   346→            <span className="app__mode-title">{t('editorMode')}</span>
   347→          </div>
   348→          {errorMessage && (
   349→            <ErrorMessage message={errorMessage} onDismiss={() => setError(null)} />
   350→          )}
   351→          <main className="app__main">
   352→            {tables.length === 0 ? (
   353→              <div className="app__empty">
   354→                <FileUpload onUpload={handleFileUpload} onLoadSample={handleLoadSample} />
   355→              </div>
   356→            ) : (
   357→              <>
   358→                <QueryEditor
   359→                  onExecute={handleQueryExecute}
   360→                  initialQuery={editorQuery}
   361→                />
   362→                {queryResult?.resultType === 'markdown' ? (
   363→                  <ResultMarkdown result={queryResult} />
   364→                ) : (
   365→                  <ResultsTable result={queryResult} />
   366→                )}
   367→              </>
   368→            )}
   369→          </main>
   370→        </div>
   371→      )}
   372→    </div>
   373→  );
   374→}
   375→
   376→export default function App() {
   377→  const sessionId = useSessionId();
   378→  const [tables, setTables] = useState<TableInfo[]>([]);
   379→  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   380→  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
   381→  const refreshTables = useCallback(async () => {
   382→    try {
   383→      const response = await fetch('/api/tables', {
   384→        headers: { 'X-Session-ID': sessionId },
   385→      });
   386→      if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to fetch tables');
   387→      const data = await response.json();
   388→      setTables(data);
   389→    } catch (e) {
   390→      console.error('Failed to refresh tables:', e);
   391→    }
   392→  }, [sessionId]);
   393→
   394→  useEffect(() => {
   395→    (async () => {
   396→      try {
   397→        const response = await fetch('/api/health');
   398→        if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Backend is not available');
   399→        await refreshTables();
   400→      } catch (e) {
   401→        setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Failed to connect to backend');
   402→      } finally {
   403→        setLoading(false);
   404→      }
   405→    })();
   406→  }, [refreshTables]);
   407→
   408→  if (loading) {
   409→    return <div className="app-loading">Connecting to backend...</div>;
   410→  }
   411→
   412→  if (error) {
   413→    return (
   414→      <div className="app-error">Failed to connect: {error}</div>
   415→    );
   416→  }
   417→
   418→  return (
   419→    <ConfigProvider>
   420→      <LanguageProvider>
   421→        <ThemeProvider>
   422→          <ConversationProvider sessionId={sessionId}>
   423→            <AgentProvider refreshTables={refreshTables}>
   424→              <AppContent tables={tables} refreshTables={refreshTables} sessionId={sessionId} />
   425→            </AgentProvider>
   426→          </ConversationProvider>
   427→        </ThemeProvider>
   428→      </LanguageProvider>
   429→    </ConfigProvider>
   430→  );
   431→}
   432→
```

   225→
   226→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   227→    # Messages
   228→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   229→
   230→    def add_message(
   231→        self,
   232→        conversation_id: str,
   233→        role: str,
   234→        content: str,
   235→        metadata: dict | None = None,
   236→    ) -> dict:
   237→        msg_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
   238→        now = _now_iso()
   239→        meta_json = json.dumps(metadata) if metadata is not None else None
   240→
   241→        with self._lock:
   242→            conn = self._connect()
   243→            try:
   244→                # Determine next sort_order for this conversation.
   245→                row = conn.execute(
   246→                    """
   247→                    SELECT COALESCE(MAX(sort_order), -1) + 1 AS next_order
   248→                    FROM messages
   249→                    WHERE conversation_id = ?
   250→                    """,
   251→                    (conversation_id,),
   252→                ).fetchone()
   253→                next_order = row["next_order"]
   254→
   255→                conn.execute(
   256→                    """
   257→                    INSERT INTO messages
   258→                        (id, conversation_id, role, content, metadata, created_at, sort_order)
   259→                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
   260→                    """,
   261→                    (msg_id, conversation_id, role, content, meta_json, now, next_order),
   262→                )
   263→                # Touch the conversation's updated_at timestamp.
   264→                conn.execute(
   265→                    "UPDATE conversations SET updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
   266→                    (now, conversation_id),
   267→                )
   268→                conn.commit()
   269→            finally:
   270→                conn.close()
   271→
   272→        return {
   273→            "id": msg_id,
   274→            "conversation_id": conversation_id,
   275→            "role": role,
   276→            "content": content,
   277→            "metadata": meta_json,
   278→            "created_at": now,
   279→            "sort_order": next_order,
   280→        }
   281→
   282→    def list_messages(self, conversation_id: str) -> list[dict]:
   283→        with self._lock:
   284→            conn = self._connect()
   285→            try:
   286→                rows = conn.execute(
   287→                    """
   288→                    SELECT * FROM messages
   289→                    WHERE conversation_id = ?
   290→                    ORDER BY sort_order
   291→                    """,
   292→                    (conversation_id,),
   293→                ).fetchall()
   294→            finally:
   295→                conn.close()
   296→        return [dict(r) for r in rows]
   297→
   298→
   299→# Module-level singleton
   300→memory_store = MemoryStore()
   301→

> AGENT

Now I have a thorough understanding of the codebase. Let me analyze the conversation management flow systematically.

## Analysis Summary

The basic flow works:
- Creating conversations, sending messages, persistence to SQLite -- all correct
- Conversation list, switching, caching -- mostly correct
- SSE streaming for the active conversation -- correct

However, I found a **critical bug** with background SSE streams when switching conversations during streaming. Let me explain and fix it.

### Critical Bug: Background SSE callbacks lack conversation guards

When a user sends a message in conversation A, switches to conversation B while A is still streaming, and sends a message in B, the old SSE stream's callbacks (`onTextChunk`, `onToolCall`, `onToolResult`, etc.) continue writing to **shared refs** (`textBufferRef`, `segmentsRef`, `currentTextRef`). Only `onDone` and `onError` have guards. This means:

1. A's text chunks accumulate in `textBufferRef`
2. When B starts streaming, `flushText()` uses `assistantIdRef.current` (now B's assistant ID)
3. A's text gets flushed to B's message -- **cross-conversation text leak**

Additionally, if `onDone` fires for A while the user is viewing A again (after switching back), it overwrites the message with empty `segmentsRef.current` (reset by `loadMessages`), **wiping the cached segments**.

### Fix: Stream generation counter

The cleanest fix is a generation counter that invalidates old callbacks without aborting the stream (keeping the backend agent alive).

  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/implement-memory/frontend/src/contexts/AgentContext.tsx",
  "old_string": "  // Track which conversation owns the current SSE stream (set in sendMessage).\n  // Used as a fallback when React state is stale (e.g. user switches before re-render).\n  const streamConversationIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);",
  "new_string": "  // Track which conversation owns the current SSE stream (set in sendMessage).\n  // Used as a fallback when React state is stale (e.g. user switches before re-render).\n  const streamConversationIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);\n  // Generation counter: incremented on every stream-disrupting event (sendMessage,\n  // loadMessages, clearMessages).  Callbacks capture the generation at creation time\n  // and bail out if the ref has since advanced, preventing cross-conversation leaks.\n  const streamGenerationRef = useRef(0);"
}

{ ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }\n                  : m\n              )\n            );\n          },\n          onDone: (newSessionId) => {\n            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;\n            if (newSessionId) sessionIdRef.current = newSessionId;\n            if (flushTimerRef.current) {\n              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);\n              flushTimerRef.current = null;\n            }\n            flushText();\n            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {\n              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'answer', text: currentTextRef.current });\n              currentTextRef.current = '';\n            }\n            setMessages((prev) =>\n              prev.map((m) =>\n                m.id === assistantId\n                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }\n                  : m\n              )\n            );\n            setIsStreaming(false);\n            abortRef.current = null;\n          },\n          onError: (error) => {\n            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;\n            if (flushTimerRef.current) {\n              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);\n              flushTimerRef.current = null;\n            }\n            flushText();\n            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {\n              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';\n              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });\n              currentTextRef.current = '';\n            }\n            segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'error', errorMessage: error });\n            setMessages((prev) =>\n              prev.map((m) =>\n                m.id === assistantId\n                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }\n                  : m\n              )\n            );\n            setIsStreaming(false);\n            abortRef.current = null;\n          },\n          onUserQuestion: (data) => {\n            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;\n            if (flushTimerRef.current) {\n              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);\n              flushTimerRef.current = null;\n            }\n            flushText();\n            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {\n              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';\n              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });\n              currentTextRef.current = '';\n            }\n            segmentsRef.current.push({\n              type: 'user_question',\n              questionData: data,\n            });\n            setMessages((prev) =>\n              prev.map((m) =>\n                m.id === assistantId\n                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }\n                  : m\n              )\n            );\n          },\n        },\n        controller.signal,\n        userSessionId,\n      );"
}

> AGENT

Build succeeds. Here's a summary of what I found and fixed:

## Findings

### What works correctly:
1. **Creating conversations** -- `ChatInput.handleSend` creates a conversation on first message, persists via backend API
2. **Sending messages** -- SSE streaming, text buffering, segment accumulation all work
3. **Switching conversations** -- `loadMessages` caches outgoing messages, loads incoming from backend/cache
4. **New conversation button** -- `clearMessages` caches old messages, resets state
5. **Backend persistence** -- User messages persisted at stream start, assistant messages in `finally` block (survives client disconnects)
6. **Conversation list** -- Refreshes on create/delete/rename, scoped by session ID

### Bug fixed: Background SSE cross-conversation text leak

**Root cause:** When a user switches conversations while streaming, the old SSE stream's callbacks (`onTextChunk`, `onToolCall`, `onToolResult`, etc.) continue writing to shared refs (`textBufferRef`, `segmentsRef`, `currentTextRef`). Only `onDone`/`onError` had guards. If the user sends a message in the new conversation while the old stream is still active, text from the old conversation can appear in the new conversation's message.

**Fix:** Added a `streamGenerationRef` counter. Every stream-disrupting event (`sendMessage`, `editMessage`, `loadMessages`, `clearMessages`) increments the generation. All SSE callbacks capture the generation at creation and bail out immediately if the ref has advanced. This prevents cross-conversation contamination without aborting the backend stream (which continues to persist its response to the database).

### Minor notes (not fixed, low impact):
- **Edited messages not persisted** -- `editMessage` doesn't pass `conversationId`, so edits are lost on reload
- **Cached streaming indicators** -- After switching back to a conversation that was streaming, the cached message shows `isStreaming: true` but no updates arrive (the generation guard correctly prevents stale updates; on next switch the backend's complete response is loaded)

   355→  const editMessage = useCallback(
   356→    async (messageIndex: number, newContent: string) => {

   360→      // Build conversation history from messages before the edit point

   362→      for (let i = 0; i < messageIndex; i++) {

   369→      const assistantId = generateId();

   601→
   602→  const deleteMessage = useCallback(
   603→    (messageIndex: number) => {
   604→      if (isStreaming) return;
   605→
   606→      // Store remaining messages as history for Langfuse context on next send
   607→      setMessages((prev) => {
   608→        const remaining = prev.slice(0, messageIndex);
   609→        pendingHistoryRef.current = remaining
   610→          .filter((m) => m.role === 'user' || m.role === 'assistant')
   611→          .map((m) => ({ role: m.role, content: m.content }));
   612→        return remaining;
   613→      });
   614→
   615→      // Clear session so next message starts fresh
   616→      sessionIdRef.current = null;
   617→    },
   618→    [isStreaming]
   619→  );
   620→
   621→  const respondToQuestion = useCallback(
   622→    async (questionId: string, answers: string[], freeText?: string) => {
   623→      // Update the segment to show the user's answer
   624→      const segIdx = segmentsRef.current.findIndex(
   625→        (s) => s.type === 'user_question' && s.questionData?.questionId === questionId
   626→      );
   627→      if (segIdx !== -1) {
   628→        segmentsRef.current[segIdx] = {
   629→          ...segmentsRef.current[segIdx],
   630→          userAnswer: answers,
   631→          userFreeText: freeText,
   632→        };
   633→        setMessages((prev) =>
   634→          prev.map((m) =>
   635→            m.id === assistantIdRef.current
   636→              ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   637→              : m
   638→          )
   639→        );
   640→      }
   641→
   642→      // POST to backend
   643→      try {
   644→        await fetch('/api/chat/respond', {
   645→          method: 'POST',
   646→          headers: {
   647→            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   648→            ...(userSessionId ? { 'X-Session-ID': userSessionId } : {}),
   649→          },
   650→          body: JSON.stringify({
   651→            question_id: questionId,
   652→            answers,
   653→            free_text: freeText || null,
   654→          }),
   655→        });
   656→      } catch (e) {
   657→        console.error('Failed to respond to question:', e);
   658→      }
   659→    },
   660→    [userSessionId]
   661→  );
   662→
   663→  const loadMessages = useCallback((msgs: ChatMessage[], outgoingConversationId?: string | null, incomingConversationId?: string | null) => {
   664→    // Invalidate any in-flight stream so its callbacks become no-ops.
   665→    ++streamGenerationRef.current;
   666→
   667→    // Fall back to the stream's conversation ID when React state is stale
   668→    // (e.g. user switches before re-render after createConversation).
   669→    const outgoing = outgoingConversationId || streamConversationIdRef.current;
   670→    activeConversationIdRef.current = incomingConversationId || null;
   671→
   672→    // Don't abort the SSE stream — let the backend agent continue running
   673→    // in the background.  The onDone/onError callbacks detect that the
   674→    // conversation has changed and handle cleanup (cache invalidation).
   675→    // Only clear the flush timer so it doesn't interfere with the new
   676→    // conversation's state.
   677→    if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   678→      clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   679→      flushTimerRef.current = null;
   680→    }
   681→
   682→    // Prefer cached messages when the cache is at least as complete as
   683→    // the backend response (cache keeps rich segments / streaming data).
   684→    let finalMsgs = msgs;
   685→    if (incomingConversationId) {
   686→      const cached = messagesCacheRef.current.get(incomingConversationId);
   687→      if (cached && cached.length > 0 && cached.length >= msgs.length) {
   688→        finalMsgs = cached;
   689→      }
   690→      // Clear used cache entry so stale data doesn't persist forever
   691→      messagesCacheRef.current.delete(incomingConversationId);
   692→    }
   693→
   694→    // Capture ref values NOW — React 18 batching may defer the updater
   695→    // function below until after the ref resets at the bottom of this
   696→    // function, so reading refs inside the updater would see empty values.
   697→    const capturedTextBuffer = textBufferRef.current;
   698→    const capturedAssistantId = assistantIdRef.current;
   699→    const capturedSegments = [...segmentsRef.current];
   700→    const capturedCurrentText = currentTextRef.current;
   701→    const capturedPhase = phaseRef.current;
   702→
   703→    // Save current messages to cache before replacing them.
   704→    setMessages(prev => {
   705→      if (outgoing && prev.length > 0) {
   706→        const pendingText = capturedTextBuffer;
   707→        const aId = capturedAssistantId;
   708→
   709→        // Build finalized segments including any unflushed text
   710→        let finalSegments = [...capturedSegments];
   711→        const pendingSegmentText = capturedCurrentText + pendingText;
   712→        if (pendingSegmentText.trim()) {
   713→          const segType = capturedPhase === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   714→          finalSegments.push({ type: segType as ContentSegment['type'], text: pendingSegmentText });
   715→        }
   716→
   717→        const cleaned = prev.map(m => {
   718→          if (m.id === aId && m.isStreaming) {
   719→            return {
   720→              ...m,
   721→              content: m.content + pendingText,
   722→              // Keep isStreaming: true — the SSE stream continues in
   723→              // the background so the UI should show a thinking indicator.
   724→              segments: finalSegments.length > 0 ? finalSegments : m.segments,
   725→            };
   726→          }
   727→          return m;
   728→        });
   729→        // Filter out completed-but-empty assistant messages. Streaming
   730→        // messages are always kept so the thinking indicator stays visible.
   731→        const toCache = cleaned.filter(m => {
   732→          if (m.role === 'assistant' && !m.isStreaming && !m.content?.trim() && (!m.segments || m.segments.length === 0)) {
   733→            return false;
   734→          }
   735→          return true;
   736→        });
   737→        messagesCacheRef.current.set(outgoing, toCache);
   738→      }
   739→      return finalMsgs;
   740→    });
   741→
   742→    setIsStreaming(false);
   743→    textBufferRef.current = '';
   744→    currentTextRef.current = '';
   745→    segmentsRef.current = [];
   746→    assistantIdRef.current = '';
   747→    phaseRef.current = 'thinking';
   748→    sessionIdRef.current = null;
   749→    pendingHistoryRef.current = null;
   750→  }, []);
   751→
   752→  const clearMessages = useCallback((outgoingConversationId?: string | null) => {
   753→    ++streamGenerationRef.current;
   754→    const outgoing = outgoingConversationId || streamConversationIdRef.current;
   755→    activeConversationIdRef.current = null;
   756→    if (abortRef.current) {
   757→      abortRef.current.abort();
   758→      abortRef.current = null;
   759→    }
   760→    if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   761→      clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   762→      flushTimerRef.current = null;
   763→    }
   764→
   765→    // Capture refs before setMessages (React 18 batching may defer the updater)
   766→    const capturedTextBuffer = textBufferRef.current;
   767→    const capturedAssistantId = assistantIdRef.current;
   768→    const capturedSegments = [...segmentsRef.current];
   769→    const capturedCurrentText = currentTextRef.current;
   770→    const capturedPhase = phaseRef.current;
   771→
   772→    setMessages(prev => {
   773→      if (outgoing && prev.length > 0) {
   774→        const pendingText = capturedTextBuffer;
   775→        const aId = capturedAssistantId;
   776→        let finalSegments = [...capturedSegments];
   777→        const pendingSegmentText = capturedCurrentText + pendingText;
   778→        if (pendingSegmentText.trim()) {
   779→          const segType = capturedPhase === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   780→          finalSegments.push({ type: segType as ContentSegment['type'], text: pendingSegmentText });
   781→        }
   782→        const cleaned = prev.map(m => {
   783→          if (m.id === aId && m.isStreaming) {
   784→            return {
   785→              ...m,
   786→              content: m.content + pendingText,
   787→              isStreaming: false,
   788→              currentPhase: undefined,
   789→              segments: finalSegments.length > 0 ? finalSegments : m.segments,
   790→            };
   791→          }
   792→          return m.isStreaming ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined } : m;
   793→        });
   794→        const toCache = cleaned.filter(m => {
   795→          if (m.role === 'assistant' && !m.content?.trim() && (!m.segments || m.segments.length === 0)) {
   796→            return false;
   797→          }
   798→          return true;
   799→        });
   800→        messagesCacheRef.current.set(outgoing, toCache);
   801→      }
   802→      return [];
   803→    });
   804→    setIsStreaming(false);
   805→    textBufferRef.current = '';
   806→    currentTextRef.current = '';
   807→    segmentsRef.current = [];
   808→    assistantIdRef.current = '';
   809→    phaseRef.current = 'thinking';
   810→    sessionIdRef.current = null;
   811→    pendingHistoryRef.current = null;
   812→  }, []);
   813→
   814→  return (
   815→    <AgentContext.Provider
   816→      value={{ messages, isStreaming, sendMessage, editMessage, deleteMessage, clearMessages, loadMessages, respondToQuestion }}
   817→    >
   818→      {children}
   819→    </AgentContext.Provider>
   820→  );
   821→}
   822→

     1→"""SQLite-backed memory store for conversations and messages."""
     2→
     3→import json
     4→import os
     5→import sqlite3
     6→import threading
     7→import uuid
     8→from datetime import datetime, timezone
     9→
    10→from app.config import MEMORY_DB_PATH
    11→
    12→
    13→def _now_iso() -> str:
    14→    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
    15→
    16→
    17→class MemoryStore:
    18→    """Thread-safe SQLite store for conversation history.
    19→
    20→    Uses WAL mode for concurrent reads, a threading.Lock for write
    21→    serialisation, and CASCADE deletes on messages.
    22→    """
    23→
    24→    def __init__(self, db_path: str = MEMORY_DB_PATH) -> None:
    25→        self._db_path = db_path
    26→        self._lock = threading.Lock()
    27→        self._ensure_dir()
    28→        self._init_schema()
    29→
    30→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    31→    # Internal helpers
    32→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    33→
    34→    def _ensure_dir(self) -> None:
    35→        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(self._db_path), exist_ok=True)
    36→
    37→    def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
    38→        conn = sqlite3.connect(self._db_path)
    39→        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    40→        conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
    41→        return conn
    42→
    43→    def _init_schema(self) -> None:
    44→        with self._lock:
    45→            conn = self._connect()
    46→            try:
    47→                conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
    48→                conn.executescript(
    49→                    """
    50→                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS conversations (
    51→                        id         TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    52→                        user_id    TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'default',
    53→                        title      TEXT,
    54→                        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    55→                        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
    56→                    );
    57→
    58→                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS messages (
    59→                        id              TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    60→                        conversation_id TEXT NOT NULL
    61→                            REFERENCES conversations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    62→                        role            TEXT NOT NULL,
    63→                        content         TEXT NOT NULL,
    64→                        metadata        TEXT,
    65→                        created_at      TEXT NOT NULL,
    66→                        sort_order      INTEGER NOT NULL
    67→                    );
    68→
    69→                    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_messages_conv_order
    70→                        ON messages(conversation_id, sort_order);
    71→
    72→                    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_conversations_user_updated
    73→                        ON conversations(user_id, updated_at);
    74→                    """
    75→                )
    76→                # Migration: add session_id column
    77→                try:
    78→                    conn.execute(
    79→                        "ALTER TABLE conversations ADD COLUMN session_id TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''"
    80→                    )
    81→                except sqlite3.OperationalError as exc:
    82→                    if "duplicate column name" not in str(exc):
    83→                        raise
    84→                conn.execute(
    85→                    "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_conversations_session "
    86→                    "ON conversations(session_id)"
    87→                )
    88→                conn.commit()
    89→            finally:
    90→                conn.close()
    91→
    92→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    93→    # Conversations
    94→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    95→
    96→    def create_conversation(
    97→        self,
    98→        user_id: str = "default",
    99→        title: str | None = None,
   100→        session_id: str = "",
   101→    ) -> dict:
   102→        now = _now_iso()
   103→        conv_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
   104→        row = {
   105→            "id": conv_id,
   106→            "user_id": user_id,
   107→            "title": title,
   108→            "session_id": session_id,
   109→            "created_at": now,
   110→            "updated_at": now,
   111→        }
   112→        with self._lock:
   113→            conn = self._connect()
   114→            try:
   115→                conn.execute(
   116→                    """
   117→                    INSERT INTO conversations (id, user_id, title, session_id, created_at, updated_at)
   118→                    VALUES (:id, :user_id, :title, :session_id, :created_at, :updated_at)
   119→                    """,
   120→                    row,
   121→                )
   122→                conn.commit()
   123→            finally:
   124→                conn.close()
   125→        return row
   126→
   127→    def list_conversations(
   128→        self,
   129→        user_id: str = "default",
   130→        limit: int = 50,
   131→        offset: int = 0,
   132→        session_id: str = "",
   133→    ) -> list[dict]:
   134→        with self._lock:
   135→            conn = self._connect()
   136→            try:
   137→                rows = conn.execute(
   138→                    """
   139→                    SELECT * FROM conversations
   140→                    WHERE user_id = ? AND session_id = ?
   141→                    ORDER BY updated_at DESC
   142→                    LIMIT ? OFFSET ?
   143→                    """,
   144→                    (user_id, session_id, limit, offset),
   145→                ).fetchall()
   146→            finally:
   147→                conn.close()
   148→        return [dict(r) for r in rows]
   149→
   150→    def get_conversation(self, conversation_id: str) -> dict | None:
   151→        with self._lock:
   152→            conn = self._connect()
   153→            try:
   154→                row = conn.execute(
   155→                    "SELECT * FROM conversations WHERE id = ?",
   156→                    (conversation_id,),
   157→                ).fetchone()
   158→                if row is None:
   159→                    return None
   160→                conv = dict(row)
   161→                msgs = conn.execute(
   162→                    """
   163→                    SELECT * FROM messages
   164→                    WHERE conversation_id = ?
   165→                    ORDER BY sort_order
   166→                    """,
   167→                    (conversation_id,),
   168→                ).fetchall()
   169→            finally:
   170→                conn.close()
   171→        conv["messages"] = [dict(m) for m in msgs]
   172→        return conv
   173→
   174→    def update_conversation(self, conversation_id: str, title: str) -> bool:
   175→        with self._lock:
   176→            conn = self._connect()
   177→            try:
   178→                cur = conn.execute(
   179→                    """
   180→                    UPDATE conversations
   181→                    SET title = ?, updated_at = ?
   182→                    WHERE id = ?
   183→                    """,
   184→                    (title, _now_iso(), conversation_id),
   185→                )
   186→                conn.commit()
   187→                changed = cur.rowcount > 0
   188→            finally:
   189→                conn.close()
   190→        return changed
   191→
   192→    def delete_conversation(self, conversation_id: str) -> bool:
   193→        with self._lock:
   194→            conn = self._connect()
   195→            try:
   196→                cur = conn.execute(
   197→                    "DELETE FROM conversations WHERE id = ?",
   198→                    (conversation_id,),
   199→                )
   200→                conn.commit()
   201→                changed = cur.rowcount > 0
   202→            finally:
   203→                conn.close()
   204→        return changed
   205→
   206→    def delete_conversations_by_session(self, session_id: str) -> int:
   207→        """Delete all conversations with the given session_id.
   208→
   209→        Returns the number of deleted rows.
   210→        """
   211→        if not session_id:
   212→            return 0
   213→        with self._lock:
   214→            conn = self._connect()
   215→            try:
   216→                cur = conn.execute(
   217→                    "DELETE FROM conversations WHERE session_id = ?",
   218→                    (session_id,),
   219→                )
   220→                conn.commit()
   221→                deleted = cur.rowcount
   222→            finally:
   223→                conn.close()
   224→        return deleted
   225→
   226→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   227→    # Messages
   228→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   229→
   230→    def add_message(
   231→        self,
   232→        conversation_id: str,
   233→        role: str,
   234→        content: str,
   235→        metadata: dict | None = None,
   236→    ) -> dict:
   237→        msg_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
   238→        now = _now_iso()
   239→        meta_json = json.dumps(metadata) if metadata is not None else None
   240→
   241→        with self._lock:
   242→            conn = self._connect()
   243→            try:
   244→                # Determine next sort_order for this conversation.
   245→                row = conn.execute(
   246→                    """
   247→                    SELECT COALESCE(MAX(sort_order), -1) + 1 AS next_order
   248→                    FROM messages
   249→                    WHERE conversation_id = ?
   250→                    """,
   251→                    (conversation_id,),
   252→                ).fetchone()
   253→                next_order = row["next_order"]
   254→
   255→                conn.execute(
   256→                    """
   257→                    INSERT INTO messages
   258→                        (id, conversation_id, role, content, metadata, created_at, sort_order)
   259→                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
   260→                    """,
   261→                    (msg_id, conversation_id, role, content, meta_json, now, next_order),
   262→                )
   263→                # Touch the conversation's updated_at timestamp.
   264→                conn.execute(
   265→                    "UPDATE conversations SET updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
   266→                    (now, conversation_id),
   267→                )
   268→                conn.commit()
   269→            finally:
   270→                conn.close()
   271→
   272→        return {
   273→            "id": msg_id,
   274→            "conversation_id": conversation_id,
   275→            "role": role,
   276→            "content": content,
   277→            "metadata": meta_json,
   278→            "created_at": now,
   279→            "sort_order": next_order,
   280→        }
   281→
   282→    def list_messages(self, conversation_id: str) -> list[dict]:
   283→        with self._lock:
   284→            conn = self._connect()
   285→            try:
   286→                rows = conn.execute(
   287→                    """
   288→                    SELECT * FROM messages
   289→                    WHERE conversation_id = ?
   290→                    ORDER BY sort_order
   291→                    """,
   292→                    (conversation_id,),
   293→                ).fetchall()
   294→            finally:
   295→                conn.close()
   296→        return [dict(r) for r in rows]
   297→
   298→
   299→# Module-level singleton
   300→memory_store = MemoryStore()
   301→

   221→

   223→    # TypeScript Langfuse SDK can create traces directly.
   224→    env: dict[str, str] = {
   225→        "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY": "placeholder",
   226→        "ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL": f"{BACKEND_BASE_URL}/anthropic",
   227→        "SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS": str(SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS),
   228→    }
   229→    if LANGFUSE_ENABLED:
   230→        env["LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY"] = LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY
   231→        env["LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY"] = LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY
   232→        env["LANGFUSE_BASE_URL"] = LANGFUSE_BASE_URL
   233→
   234→    # Use the backend session ID (X-Session-ID header) for both:
   235→    # 1. MCP SSE URL — so the container queries the correct DuckDB instance
   236→    # 2. Container lifecycle key — so the same container is reused across
   237→    #    requests from the same browser tab (the Claude agent session_id
   238→    #    changes after the first response, which would orphan the container)
   239→    stable_session = backend_session_id or session_id or "default"
   240→
   241→    # Declare persistence variables before try so they're accessible in finally
   242→    assistant_text_parts: list[str] = []
   243→    all_text_parts: list[str] = []

   249→    actual_session_id = session_id

   251→    try:
   252→        # Send SSE keepalive immediately so the HTTP response starts and
   253→        # intermediate proxies (Vite, nginx) don't drop the idle connection
   254→        # before we've finished the blocking Docker container creation.
   255→        yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   256→
   257→        # container_manager.create() is synchronous (blocking Docker API call).
   258→        # Run it in a thread executor so the event loop stays responsive and

   260→        # gVisor (runsc) containers can take 10-30 seconds to spin up.
   261→        loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
   262→        create_future = loop.run_in_executor(None, container_manager.create, stable_session, env)
   263→
   264→        max_create_wait = 60.0
   265→        elapsed = 0.0
   266→        while not create_future.done():
   267→            await asyncio.sleep(2.0)
   268→            elapsed += 2.0
   269→            if elapsed >= max_create_wait:
   270→                create_future.cancel()
   271→                raise RuntimeError(f"Container creation timed out after {max_create_wait:.0f}s")
   272→            yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   273→        info = await create_future
   274→        container_manager.touch(stable_session)
   275→
   276→        # Wait for container to be ready
   277→        for attempt in range(10):

   279→                async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=httpx.Timeout(5.0)) as check_client:
   280→                    resp = await check_client.get(f"{info.url}/health")
   281→                    if resp.status_code == 200:
   282→                        break
   283→            except Exception:
   284→                pass
   285→            yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   286→            await asyncio.sleep(1)
   287→        else:
   288→            raise RuntimeError("Sidecar container failed health check after 10 attempts")

     1→import {

     7→} from 'react';
     8→import { AgentContext } from '../hooks/useAgent';
     9→import { runAgentLoop, runAgentEditLoop } from '../agent/agentService';
    10→import type { ChatMessage, ContentSegment, ToolCallResult } from '../types';
    11→import { useSessionId } from '../hooks/useSessionId';
    12→import { generateUUID } from '../utils/uuid';
    13→
    14→function generateId() {
    15→  return generateUUID();

    17→
    18→export function AgentProvider({
    19→  children,
    20→  refreshTables,
    21→}: {
    22→  children: ReactNode;
    23→  refreshTables: () => Promise<void>;
    24→}) {
    25→  const userSessionId = useSessionId();
    26→  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<ChatMessage[]>([]);
    27→  const [isStreaming, setIsStreaming] = useState(false);
    28→  const abortRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);
    29→  const textBufferRef = useRef('');
    30→  const flushTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
    31→  const assistantIdRef = useRef('');
    32→  const segmentsRef = useRef<ContentSegment[]>([]);
    33→  const currentTextRef = useRef('');
    34→  const phaseRef = useRef<'thinking' | 'answer'>('thinking');
    35→  const sessionIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
    36→  const pendingHistoryRef = useRef<{ role: string; content: string }[] | null>(null);
    37→  // Keep a ref in sync with messages so sendMessage always reads the latest
    38→  const messagesRef = useRef<ChatMessage[]>(messages);
    39→  useEffect(() => { messagesRef.current = messages; }, [messages]);
    40→  // Cache messages per conversation so switching away doesn't lose in-progress data
    41→  const messagesCacheRef = useRef<Map<string, ChatMessage[]>>(new Map());
    42→  // Track which conversation is currently displayed so background streams can detect they're stale
    43→  const activeConversationIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
    44→  // Track which conversation owns the current SSE stream (set in sendMessage).
    45→  // Used as a fallback when React state is stale (e.g. user switches before re-render).
    46→  const streamConversationIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
    47→  // Generation counter: incremented on every stream-disrupting event (sendMessage,
    48→  // loadMessages, clearMessages).  Callbacks capture the generation at creation time
    49→  // and bail out if the ref has since advanced, preventing cross-conversation leaks.
    50→  const streamGenerationRef = useRef(0);
    51→
    52→  const flushText = useCallback(() => {
    53→    const text = textBufferRef.current;
    54→    if (!text) return;
    55→    const id = assistantIdRef.current;
    56→    currentTextRef.current += text;
    57→    setMessages((prev) =>
    58→      prev.map((m) =>
    59→        m.id === id ? { ...m, content: m.content + text } : m
    60→      )
    61→    );
    62→    textBufferRef.current = '';
    63→  }, []);
    64→
    65→  const sendMessage = useCallback(
    66→    async (text: string, conversationId?: string | null) => {
    67→      if (isStreaming) return;
    68→      streamConversationIdRef.current = conversationId || null;
    69→      const generation = ++streamGenerationRef.current;
    70→
    71→      const userMsg: ChatMessage = {
    72→        id: generateId(),
    73→        role: 'user',
    74→        content: text,
    75→      };
    76→
    77→      const assistantId = generateId();
    78→      assistantIdRef.current = assistantId;
    79→      const assistantMsg: ChatMessage = {
    80→        id: assistantId,
    81→        role: 'assistant',
    82→        content: '',

    84→        isStreaming: true,
    85→      };
    86→
    87→      setMessages((prev) => [...prev, userMsg, assistantMsg]);
    88→      setIsStreaming(true);
    89→      textBufferRef.current = '';
    90→      segmentsRef.current = [];
    91→      currentTextRef.current = '';

    93→
    94→      // Build conversation history from current messages for resume fallback.
    95→      // Read from messagesRef (not the stale closure `messages`) so that
    96→      // switching conversations is reflected immediately.
    97→      const history = messagesRef.current
    98→        .filter((m) => m.role === 'user' || m.role === 'assistant')

   100→
   101→      // If there's pending history from a delete, start a new Langfuse session with that context
   102→      const pendingHistory = pendingHistoryRef.current;
   103→      pendingHistoryRef.current = null;
   104→      const langfuseSessionId = pendingHistory ? generateUUID() : null;
   105→
   106→      const controller = new AbortController();
   107→      abortRef.current = controller;
   108→
   109→      // Extract all /skill-name references from message
   110→      let actualMessage = text;

   116→      }

   118→      await runAgentLoop(

   127→            textBufferRef.current += chunk;

   155→            );
   156→          },
