   158→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   159→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   160→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   161→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   162→            }
   163→            flushText();
   164→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   165→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   166→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   167→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   168→            }
   169→            // Add a pending tool segment so the input is shown immediately
   170→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   171→              type: 'tool',
   172→              toolResult: pending,
   173→            });
   174→            setMessages((prev) =>
   175→              prev.map((m) =>
   176→                m.id === assistantId
   177→                  ? { ...m, currentPhase: 'answer', segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   178→                  : m
   179→              )
   180→            );
   181→          },
   182→          onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => {
   183→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   184→            // Merge result into pending tool segment (keep input info, add output)
   185→            const pendingIdx = segmentsRef.current.findIndex(
   186→              (s) => s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.toolCallId === result.toolCallId
   187→            );
   188→            if (pendingIdx !== -1) {
   189→              const pending = segmentsRef.current[pendingIdx].toolResult!;
   190→              segmentsRef.current[pendingIdx] = {
   191→                type: 'tool',
   192→                toolResult: {
   193→                  ...pending,
   194→                  ...result,
   195→                  sql: result.sql || pending.sql,
   196→                  toolName: result.toolName || pending.toolName,
   197→                  command: result.command || pending.command,
   198→                  toolInput: result.toolInput || pending.toolInput,
   199→                },
   200→              };
   201→            } else {
   202→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'tool', toolResult: result });
   203→            }
   204→            setMessages((prev) =>
   205→              prev.map((m) =>
   206→                m.id === assistantId
   207→                  ? { ...m, toolCalls: [...(m.toolCalls || []), result], segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   208→                  : m
   209→              )
   210→            );
   211→            refreshTables();
   212→            // When a skill is created, notify SkillsPanel to refresh
   213→            if (result.toolName?.includes('create_skill')) {
   214→              window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('skills-updated'));
   215→            }
   216→            // When a memory is saved or forgotten, notify MemoriesPanel to refresh
   217→            if (result.toolName?.includes('save_memory') || result.toolName?.includes('forget_memory')) {
   218→              window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('memories-updated'));
   219→            }
   220→          },
   221→          onSubagentStart: (data) => {
   222→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   223→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   224→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   225→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   226→            }
   227→            flushText();
   228→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   229→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   230→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   231→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   232→            }
   233→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   234→              type: 'subagent_start',
   235→              subagentId: data.id,
   236→              subagentName: data.name,
   237→              text: data.prompt,
   238→            });
   239→            setMessages((prev) =>
   240→              prev.map((m) =>
   241→                m.id === assistantId
   242→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   243→                  : m
   244→              )
   245→            );
   246→          },
   247→          onSubagentEnd: (data) => {
   248→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   249→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   250→              type: 'subagent_end',
   251→              subagentId: data.id,
   252→              subagentName: data.name,
   253→              chart_spec: data.chart_spec,
   254→              sqlResults: data.sql_results,
   255→              text: data.result,
   256→            });
   257→            setMessages((prev) =>
   258→              prev.map((m) =>
   259→                m.id === assistantId
   260→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   261→                  : m
   262→              )
   263→            );
   264→          },
   265→          onDone: (newSessionId) => {
   266→            // If this stream's generation is stale (user switched conversations
   267→            // or started a new stream), don't touch shared state — just clear
   268→            // the stale cache so the next switch loads the full backend response.
   269→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) {
   270→              if (conversationId) messagesCacheRef.current.delete(conversationId);
   271→              return;
   272→            }
   273→            if (newSessionId) sessionIdRef.current = newSessionId;
   274→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   275→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   276→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   277→            }
   278→            flushText();
   279→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   280→              segmentsRef.current.push({
   281→                type: 'answer',
   282→                text: currentTextRef.current,
   283→              });
   284→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   285→            }
   286→            setMessages((prev) =>
   287→              prev.map((m) =>
   288→                m.id === assistantId
   289→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   290→                  : m
   291→              )
   292→            );
   293→            setIsStreaming(false);
   294→            abortRef.current = null;
   295→          },
   296→          onError: (error) => {
   297→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) {
   298→              if (conversationId) messagesCacheRef.current.delete(conversationId);
   299→              return;
   300→            }
   301→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   302→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   303→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   304→            }
   305→            flushText();
   306→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   307→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   308→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   309→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   310→            }
   311→            segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'error', errorMessage: error });
   312→            setMessages((prev) =>
   313→              prev.map((m) =>
   314→                m.id === assistantId
   315→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   316→                  : m
   317→              )
   318→            );
   319→            setIsStreaming(false);
   320→            abortRef.current = null;
   321→          },
   322→          onUserQuestion: (data) => {
   323→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   324→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   325→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   326→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   327→            }
   328→            flushText();
   329→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   330→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   331→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   332→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   333→            }
   334→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   335→              type: 'user_question',
   336→              questionData: data,
   337→            });
   338→            setMessages((prev) =>
   339→              prev.map((m) =>
   340→                m.id === assistantId
   341→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   342→                  : m
   343→              )
   344→            );
   345→          },
   346→        },
   347→        controller.signal,
   348→        userSessionId,
   349→        skills,
   350→      );
   351→    },
   352→    [isStreaming, flushText, refreshTables, userSessionId]
   353→  );
   354→
   355→  const editMessage = useCallback(
   356→    async (messageIndex: number, newContent: string) => {
   357→      if (isStreaming) return;
   358→      const generation = ++streamGenerationRef.current;
   359→
   360→      // Build conversation history from messages before the edit point
   361→      const conversationHistory: { role: string; content: string }[] = [];
   362→      for (let i = 0; i < messageIndex; i++) {
   363→        const msg = messages[i];
   364→        if (msg.role === 'user' || msg.role === 'assistant') {
   365→          conversationHistory.push({ role: msg.role, content: msg.content });
   366→        }
   367→      }
   368→
   369→      const assistantId = generateId();
   370→      assistantIdRef.current = assistantId;
   371→
   372→      const userMsg: ChatMessage = {
   373→        id: generateId(),
   374→        role: 'user',
   375→        content: newContent,
   376→      };
   377→      const assistantMsg: ChatMessage = {
   378→        id: assistantId,
   379→        role: 'assistant',
   380→        content: '',
   381→        toolCalls: [],
   382→        isStreaming: true,
   383→      };
   384→
   385→      setMessages((prev) => [...prev.slice(0, messageIndex), userMsg, assistantMsg]);
   386→      setIsStreaming(true);
   387→      textBufferRef.current = '';
   388→      segmentsRef.current = [];
   389→      currentTextRef.current = '';
   390→      phaseRef.current = 'thinking';
   391→
   392→      const controller = new AbortController();
   393→      abortRef.current = controller;
   394→
   395→      await runAgentEditLoop(
   396→        newContent,
   397→        conversationHistory,
   398→        generateUUID(),
   399→        {
   400→          onTextChunk: (chunk) => {
   401→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   402→            textBufferRef.current += chunk;
   403→            if (!flushTimerRef.current) {
   404→              flushTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
   405→                flushText();
   406→                flushTimerRef.current = null;
   407→              }, 50);
   408→            }
   409→          },
   410→          onThinkingDone: () => {
   411→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   412→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   413→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   414→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   415→            }
   416→            flushText();
   417→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   418→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'thinking', text: currentTextRef.current });
   419→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   420→            }
   421→            phaseRef.current = 'answer';
   422→            setMessages((prev) =>
   423→              prev.map((m) =>
   424→                m.id === assistantId
   425→                  ? { ...m, currentPhase: 'answer', segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   426→                  : m
   427→              )
   428→            );
   429→          },
   430→          onToolCall: (pending: ToolCallResult) => {
   431→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   432→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   433→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   434→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   435→            }
   436→            flushText();
   437→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   438→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   439→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   440→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   441→            }
   442→            segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'tool', toolResult: pending });
   443→            setMessages((prev) =>
   444→              prev.map((m) =>
   445→                m.id === assistantId
   446→                  ? { ...m, currentPhase: 'answer', segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   447→                  : m
   448→              )
   449→            );
   450→          },
   451→          onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => {
   452→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   453→            const pendingIdx = segmentsRef.current.findIndex(
   454→              (s) => s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.toolCallId === result.toolCallId
   455→            );
   456→            if (pendingIdx !== -1) {
   457→              const pending = segmentsRef.current[pendingIdx].toolResult!;
   458→              segmentsRef.current[pendingIdx] = {
   459→                type: 'tool',
   460→                toolResult: {
   461→                  ...pending,
   462→                  ...result,
   463→                  sql: result.sql || pending.sql,
   464→                  toolName: result.toolName || pending.toolName,
   465→                  command: result.command || pending.command,
   466→                  toolInput: result.toolInput || pending.toolInput,
   467→                },
   468→              };
   469→            } else {
   470→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'tool', toolResult: result });
   471→            }
   472→            setMessages((prev) =>
   473→              prev.map((m) =>
   474→                m.id === assistantId
   475→                  ? { ...m, toolCalls: [...(m.toolCalls || []), result], segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   476→                  : m
   477→              )
   478→            );
   479→            refreshTables();
   480→          },
   481→          onSubagentStart: (data) => {
   482→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   483→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   484→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   485→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   486→            }
   487→            flushText();
   488→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   489→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   490→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   491→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   492→            }
   493→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   494→              type: 'subagent_start',
   495→              subagentId: data.id,
   496→              subagentName: data.name,
   497→              text: data.prompt,
   498→            });
   499→            setMessages((prev) =>
   500→              prev.map((m) =>
   501→                m.id === assistantId
   502→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   503→                  : m
   504→              )
   505→            );
   506→          },
   507→          onSubagentEnd: (data) => {
   508→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   509→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   510→              type: 'subagent_end',
   511→              subagentId: data.id,
   512→              subagentName: data.name,
   513→              chart_spec: data.chart_spec,
   514→              sqlResults: data.sql_results,
   515→              text: data.result,
   516→            });
   517→            setMessages((prev) =>
   518→              prev.map((m) =>
   519→                m.id === assistantId
   520→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   521→                  : m
   522→              )
   523→            );
   524→          },
   525→          onDone: (newSessionId) => {
   526→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   527→            if (newSessionId) sessionIdRef.current = newSessionId;
   528→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   529→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   530→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   531→            }
   532→            flushText();
   533→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   534→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'answer', text: currentTextRef.current });
   535→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   536→            }
   537→            setMessages((prev) =>
   538→              prev.map((m) =>
   539→                m.id === assistantId
   540→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   541→                  : m
   542→              )
   543→            );
   544→            setIsStreaming(false);
   545→            abortRef.current = null;
   546→          },
   547→          onError: (error) => {
   548→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   549→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   550→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   551→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   552→            }
   553→            flushText();
   554→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   555→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   556→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   557→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   558→            }
   559→            segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'error', errorMessage: error });
   560→            setMessages((prev) =>
   561→              prev.map((m) =>
   562→                m.id === assistantId
   563→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   564→                  : m
   565→              )
   566→            );
   567→            setIsStreaming(false);
   568→            abortRef.current = null;
   569→          },
   570→          onUserQuestion: (data) => {
   571→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   572→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   573→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   574→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   575→            }
   576→            flushText();
   577→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   578→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   579→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   580→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   581→            }
   582→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   583→              type: 'user_question',
   584→              questionData: data,
   585→            });
   586→            setMessages((prev) =>
   587→              prev.map((m) =>
   588→                m.id === assistantId
   589→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   590→                  : m
   591→              )
   592→            );
   593→          },
   594→        },
   595→        controller.signal,
   596→        userSessionId,
   597→      );
   598→    },
   599→    [isStreaming, messages, flushText, refreshTables, userSessionId]
   600→  );
   601→
   602→  const deleteMessage = useCallback(
   603→    (messageIndex: number) => {
   604→      if (isStreaming) return;
   605→
   606→      // Store remaining messages as history for Langfuse context on next send
   607→      setMessages((prev) => {
   608→        const remaining = prev.slice(0, messageIndex);
   609→        pendingHistoryRef.current = remaining
   610→          .filter((m) => m.role === 'user' || m.role === 'assistant')
   611→          .map((m) => ({ role: m.role, content: m.content }));
   612→        return remaining;
   613→      });
   614→
   615→      // Clear session so next message starts fresh
   616→      sessionIdRef.current = null;
   617→    },
   618→    [isStreaming]
   619→  );
   620→
   621→  const respondToQuestion = useCallback(
   622→    async (questionId: string, answers: string[], freeText?: string) => {
   623→      // Update the segment to show the user's answer
   624→      const segIdx = segmentsRef.current.findIndex(
   625→        (s) => s.type === 'user_question' && s.questionData?.questionId === questionId
   626→      );
   627→      if (segIdx !== -1) {
   628→        segmentsRef.current[segIdx] = {
   629→          ...segmentsRef.current[segIdx],
   630→          userAnswer: answers,
   631→          userFreeText: freeText,
   632→        };
   633→        setMessages((prev) =>
   634→          prev.map((m) =>
   635→            m.id === assistantIdRef.current
   636→              ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   637→              : m
   638→          )
   639→        );
   640→      }
   641→
   642→      // POST to backend
   643→      try {
   644→        await fetch('/api/chat/respond', {
   645→          method: 'POST',
   646→          headers: {
   647→            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   648→            ...(userSessionId ? { 'X-Session-ID': userSessionId } : {}),
   649→          },
   650→          body: JSON.stringify({
   651→            question_id: questionId,
   652→            answers,
   653→            free_text: freeText || null,
   654→          }),
   655→        });
   656→      } catch (e) {
   657→        console.error('Failed to respond to question:', e);
   658→      }
   659→    },
   660→    [userSessionId]
   661→  );
   662→
   663→  const loadMessages = useCallback((msgs: ChatMessage[], outgoingConversationId?: string | null, incomingConversationId?: string | null) => {
   664→    // Invalidate any in-flight stream so its callbacks become no-ops.
   665→    ++streamGenerationRef.current;
   666→
   667→    // Fall back to the stream's conversation ID when React state is stale
   668→    // (e.g. user switches before re-render after createConversation).
   669→    const outgoing = outgoingConversationId || streamConversationIdRef.current;
   670→    activeConversationIdRef.current = incomingConversationId || null;
   671→
   672→    // Don't abort the SSE stream — let the backend agent continue running
   673→    // in the background.  The onDone/onError callbacks detect that the
   674→    // conversation has changed and handle cleanup (cache invalidation).
   675→    // Only clear the flush timer so it doesn't interfere with the new
   676→    // conversation's state.
   677→    if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   678→      clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   679→      flushTimerRef.current = null;
   680→    }
   681→
   682→    // Prefer cached messages when the cache is at least as complete as
   683→    // the backend response (cache keeps rich segments / streaming data).
   684→    let finalMsgs = msgs;
   685→    if (incomingConversationId) {
   686→      const cached = messagesCacheRef.current.get(incomingConversationId);
   687→      if (cached && cached.length > 0 && cached.length >= msgs.length) {
   688→        finalMsgs = cached;
   689→      }
   690→      // Clear used cache entry so stale data doesn't persist forever
   691→      messagesCacheRef.current.delete(incomingConversationId);
   692→    }
   693→
   694→    // Capture ref values NOW — React 18 batching may defer the updater
   695→    // function below until after the ref resets at the bottom of this
   696→    // function, so reading refs inside the updater would see empty values.
   697→    const capturedTextBuffer = textBufferRef.current;
   698→    const capturedAssistantId = assistantIdRef.current;
   699→    const capturedSegments = [...segmentsRef.current];
   700→    const capturedCurrentText = currentTextRef.current;
   701→    const capturedPhase = phaseRef.current;
   702→
   703→    // Save current messages to cache before replacing them.
   704→    setMessages(prev => {
   705→      if (outgoing && prev.length > 0) {
   706→        const pendingText = capturedTextBuffer;
   707→        const aId = capturedAssistantId;
   708→
   709→        // Build finalized segments including any unflushed text
   710→        let finalSegments = [...capturedSegments];
   711→        const pendingSegmentText = capturedCurrentText + pendingText;
   712→        if (pendingSegmentText.trim()) {
   713→          const segType = capturedPhase === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   714→          finalSegments.push({ type: segType as ContentSegment['type'], text: pendingSegmentText });
   715→        }
   716→
   717→        const cleaned = prev.map(m => {
   718→          if (m.id === aId && m.isStreaming) {
   719→            return {
   720→              ...m,
   721→              content: m.content + pendingText,
   722→              // Keep isStreaming: true — the SSE stream continues in
   723→              // the background so the UI should show a thinking indicator.
   724→              segments: finalSegments.length > 0 ? finalSegments : m.segments,
   725→            };
   726→          }
   727→          return m;
   728→        });
   729→        // Filter out completed-but-empty assistant messages. Streaming
   730→        // messages are always kept so the thinking indicator stays visible.
   731→        const toCache = cleaned.filter(m => {
   732→          if (m.role === 'assistant' && !m.isStreaming && !m.content?.trim() && (!m.segments || m.segments.length === 0)) {
   733→            return false;
   734→          }
   735→          return true;
   736→        });
   737→        messagesCacheRef.current.set(outgoing, toCache);
   738→      }
   739→      return finalMsgs;
   740→    });
   741→
   742→    setIsStreaming(false);
   743→    textBufferRef.current = '';
   744→    currentTextRef.current = '';
   745→    segmentsRef.current = [];
   746→    assistantIdRef.current = '';
   747→    phaseRef.current = 'thinking';
   748→    sessionIdRef.current = null;
   749→    pendingHistoryRef.current = null;
   750→  }, []);
   751→
   752→  const clearMessages = useCallback((outgoingConversationId?: string | null) => {
   753→    ++streamGenerationRef.current;
   754→    const outgoing = outgoingConversationId || streamConversationIdRef.current;
   755→    activeConversationIdRef.current = null;
   756→    if (abortRef.current) {
   757→      abortRef.current.abort();
   758→      abortRef.current = null;
   759→    }
   760→    if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   761→      clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   762→      flushTimerRef.current = null;
   763→    }
   764→
   765→    // Capture refs before setMessages (React 18 batching may defer the updater)
   766→    const capturedTextBuffer = textBufferRef.current;
   767→    const capturedAssistantId = assistantIdRef.current;
   768→    const capturedSegments = [...segmentsRef.current];
   769→    const capturedCurrentText = currentTextRef.current;
   770→    const capturedPhase = phaseRef.current;
   771→
   772→    setMessages(prev => {
   773→      if (outgoing && prev.length > 0) {
   774→        const pendingText = capturedTextBuffer;
   775→        const aId = capturedAssistantId;
   776→        let finalSegments = [...capturedSegments];
   777→        const pendingSegmentText = capturedCurrentText + pendingText;
   778→        if (pendingSegmentText.trim()) {
   779→          const segType = capturedPhase === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   780→          finalSegments.push({ type: segType as ContentSegment['type'], text: pendingSegmentText });
   781→        }
   782→        const cleaned = prev.map(m => {
   783→          if (m.id === aId && m.isStreaming) {
   784→            return {
   785→              ...m,
   786→              content: m.content + pendingText,
   787→              isStreaming: false,
   788→              currentPhase: undefined,
   789→              segments: finalSegments.length > 0 ? finalSegments : m.segments,
   790→            };
   791→          }
   792→          return m.isStreaming ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined } : m;
   793→        });
   794→        const toCache = cleaned.filter(m => {
   795→          if (m.role === 'assistant' && !m.content?.trim() && (!m.segments || m.segments.length === 0)) {
   796→            return false;
   797→          }
   798→          return true;
   799→        });
   800→        messagesCacheRef.current.set(outgoing, toCache);
   801→      }
   802→      return [];
   803→    });
   804→    setIsStreaming(false);
   805→    textBufferRef.current = '';
   806→    currentTextRef.current = '';
   807→    segmentsRef.current = [];
   808→    assistantIdRef.current = '';
   809→    phaseRef.current = 'thinking';
   810→    sessionIdRef.current = null;
   811→    pendingHistoryRef.current = null;
   812→  }, []);
   813→
   814→  return (
   815→    <AgentContext.Provider
   816→      value={{ messages, isStreaming, sendMessage, editMessage, deleteMessage, clearMessages, loadMessages, respondToQuestion }}
   817→    >
   818→      {children}
   819→    </AgentContext.Provider>
   820→  );
   821→}
   822→
```

     1→import { createContext, useState, useCallback, useContext, ReactNode } from 'react';
     2→import type { ChatMessage, ContentSegment } from '../types';
     3→
     4→interface Conversation {
     5→  id: string;
     6→  title: string | null;
     7→  created_at: string;
     8→  updated_at: string;
     9→  messages?: Array<{
    10→    id: string;
    11→    role: string;
    12→    content: string;
    13→    metadata: string | null;
    14→    sort_order: number;
    15→  }>;
    16→}
    17→
    18→interface ConversationContextType {
    19→  activeConversationId: string | null;
    20→  refreshTrigger: number;
    21→  createConversation: (firstMessage: string) => Promise<string>;
    22→  selectConversation: (id: string) => Promise<ChatMessage[]>;
    23→  deleteConversation: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
    24→  renameConversation: (id: string, title: string) => Promise<void>;
    25→  startNewConversation: () => void;
    26→  triggerRefresh: () => void;
    27→}
    28→
    29→const ConversationContext = createContext<ConversationContextType | null>(null);
    30→
    31→export function ConversationProvider({ children, sessionId }: { children: ReactNode; sessionId: string }) {
    32→  const [activeConversationId, setActiveConversationId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    33→  const [refreshTrigger, setRefreshTrigger] = useState(0);
    34→
    35→  const triggerRefresh = useCallback(() => {
    36→    setRefreshTrigger((n) => n + 1);
    37→  }, []);
    38→
    39→  const createConversation = useCallback(async (firstMessage: string): Promise<string> => {
    40→    const title = firstMessage.length > 50 ? firstMessage.slice(0, 50) + '...' : firstMessage;
    41→    const res = await fetch('/api/conversations', {
    42→      method: 'POST',
    43→      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-Session-ID': sessionId },
    44→      body: JSON.stringify({ title }),
    45→    });
    46→    const conv = await res.json();
    47→    setActiveConversationId(conv.id);
    48→    triggerRefresh();
    49→    return conv.id;
    50→  }, [triggerRefresh, sessionId]);
    51→
    52→  const selectConversation = useCallback(async (id: string): Promise<ChatMessage[]> => {
    53→    const res = await fetch(`/api/conversations/${id}`);
    54→    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load conversation');
    55→    const conv: Conversation = await res.json();
    56→    setActiveConversationId(id);
    57→
    58→    const chatMessages: ChatMessage[] = (conv.messages || []).map((msg) => {
    59→      if (msg.role !== 'assistant') {
    60→        return { id: msg.id, role: 'user' as const, content: msg.content };
    61→      }
    62→
    63→      // Try to restore segments from metadata
    64→      let segments: ContentSegment[] | undefined;
    65→      if (msg.metadata) {
    66→        try {
    67→          const meta = typeof msg.metadata === 'string' ? JSON.parse(msg.metadata) : msg.metadata;
    68→          if (Array.isArray(meta?.segments)) {
    69→            segments = (meta.segments as Array<Record<string, unknown>>).map((seg) => {
    70→              const s: ContentSegment = { type: seg.type as ContentSegment['type'] };
    71→              if (seg.text) s.text = seg.text as string;
    72→              if (seg.toolCallId || seg.toolName) {
    73→                s.toolResult = {
    74→                  toolCallId: (seg.toolCallId as string) || '',
    75→                  toolName: seg.toolName as string,
    76→                  sql: (seg.sql as string) || '',
    77→                  columns: [],
    78→                  rows: [],
    79→                  rowCount: (seg.rowCount as number) || 0,
    80→                  error: seg.error as string | undefined,
    81→                  chart_spec: seg.chart_spec as ContentSegment['chart_spec'],
    82→                };
    83→              }
    84→              if (seg.subagentId) s.subagentId = seg.subagentId as string;
    85→              if (seg.subagentName) s.subagentName = seg.subagentName as string;
    86→              if (seg.sqlResults) s.sqlResults = seg.sqlResults as ContentSegment['sqlResults'];
    87→              if (seg.chart_spec && seg.type === 'subagent_end') {
    88→                s.chart_spec = seg.chart_spec as ContentSegment['chart_spec'];
    89→              }
    90→              return s;
    91→            });
    92→          }
    93→        } catch {
    94→          // metadata parse failed, fall through to default
    95→        }
    96→      }
    97→
    98→      // Fallback: single answer segment from content
    99→      if (!segments) {
   100→        segments = [{ type: 'answer' as const, text: msg.content }];
   101→      }
   102→
   103→      return {
   104→        id: msg.id,
   105→        role: 'assistant' as const,
   106→        content: msg.content,
   107→        segments,
   108→      };
   109→    });
   110→    return chatMessages;
   111→  }, []);
   112→
   113→  const deleteConversation = useCallback(async (id: string) => {
   114→    await fetch(`/api/conversations/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
   115→    if (activeConversationId === id) {
   116→      setActiveConversationId(null);
   117→    }
   118→    triggerRefresh();
   119→  }, [activeConversationId, triggerRefresh]);
   120→
   121→  const renameConversation = useCallback(async (id: string, title: string) => {
   122→    await fetch(`/api/conversations/${id}`, {
   123→      method: 'PUT',
   124→      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
   125→      body: JSON.stringify({ title }),
   126→    });
   127→    triggerRefresh();
   128→  }, [triggerRefresh]);
   129→
   130→  const startNewConversation = useCallback(() => {
   131→    setActiveConversationId(null);
   132→  }, []);
   133→
   134→  return (
   135→    <ConversationContext.Provider
   136→      value={{
   137→        activeConversationId,
   138→        refreshTrigger,
   139→        createConversation,
   140→        selectConversation,
   141→        deleteConversation,
   142→        renameConversation,
   143→        startNewConversation,
   144→        triggerRefresh,
   145→      }}
   146→    >
   147→      {children}
   148→    </ConversationContext.Provider>
   149→  );
   150→}
   151→
   152→export function useConversation() {
   153→  const ctx = useContext(ConversationContext);
   154→  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useConversation must be used within ConversationProvider');
   155→  return ctx;
   156→}
   157→
```

     1→import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
     2→import { useTranslation } from '../hooks/useTranslation';
     3→import { useAgent } from '../hooks/useAgent';
     4→import { useConversation } from '../contexts/ConversationContext';
     5→import { SlashCommandMenu } from './SlashCommandMenu';
     6→import { fetchSkills } from '../services/skillsService';
     7→import type { SkillInfo } from '../types';
     8→import './ChatInput.css';
     9→
    10→interface ChatInputProps {
    11→  pendingSkillCommand?: string | null;
    12→  onSkillCommandConsumed?: () => void;
    13→}
    14→
    15→export function ChatInput({ pendingSkillCommand, onSkillCommandConsumed }: ChatInputProps) {
    16→  const { t } = useTranslation();
    17→  const { sendMessage, isStreaming } = useAgent();
    18→  const { activeConversationId, createConversation, triggerRefresh } = useConversation();
    19→  const [text, setText] = useState('');
    20→  const textareaRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);
    21→
    22→  const [skills, setSkills] = useState<SkillInfo[]>([]);
    23→  const [showSlashMenu, setShowSlashMenu] = useState(false);
    24→  const [slashFilter, setSlashFilter] = useState('');
    25→
    26→  // Load skills on mount and refresh when toggled/updated
    27→  useEffect(() => {
    28→    fetchSkills().then(setSkills).catch(() => {});
    29→    const handler = () => { fetchSkills().then(setSkills).catch(() => {}); };
    30→    window.addEventListener('skills-updated', handler);
    31→    return () => window.removeEventListener('skills-updated', handler);
    32→  }, []);
    33→
    34→  // Consume pending skill command from sidebar "Use" button
    35→  useEffect(() => {
    36→    if (pendingSkillCommand) {
    37→      setText(prev => {
    38→        const trimmed = prev.trim();
    39→        const command = `${t('useSkillPlaceholder')} /${pendingSkillCommand}`;
    40→        return trimmed ? `${trimmed} ${command}` : command;
    41→      });
    42→      onSkillCommandConsumed?.();
    43→      textareaRef.current?.focus();
    44→    }
    45→  }, [pendingSkillCommand, onSkillCommandConsumed]);
    46→
    47→  const handleSend = async () => {
    48→    const trimmed = text.trim();
    49→    if (!trimmed || isStreaming) return;
    50→    setText('');
    51→    setShowSlashMenu(false);
    52→    if (textareaRef.current) {
    53→      textareaRef.current.style.height = 'auto';
    54→    }
    55→
    56→    let convId = activeConversationId;
    57→    if (!convId) {
    58→      convId = await createConversation(trimmed);
    59→    }
    60→    await sendMessage(trimmed, convId);
    61→    triggerRefresh();
    62→  };
    63→
    64→  const handleKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    65→    // When slash menu is open, let it handle arrow keys and Enter
    66→    if (showSlashMenu) {
    67→      if (['ArrowDown', 'ArrowUp', 'Enter', 'Escape'].includes(e.key)) {
    68→        return; // SlashCommandMenu handles these via document listener
    69→      }
    70→    }
    71→
    72→    if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey && !e.nativeEvent.isComposing) {
    73→      e.preventDefault();
    74→      handleSend();
    75→    }
    76→  };
    77→
    78→  const slashStartRef = useRef<number>(-1);
    79→
    80→  const handleInput = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLTextAreaElement>) => {
    81→    const val = e.target.value;
    82→    const cursorPos = e.target.selectionStart;
    83→    setText(val);
    84→
    85→    // Find the slash token at or before the cursor
    86→    const beforeCursor = val.slice(0, cursorPos);
    87→    const slashIdx = beforeCursor.lastIndexOf('/');
    88→    if (slashIdx !== -1) {
    89→      const token = beforeCursor.slice(slashIdx + 1);
    90→      // Valid slash command: no spaces in the token
    91→      if (!token.includes(' ')) {
    92→        slashStartRef.current = slashIdx;
    93→        setShowSlashMenu(true);
    94→        setSlashFilter(token);
    95→      } else {
    96→        setShowSlashMenu(false);
    97→      }
    98→    } else {
    99→      setShowSlashMenu(false);
   100→    }
   101→
   102→    const el = e.target;
   103→    el.style.height = 'auto';
   104→    el.style.height = Math.min(el.scrollHeight, 150) + 'px';
   105→  };
   106→
   107→  const handleSlashSelect = (skillName: string) => {
   108→    const start = slashStartRef.current;
   109→    const cursorPos = textareaRef.current?.selectionStart ?? text.length;
   110→    const before = text.slice(0, start);
   111→    const after = text.slice(cursorPos);
   112→    setText(`${before}/${skillName} ${after}`);
   113→    setShowSlashMenu(false);
   114→    slashStartRef.current = -1;
   115→    // Set cursor after the inserted command
   116→    const newCursorPos = start + skillName.length + 2; // "/" + name + " "
   117→    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   118→      textareaRef.current?.focus();
   119→      textareaRef.current?.setSelectionRange(newCursorPos, newCursorPos);
   120→    });
   121→  };
   122→
   123→  return (
   124→    <div className="chat-input">
   125→      <div className="chat-input__textarea-wrapper">
   126→        {showSlashMenu && skills.length > 0 && (
   127→          <SlashCommandMenu
   128→            skills={skills}
   129→            filter={slashFilter}
   130→            onSelect={handleSlashSelect}
   131→            onClose={() => setShowSlashMenu(false)}
   132→          />
   133→        )}
   134→        <textarea
   135→          ref={textareaRef}
   136→          className="chat-input__textarea"
   137→          value={text}
   138→          onChange={handleInput}
   139→          onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
   140→          placeholder={isStreaming ? '' : t('chatPlaceholder')}
   141→          disabled={isStreaming}
   142→          rows={1}
   143→        />
   144→        {isStreaming && (
   145→          <span className="chat-input__shimmer">{t('chatPlaceholderWaiting')}</span>
   146→        )}
   147→      </div>
   148→      <button
   149→        className="chat-input__send"
   150→        onClick={handleSend}
   151→        disabled={isStreaming || !text.trim()}
   152→      >
   153→        {t('send')}
   154→      </button>
   155→    </div>
   156→  );
   157→}
   158→

     1→from fastapi import APIRouter, Header, Query
     2→from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
     3→from pydantic import BaseModel
     4→
     5→from app.memory_store import memory_store
     6→
     7→router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/conversations", tags=["conversations"])
     8→
     9→
    10→class CreateConversationRequest(BaseModel):
    11→    user_id: str = "default"
    12→    title: str | None = None
    13→
    14→
    15→class UpdateConversationRequest(BaseModel):
    16→    title: str
    17→
    18→
    19→@router.get("")
    20→async def list_conversations(
    21→    user_id: str = Query("default"),
    22→    limit: int = Query(50),
    23→    offset: int = Query(0),
    24→    x_session_id: str = Header(""),
    25→):
    26→    return memory_store.list_conversations(
    27→        user_id=user_id, limit=limit, offset=offset, session_id=x_session_id,
    28→    )
    29→
    30→
    31→@router.post("")
    32→async def create_conversation(
    33→    request: CreateConversationRequest,
    34→    x_session_id: str = Header(""),
    35→):
    36→    return memory_store.create_conversation(
    37→        user_id=request.user_id, title=request.title, session_id=x_session_id,
    38→    )
    39→
    40→
    41→@router.get("/{conversation_id}")
    42→async def get_conversation(conversation_id: str):
    43→    conv = memory_store.get_conversation(conversation_id)
    44→    if conv is None:
    45→        return JSONResponse(status_code=404, content={"error": "Conversation not found"})
    46→    return conv
    47→
    48→
    49→@router.put("/{conversation_id}")
    50→async def update_conversation(conversation_id: str, request: UpdateConversationRequest):
    51→    if not memory_store.update_conversation(conversation_id, request.title):
    52→        return JSONResponse(status_code=404, content={"error": "Conversation not found"})
    53→    return {"status": "ok"}
    54→
    55→
    56→@router.delete("/{conversation_id}")
    57→async def delete_conversation(conversation_id: str):
    58→    if not memory_store.delete_conversation(conversation_id):
    59→        return JSONResponse(status_code=404, content={"error": "Conversation not found"})
    60→    return {"status": "ok"}
    61→

     1→"""SQLite-backed memory store for conversations and messages."""
     2→
     3→import json
     4→import os
     5→import sqlite3
     6→import threading
     7→import uuid
     8→from datetime import datetime, timezone
     9→
    10→from app.config import MEMORY_DB_PATH
    11→
    12→
    13→def _now_iso() -> str:
    14→    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
    15→
    16→
    17→class MemoryStore:
    18→    """Thread-safe SQLite store for conversation history.
    19→
    20→    Uses WAL mode for concurrent reads, a threading.Lock for write
    21→    serialisation, and CASCADE deletes on messages.
    22→    """
    23→
    24→    def __init__(self, db_path: str = MEMORY_DB_PATH) -> None:
    25→        self._db_path = db_path
    26→        self._lock = threading.Lock()
    27→        self._ensure_dir()
    28→        self._init_schema()
    29→
    30→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    31→    # Internal helpers
    32→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    33→
    34→    def _ensure_dir(self) -> None:
    35→        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(self._db_path), exist_ok=True)
    36→
    37→    def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
    38→        conn = sqlite3.connect(self._db_path)
    39→        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    40→        conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
    41→        return conn
    42→
    43→    def _init_schema(self) -> None:
    44→        with self._lock:
    45→            conn = self._connect()
    46→            try:
    47→                conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
    48→                conn.executescript(
    49→                    """
    50→                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS conversations (
    51→                        id         TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    52→                        user_id    TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'default',
    53→                        title      TEXT,
    54→                        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    55→                        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
    56→                    );
    57→
    58→                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS messages (
    59→                        id              TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    60→                        conversation_id TEXT NOT NULL
    61→                            REFERENCES conversations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    62→                        role            TEXT NOT NULL,
    63→                        content         TEXT NOT NULL,
    64→                        metadata        TEXT,
    65→                        created_at      TEXT NOT NULL,
    66→                        sort_order      INTEGER NOT NULL
    67→                    );
    68→
    69→                    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_messages_conv_order
    70→                        ON messages(conversation_id, sort_order);
    71→
    72→                    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_conversations_user_updated
    73→                        ON conversations(user_id, updated_at);
    74→                    """
    75→                )
    76→                # Migration: add session_id column
    77→                try:
    78→                    conn.execute(
    79→                        "ALTER TABLE conversations ADD COLUMN session_id TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''"
    80→                    )
    81→                except sqlite3.OperationalError as exc:
    82→                    if "duplicate column name" not in str(exc):
    83→                        raise
    84→                conn.execute(
    85→                    "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_conversations_session "
    86→                    "ON conversations(session_id)"
    87→                )
    88→                conn.commit()
    89→            finally:
    90→                conn.close()
    91→
    92→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    93→    # Conversations
    94→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    95→
    96→    def create_conversation(
    97→        self,
    98→        user_id: str = "default",
    99→        title: str | None = None,
   100→        session_id: str = "",
   101→    ) -> dict:
   102→        now = _now_iso()
   103→        conv_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
   104→        row = {
   105→            "id": conv_id,
   106→            "user_id": user_id,
   107→            "title": title,
   108→            "session_id": session_id,
   109→            "created_at": now,
   110→            "updated_at": now,
   111→        }
   112→        with self._lock:
   113→            conn = self._connect()
   114→            try:
   115→                conn.execute(
   116→                    """
   117→                    INSERT INTO conversations (id, user_id, title, session_id, created_at, updated_at)
   118→                    VALUES (:id, :user_id, :title, :session_id, :created_at, :updated_at)
   119→                    """,
   120→                    row,
   121→                )
   122→                conn.commit()
   123→            finally:
   124→                conn.close()
   125→        return row
   126→
   127→    def list_conversations(
   128→        self,
   129→        user_id: str = "default",
   130→        limit: int = 50,
   131→        offset: int = 0,
   132→        session_id: str = "",
   133→    ) -> list[dict]:
   134→        with self._lock:
   135→            conn = self._connect()
   136→            try:
   137→                rows = conn.execute(
   138→                    """
   139→                    SELECT * FROM conversations
   140→                    WHERE user_id = ? AND session_id = ?
   141→                    ORDER BY updated_at DESC
   142→                    LIMIT ? OFFSET ?
   143→                    """,
   144→                    (user_id, session_id, limit, offset),
   145→                ).fetchall()
   146→            finally:
   147→                conn.close()
   148→        return [dict(r) for r in rows]
   149→
   150→    def get_conversation(self, conversation_id: str) -> dict | None:
   151→        with self._lock:
   152→            conn = self._connect()
   153→            try:
   154→                row = conn.execute(
   155→                    "SELECT * FROM conversations WHERE id = ?",
   156→                    (conversation_id,),
   157→                ).fetchone()
   158→                if row is None:
   159→                    return None
   160→                conv = dict(row)
   161→                msgs = conn.execute(
   162→                    """
   163→                    SELECT * FROM messages
   164→                    WHERE conversation_id = ?
   165→                    ORDER BY sort_order
   166→                    """,
   167→                    (conversation_id,),
   168→                ).fetchall()
   169→            finally:
   170→                conn.close()
   171→        conv["messages"] = [dict(m) for m in msgs]
   172→        return conv
   173→
   174→    def update_conversation(self, conversation_id: str, title: str) -> bool:
   175→        with self._lock:
   176→            conn = self._connect()
   177→            try:
   178→                cur = conn.execute(
   179→                    """
   180→                    UPDATE conversations
   181→                    SET title = ?, updated_at = ?
   182→                    WHERE id = ?
   183→                    """,
   184→                    (title, _now_iso(), conversation_id),
   185→                )
   186→                conn.commit()
   187→                changed = cur.rowcount > 0
   188→            finally:
   189→                conn.close()
   190→        return changed
   191→
   192→    def delete_conversation(self, conversation_id: str) -> bool:
   193→        with self._lock:
   194→            conn = self._connect()
   195→            try:
   196→                cur = conn.execute(
   197→                    "DELETE FROM conversations WHERE id = ?",
   198→                    (conversation_id,),
   199→                )
   200→                conn.commit()
   201→                changed = cur.rowcount > 0
   202→            finally:
   203→                conn.close()
   204→        return changed
   205→
   206→    def delete_conversations_by_session(self, session_id: str) -> int:
   207→        """Delete all conversations with the given session_id.
   208→
   209→        Returns the number of deleted rows.
   210→        """
   211→        if not session_id:
   212→            return 0
   213→        with self._lock:
   214→            conn = self._connect()
   215→            try:
   216→                cur = conn.execute(
   217→                    "DELETE FROM conversations WHERE session_id = ?",
   218→                    (session_id,),
   219→                )
   220→                conn.commit()
   221→                deleted = cur.rowcount
   222→            finally:
   223→                conn.close()
   224→        return deleted
   225→
   226→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   227→    # Messages
   228→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   229→
   230→    def add_message(
   231→        self,
   232→        conversation_id: str,
   233→        role: str,
   234→        content: str,
   235→        metadata: dict | None = None,
   236→    ) -> dict:
   237→        msg_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
   238→        now = _now_iso()
   239→        meta_json = json.dumps(metadata) if metadata is not None else None
   240→
   241→        with self._lock:
   242→            conn = self._connect()
   243→            try:
   244→                # Determine next sort_order for this conversation.
   245→                row = conn.execute(
   246→                    """
   247→                    SELECT COALESCE(MAX(sort_order), -1) + 1 AS next_order
   248→                    FROM messages
   249→                    WHERE conversation_id = ?
   250→                    """,
   251→                    (conversation_id,),
   252→                ).fetchone()
   253→                next_order = row["next_order"]
   254→
   255→                conn.execute(
   256→                    """
   257→                    INSERT INTO messages
   258→                        (id, conversation_id, role, content, metadata, created_at, sort_order)
   259→                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
   260→                    """,
   261→                    (msg_id, conversation_id, role, content, meta_json, now, next_order),
   262→                )
   263→                # Touch the conversation's updated_at timestamp.
   264→                conn.execute(
   265→                    "UPDATE conversations SET updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
   266→                    (now, conversation_id),
   267→                )
   268→                conn.commit()
   269→            finally:
   270→                conn.close()
   271→
   272→        return {
   273→            "id": msg_id,
   274→            "conversation_id": conversation_id,
   275→            "role": role,
   276→            "content": content,
   277→            "metadata": meta_json,
   278→            "created_at": now,
   279→            "sort_order": next_order,
   280→        }
   281→
   282→    def list_messages(self, conversation_id: str) -> list[dict]:
   283→        with self._lock:
   284→            conn = self._connect()
   285→            try:
   286→                rows = conn.execute(
   287→                    """
   288→                    SELECT * FROM messages
   289→                    WHERE conversation_id = ?
   290→                    ORDER BY sort_order
   291→                    """,
   292→                    (conversation_id,),
   293→                ).fetchall()
   294→            finally:
   295→                conn.close()
   296→        return [dict(r) for r in rows]
   297→
   298→
   299→# Module-level singleton
   300→memory_store = MemoryStore()
   301→
```

     1→import type { ToolCallResult, UserQuestionData, UserQuestionOption } from '../types';
     2→
     3→interface AgentCallbacks {
     4→  onTextChunk: (text: string) => void;
     5→  onThinkingDone: () => void;
     6→  onToolCall: (pending: ToolCallResult) => void;
     7→  onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => void;
     8→  onSubagentStart?: (data: { id: string; name: string; prompt: string }) => void;
     9→  onSubagentEnd?: (data: { id: string; name: string; result?: string; sql_results?: Array<{ sql: string; columns?: string[]; rows?: Record<string, unknown>[]; rowCount?: number; error?: string }>; chart_spec?: { data: unknown[]; layout?: Record<string, unknown> } }) => void;
    10→  onDone: (sessionId: string | null) => void;
    11→  onError: (error: string) => void;
    12→  onUserQuestion?: (data: UserQuestionData) => void;
    13→}
    14→
    15→export type { AgentCallbacks };
    16→
    17→async function streamSSE(
    18→  response: Response,
    19→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
    20→  signal?: AbortSignal,
    21→): Promise<void> {
    22→  const reader = response.body?.getReader();
    23→  if (!reader) {
    24→    callbacks.onError('No response stream');
    25→    return;
    26→  }
    27→
    28→  let doneReceived = false;
    29→  const wrappedCallbacks: AgentCallbacks = {
    30→    ...callbacks,
    31→    onDone: (sessionId) => {
    32→      doneReceived = true;
    33→      callbacks.onDone(sessionId);
    34→    },
    35→    onError: (error) => {
    36→      doneReceived = true;
    37→      callbacks.onError(error);
    38→    },
    39→  };
    40→
    41→  try {
    42→    const decoder = new TextDecoder();
    43→    let buffer = '';
    44→    let eventType = '';
    45→
    46→    while (true) {
    47→      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
    48→      if (done) break;
    49→
    50→      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
    51→      const lines = buffer.split('\n');
    52→      buffer = lines.pop() ?? '';
    53→      for (const line of lines) {
    54→        if (line.startsWith('event: ')) {
    55→          eventType = line.slice(7).trim();
    56→        } else if (line.startsWith('data: ')) {
    57→          const data = line.slice(6);
    58→          try {
    59→            const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
    60→            handleSSEEvent(eventType, parsed, wrappedCallbacks);
    61→          } catch {
    62→            // Skip malformed JSON
    63→          }
    64→          eventType = '';
    65→        }
    66→      }
    67→    }
    68→
    69→    // Process any remaining data left in buffer after stream ends
    70→    if (buffer.trim()) {
    71→      const remainingLines = buffer.split('\n');
    72→      for (const line of remainingLines) {
    73→        if (line.startsWith('event: ')) {
    74→          eventType = line.slice(7).trim();
    75→        } else if (line.startsWith('data: ')) {
    76→          const data = line.slice(6);
    77→          try {
    78→            const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
    79→            handleSSEEvent(eventType, parsed, wrappedCallbacks);
    80→          } catch {
    81→            // Skip malformed JSON
    82→          }
    83→          eventType = '';
    84→        }
    85→      }
    86→    }
    87→
    88→    // Safety net: if stream ended without done/error, force done
    89→    if (!doneReceived) {
    90→      callbacks.onDone(null);
    91→    }
    92→  } catch (e: unknown) {
    93→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
    94→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
    95→    callbacks.onError(msg);
    96→  }
    97→}
    98→
    99→export async function runAgentLoop(
   100→  message: string,
   101→  agentSessionId: string | null,
   102→  langfuseSessionId: string | null,
   103→  conversationHistory: { role: string; content: string }[] | null,
   104→  conversationId?: string | null,
   105→  callbacks?: AgentCallbacks,
   106→  signal?: AbortSignal,
   107→  userSessionId?: string,
   108→  skills?: string[],
   109→): Promise<void> {
   110→  if (!callbacks) return;
   111→  try {
   112→    const response = await fetch('/api/chat', {
   113→      method: 'POST',
   114→      headers: {
   115→        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   116→        ...(userSessionId ? { 'X-Session-ID': userSessionId } : {}),
   117→      },
   118→      body: JSON.stringify({
   119→        message,
   120→        session_id: agentSessionId,
   121→        conversation_id: conversationId,
   122→        langfuse_session_id: langfuseSessionId,
   123→        conversation_history: conversationHistory ?? [],
   124→        ...(skills?.length ? { skills } : {}),
   125→      }),
   126→      signal,
   127→    });
   128→
   129→    if (!response.ok) {
   130→      const errorText = await response.text();
   131→      callbacks.onError(`Server error: ${errorText}`);
   132→      return;
   133→    }
   134→
   135→    await streamSSE(response, callbacks, signal);
   136→  } catch (e: unknown) {
   137→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
   138→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
   139→    callbacks.onError(msg);
   140→  }
   141→}
   142→
   143→export async function runAgentEditLoop(
   144→  newMessage: string,
   145→  conversationHistory: { role: string; content: string }[],
   146→  langfuseSessionId: string | null,
   147→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
   148→  signal?: AbortSignal,
   149→  userSessionId?: string,
   150→): Promise<void> {
   151→  try {
   152→    const response = await fetch('/api/chat/edit', {
   153→      method: 'POST',
   154→      headers: {
   155→        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   156→        ...(userSessionId ? { 'X-Session-ID': userSessionId } : {}),
   157→      },
   158→      body: JSON.stringify({
   159→        new_message: newMessage,
   160→        conversation_history: conversationHistory,
   161→        langfuse_session_id: langfuseSessionId,
   162→      }),
   163→      signal,
   164→    });
   165→
   166→    if (!response.ok) {
   167→      const errorText = await response.text();
   168→      callbacks.onError(`Server error: ${errorText}`);
   169→      return;
   170→    }
   171→
   172→    await streamSSE(response, callbacks, signal);
   173→  } catch (e: unknown) {
   174→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
   175→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
   176→    callbacks.onError(msg);
   177→  }
   178→}
   179→
   180→function handleSSEEvent(
   181→  eventType: string,
   182→  data: Record<string, unknown>,
   183→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
   184→) {
   185→  switch (eventType) {
   186→    case 'thinking':
   187→    case 'answer':
   188→      callbacks.onTextChunk(data.text as string);
   189→      break;
   190→    case 'thinking_done':
   191→      callbacks.onThinkingDone();
   192→      break;
   193→    case 'tool_call':
   194→      callbacks.onToolCall({
   195→        toolCallId: (data.id as string) ?? '',
   196→        toolName: (data.name as string) ?? undefined,
   197→        sql: (data.sql as string) ?? '',
   198→        command: (data.command as string) ?? undefined,
   199→        toolInput: (data.input as Record<string, unknown>) ?? undefined,
   200→        columns: [],
   201→        rows: [],
   202→        rowCount: 0,
   203→      });
   204→      break;
   205→    case 'tool_result': {
   206→      const result: ToolCallResult = {
   207→        toolCallId: (data.id as string) ?? '',
   208→        toolName: (data.name as string) ?? undefined,
   209→        sql: (data.sql as string) ?? '',
   210→        columns: (data.columns as string[]) ?? [],
   211→        rows: (data.rows as Record<string, unknown>[]) ?? [],
   212→        rowCount: (data.rowCount as number) ?? 0,
   213→        error: (data.error as string) ?? undefined,
   214→        output: (data.output as string) ?? undefined,
   215→        rawContent: (data.content as string) ?? undefined,
   216→        chart_spec: (data.chart_spec as { data: unknown[]; layout?: Record<string, unknown> }) ?? undefined,
   217→      };
   218→      callbacks.onToolResult(result);
   219→      break;
   220→    }
   221→    case 'subagent_start':
   222→      callbacks.onSubagentStart?.({
   223→        id: data.id as string,
   224→        name: data.name as string,
   225→        prompt: (data.prompt as string) ?? '',
   226→      });
   227→      break;
   228→    case 'subagent_end': {
   229→      type ChartSpec = { data: unknown[]; layout?: Record<string, unknown>; frames?: unknown[] };
   230→      // Backend sends chart_specs (array) for multi-chart support.
   231→      // Fall back to legacy chart_spec (single object) for backwards compat.
   232→      const rawSpecs = data.chart_specs as ChartSpec[] | undefined;
   233→      const legacySpec = data.chart_spec as ChartSpec | undefined;
   234→      const chartSpecs: ChartSpec[] = rawSpecs ?? (legacySpec ? [legacySpec] : []);
   235→
   236→      if (chartSpecs.length > 0) {
   237→        // Emit one callback per chart so each gets its own segment
   238→        for (const spec of chartSpecs) {
   239→          callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
   240→            id: data.id as string,
   241→            name: data.name as string,
   242→            chart_spec: spec,
   243→          });
   244→        }
   245→        // Emit text result separately (if any) so it doesn't get lost
   246→        if (data.result) {
   247→          callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
   248→            id: data.id as string,
   249→            name: data.name as string,
   250→            result: data.result as string,
   251→          });
   252→        }
   253→      } else {
   254→        callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
   255→          id: data.id as string,
   256→          name: data.name as string,
   257→          result: (data.result as string) ?? undefined,
   258→          sql_results: (data.sql_results as Array<{ sql: string; columns?: string[]; rows?: Record<string, unknown>[]; rowCount?: number; error?: string }>) ?? undefined,
   259→        });
   260→      }
   261→      break;
   262→    }
   263→    case 'user_question': {
   264→      // Normalize options: the agent may send plain strings or {label, description} objects
   265→      const rawOptions = (data.options as unknown[]) ?? [];
   266→      const normalizedOptions: UserQuestionOption[] = (Array.isArray(rawOptions) ? rawOptions : []).map(
   267→        (opt: unknown) => {
   268→          if (typeof opt === 'string') return { label: opt };
   269→          if (typeof opt === 'object' && opt !== null && 'label' in opt) return opt as UserQuestionOption;
   270→          return { label: String(opt) };
   271→        }
   272→      );
   273→      callbacks.onUserQuestion?.({
   274→        questionId: data.question_id as string,
   275→        question: data.question as string,
   276→        options: normalizedOptions,
   277→        multiSelect: (data.multi_select as boolean) ?? false,
   278→      });
   279→      break;
   280→    }
   281→    case 'done':
   282→      callbacks.onDone((data.session_id as string) ?? null);
   283→      break;
   284→    case 'error':
   285→      callbacks.onError((data.message as string) ?? 'Unknown error');
   286→      break;
   287→  }
   288→}
   289→
