     1→import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
     2→import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
     3→import remarkGfm from 'remark-gfm';
     4→import { useTranslation } from '../hooks/useTranslation';
     5→import type { ChatMessage, ContentSegment } from '../types';
     6→import { useAgent } from '../hooks/useAgent';
     7→import { InlineQueryResult } from './InlineQueryResult';
     8→import { ChartWidget } from './ChartWidget';
     9→import { UserQuestion } from './UserQuestion';
    10→import './MessageBubble.css';
    11→
    12→/**
    13→ * Strip chart_spec JSON code blocks from answer text.
    14→ * The orchestrator may include the raw chart spec JSON even though the chart
    15→ * is rendered separately. Remove ```json ... ``` blocks containing chart_spec
    16→ * or Plotly trace data so they don't appear as raw text.
    17→ */
    18→function stripChartSpecBlocks(text: string): string {
    19→  return text.replace(/```(?:json)?\s*\n?\s*\{[\s\S]*?"(?:chart_spec|data)"[\s\S]*?\}\s*\n?\s*```/g, '').trim();
    20→}
    21→
    22→/**
    23→ * Fix missing line breaks before bold section headers in text.
    24→ * The model sometimes concatenates bold headers directly after the previous
    25→ * sentence (e.g. "...for arrays.**Preparing data arrays**"). This inserts
    26→ * paragraph breaks so they render on new lines.
    27→ */
    28→function fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(text: string): string {
    29→  // Insert \n\n before **Header** when preceded by sentence-ending punctuation
    30→  // with no whitespace. The [A-Z] ensures we only match section-header-style
    31→  // bold (starting with a capital letter), avoiding inline bold like **data**.
    32→  return text.replace(/([.!?:])(\*\*[A-Z])/g, '$1\n\n$2');
    33→}
    34→
    35→/** Strip markdown formatting for plain-text preview display. */
    36→function stripMarkdown(text: string): string {
    37→  return text
    38→    .replace(/\*\*(.+?)\*\*/g, '$1')   // **bold**
    39→    .replace(/__(.+?)__/g, '$1')        // __bold__
    40→    .replace(/\*(.+?)\*/g, '$1')        // *italic*
    41→    .replace(/_(.+?)_/g, '$1')          // _italic_
    42→    .replace(/`(.+?)`/g, '$1')          // `code`
    43→    .replace(/^#+\s+/gm, '');           // # headings
    44→}
    45→
    46→function getLastThinkingLine(segments: ContentSegment[], streamingRemainder: string | undefined, t: (key: string) => string): string {
    47→  // Use streaming remainder if available
    48→  if (streamingRemainder?.trim()) {
    49→    const lines = streamingRemainder.trim().split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim());
    50→    const last = stripMarkdown(lines[lines.length - 1] || '');
    51→    return last.length > 100 ? last.slice(0, 100) + '...' : last;
    52→  }
    53→  // Otherwise use last thinking segment's last line
    54→  for (let i = segments.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    55→    if (segments[i].type === 'thinking' && segments[i].text?.trim()) {
    56→      const lines = segments[i].text!.trim().split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim());
    57→      const last = stripMarkdown(lines[lines.length - 1] || '');
    58→      return last.length > 100 ? last.slice(0, 100) + '...' : last;
    59→    }
    60→  }
    61→  return t('thinking');
    62→}
    63→
    64→function ThinkingBlock({ segments, streamingRemainder, isThinkingPhase, isAgentStreaming, hasAnswer }: {
    65→  segments: ContentSegment[];
    66→  streamingRemainder?: string;
    67→  isThinkingPhase: boolean;
    68→  isAgentStreaming: boolean;
    69→  hasAnswer: boolean;
    70→}) {
    71→  const { t } = useTranslation();
    72→  // All non-answer, non-chart, non-user_question segments go inside the thinking block.
    73→  // subagent_end with sqlResults always stays in thinking; text-only subagent_end without
    74→  // answer goes to the answer block as a preliminary result.
    75→  const thinkingSegments = segments.filter(
    76→    (s) => s.type !== 'answer' && s.type !== 'user_question' && !(s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.chart_spec) && !(s.type === 'subagent_end' && (s.chart_spec || (!s.sqlResults?.length && s.text?.trim() && !hasAnswer)))
    77→  );
    78→  const hasContent = thinkingSegments.some(
    79→    (s) => (s.type === 'thinking' && s.text?.trim()) || (s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult) || s.type === 'subagent_start' || (s.type === 'subagent_end' && !s.chart_spec && (s.text?.trim() || s.sqlResults?.length))
    80→  ) || streamingRemainder?.trim();
    81→
    82→  if (!hasContent) return null;
    83→
    84→  // Show preview only while agent is still working (hidden by CSS when collapsible is open anyway)
    85→  const summary = isAgentStreaming ? getLastThinkingLine(segments, streamingRemainder, t) : '';
    86→
    87→  return (
    88→    <details className="message-bubble__segment message-bubble__segment--thinking message-bubble__collapsible" open={isThinkingPhase || (!hasAnswer && !isAgentStreaming) || undefined}>
    89→      <summary className="message-bubble__collapsible-summary">
    90→        <span className="message-bubble__segment-label">{t('thinkingLabel')}</span>
    91→        {summary && <span className="message-bubble__collapsible-preview">{summary}</span>}
    92→      </summary>
    93→      <div className="message-bubble__thinking-body">
    94→        {thinkingSegments.map((seg, i) => {
    95→          if (seg.type === 'thinking' && seg.text?.trim()) {
    96→            return (
    97→              <div key={i} className="message-bubble__segment-content">
    98→                <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(seg.text)}</ReactMarkdown>
    99→              </div>
   100→            );
   101→          }
   102→          if (seg.type === 'tool' && seg.toolResult) {
   103→            return (
   104→              <div key={i} className="message-bubble__tool-segment">
   105→                <InlineQueryResult result={seg.toolResult} />
   106→              </div>
   107→            );
   108→          }
   109→          if (seg.type === 'subagent_start') {
   110→            const displayName = seg.subagentName === 'sql-analyst'
   111→              ? t('sqlAnalystWorking')
   112→              : `${seg.subagentName} working...`;
   113→            return (
   114→              <div key={i} className="message-bubble__subagent-indicator">
   115→                <span className="message-bubble__subagent-label">{displayName}</span>
   116→              </div>
   117→            );
   118→          }
   119→          if (seg.type === 'subagent_end' && !seg.chart_spec && (seg.sqlResults?.length || seg.text?.trim())) {
   120→            // Render SQL results using InlineQueryResult — same as direct tool calls
   121→            if (seg.sqlResults?.length) {
   122→              return (
   123→                <div key={i}>
   124→                  {seg.sqlResults.map((sqlResult, qi) => (
   125→                    <div key={qi} className="message-bubble__tool-segment">
   126→                      <InlineQueryResult result={{
   127→                        toolCallId: `${seg.subagentId}-sql-${qi}`,
   128→                        toolName: 'execute_sql',
   129→                        sql: sqlResult.sql,
   130→                        columns: sqlResult.columns ?? [],
   131→                        rows: sqlResult.rows ?? [],
   132→                        rowCount: sqlResult.rowCount ?? 0,
   133→                        error: sqlResult.error,
   134→                      }} />
   135→                    </div>
   136→                  ))}
   137→                </div>
   138→              );
   139→            }
   140→            return (
   141→              <div key={i} className="message-bubble__segment-content">
   142→                <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{seg.text!}</ReactMarkdown>
   143→              </div>
   144→            );
   145→          }
   146→          return null;
   147→        })}
   148→        {streamingRemainder?.trim() && (
   149→          <div className="message-bubble__segment-content">
   150→            <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(streamingRemainder)}</ReactMarkdown>
   151→          </div>
   152→        )}
   153→      </div>
   154→    </details>
   155→  );
   156→}
   157→
   158→function ErrorBlock({ errorMessage, onRetry }: { errorMessage: string; onRetry?: () => void }) {
   159→  const { t } = useTranslation();
   160→  return (
   161→    <div className="message-bubble__segment message-bubble__segment--error">
   162→      <div className="message-bubble__error-header">{t('errorOccurred')}</div>
   163→      <div className="message-bubble__error-message">{errorMessage}</div>
   164→      {onRetry && (
   165→        <button className="message-bubble__error-retry" onClick={onRetry}>
   166→          {t('tryAgain')}
   167→        </button>
   168→      )}
   169→    </div>
   170→  );
   171→}
   172→
   173→export function MessageBubble({ message, messageIndex }: { message: ChatMessage; messageIndex: number }) {
   174→  const { t } = useTranslation();
   175→  const { isStreaming, editMessage, deleteMessage, sendMessage, messages } = useAgent();
   176→  const [isEditing, setIsEditing] = useState(false);
   177→  const [editText, setEditText] = useState(message.content);
   178→  const [isConfirmingDelete, setIsConfirmingDelete] = useState(false);
   179→  const textareaRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);
   180→
   181→  const isUser = message.role === 'user';
   182→  const hasSegments = !isUser && message.segments && message.segments.length > 0;
   183→
   184→  useEffect(() => {
   185→    if (isEditing && textareaRef.current) {
   186→      textareaRef.current.focus();
   187→      textareaRef.current.selectionStart = textareaRef.current.value.length;
   188→    }
   189→  }, [isEditing]);
   190→
   191→  const handleEdit = () => {
   192→    setEditText(message.content);
   193→    setIsEditing(true);
   194→    setIsConfirmingDelete(false);
   195→  };
   196→
   197→  const handleCancelEdit = () => {
   198→    setIsEditing(false);
   199→    setEditText(message.content);
   200→  };
   201→
   202→  const handleSaveEdit = () => {
   203→    const trimmed = editText.trim();
   204→    if (!trimmed || trimmed === message.content) {
   205→      handleCancelEdit();
   206→      return;
   207→    }
   208→    setIsEditing(false);
   209→    editMessage(messageIndex, trimmed);
   210→  };
   211→
   212→  const handleDeleteConfirm = () => {
   213→    setIsConfirmingDelete(false);
   214→    deleteMessage(messageIndex);
   215→  };
   216→
   217→  const handleKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
   218→    if (e.key === 'Escape') {
   219→      handleCancelEdit();
   220→    } else if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey && !e.nativeEvent.isComposing) {
   221→      e.preventDefault();
   222→      handleSaveEdit();
   223→    }
   224→  };
   225→
   226→  let streamingRemainder: string | undefined;
   227→  if (hasSegments && message.isStreaming && message.content) {
   228→    const segmentedText = message.segments!
   229→      .filter((s) => s.type === 'thinking' || s.type === 'answer')
   230→      .map((s) => s.text || '')
   231→      .join('');
   232→    const remaining = message.content.slice(segmentedText.length);
   233→    if (remaining.trim()) {
   234→      streamingRemainder = remaining;
   235→    }
   236→  }
   237→
   238→  const hasAnswer = !!hasSegments && message.segments!.some((s) => s.type === 'answer');
   239→  const isInAnswerPhase = message.currentPhase === 'answer';
   240→  const isThinkingPhase = !!message.isStreaming && !hasAnswer && !isInAnswerPhase;
   241→
   242→  const hasCharts = hasSegments && message.segments!.some(
   243→    (s) => (s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.chart_spec) || (s.type === 'subagent_end' && s.chart_spec)
   244→  );
   245→
   246→  const answerBlockSegments = hasSegments
   247→    ? message.segments!
   248→        .filter((s) =>
   249→          (s.type === 'answer' && s.text?.trim()) ||
   250→          (s.type === 'subagent_end' && !s.chart_spec && !s.sqlResults?.length && s.text?.trim() && !hasAnswer) ||
   251→          (s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.chart_spec) ||
   252→          (s.type === 'subagent_end' && s.chart_spec)
   253→        )
   254→        .map((s) => {
   255→          if ((s.type === 'answer' || (s.type === 'subagent_end' && !s.chart_spec)) && s.text && hasCharts) {
   256→            return { ...s, text: stripChartSpecBlocks(s.text) };
   257→          }
   258→          return s;
   259→        })
   260→        .filter((s) => {
   261→          // Remove text segments that became empty after stripping chart blocks
   262→          if ((s.type === 'answer' || (s.type === 'subagent_end' && !s.chart_spec)) && !s.text?.trim()) {
   263→            return false;
   264→          }
   265→          return true;
   266→        })
   267→    : [];
   268→
   269→  const questionSegments = hasSegments
   270→    ? message.segments!.filter((s) => s.type === 'user_question' && s.questionData)
   271→    : [];
   272→
   273→  return (
   274→    <div className={`message-bubble message-bubble--${message.role}`}>
   275→      <div className="message-bubble__header">
   276→        {isUser ? t('you') : t('assistant')}
   277→        {isUser && !isStreaming && !isEditing && !isConfirmingDelete && (
   278→          <span className="message-bubble__actions">
   279→            <button
   280→              className="message-bubble__action-btn"
   281→              onClick={handleEdit}
   282→              title={t('editMessage')}
   283→            >
   284→              &#9998;
   285→            </button>
   286→            <button
   287→              className="message-bubble__action-btn message-bubble__action-btn--delete"
   288→              onClick={() => setIsConfirmingDelete(true)}
   289→              title={t('deleteMessage')}
   290→            >
   291→              &#128465;
   292→            </button>
   293→          </span>
   294→        )}
   295→      </div>
   296→
   297→      {isUser && isConfirmingDelete && (
   298→        <div className="message-bubble__confirm-delete">
   299→          <span>{t('deleteConfirm')}</span>
   300→          <div className="message-bubble__confirm-actions">
   301→            <button
   302→              className="message-bubble__confirm-btn message-bubble__confirm-btn--delete"
   303→              onClick={handleDeleteConfirm}
   304→            >
   305→              {t('delete')}
   306→            </button>
   307→            <button
   308→              className="message-bubble__confirm-btn"
   309→              onClick={() => setIsConfirmingDelete(false)}
   310→            >
   311→              {t('cancel')}
   312→            </button>
   313→          </div>
   314→        </div>
   315→      )}
   316→
   317→      {isUser && isEditing ? (
   318→        <div className="message-bubble__edit-mode">
   319→          <textarea
   320→            ref={textareaRef}
   321→            className="message-bubble__edit-textarea"
   322→            value={editText}
   323→            onChange={(e) => setEditText(e.target.value)}
   324→            onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
   325→            rows={3}
   326→          />
   327→          <div className="message-bubble__edit-actions">
   328→            <button
   329→              className="message-bubble__edit-btn message-bubble__edit-btn--save"
   330→              onClick={handleSaveEdit}
   331→            >
   332→              {t('saveResend')}
   333→            </button>
   334→            <button
   335→              className="message-bubble__edit-btn"
   336→              onClick={handleCancelEdit}
   337→            >
   338→              {t('cancel')}
   339→            </button>
   340→          </div>
   341→        </div>
   342→      ) : hasSegments ? (
   343→        <div className="message-bubble__segments">
   344→          {!questionSegments.some((s) => !s.userAnswer) && (
   345→            <ThinkingBlock
   346→              segments={message.segments!}
   347→              streamingRemainder={isThinkingPhase ? streamingRemainder : undefined}
   348→              isThinkingPhase={isThinkingPhase}
   349→              isAgentStreaming={!!message.isStreaming}
   350→              hasAnswer={hasAnswer}
   351→            />
   352→          )}
   353→          {questionSegments.map((seg, i) => (
   354→            <div key={`question-${i}`} className="message-bubble__segment message-bubble__segment--question">
   355→              <UserQuestion
   356→                questionData={seg.questionData!}
   357→                userAnswer={seg.userAnswer}
   358→                userFreeText={seg.userFreeText}
   359→              />
   360→            </div>
   361→          ))}
   362→          {answerBlockSegments.length > 0 && (
   363→            <div className="message-bubble__segment message-bubble__segment--answer">
   364→              <div className="message-bubble__segment-label message-bubble__segment-label--answer">{t('answer')}</div>
   365→              {answerBlockSegments.map((seg, i) => {
   366→                // Chart segment (tool with chart_spec or subagent_end with chart_spec)
   367→                if ((seg.type === 'tool' && seg.toolResult?.chart_spec) || (seg.type === 'subagent_end' && seg.chart_spec)) {
   368→                  return (
   369→                    <div key={`chart-${i}`} className="message-bubble__chart-in-answer">
   370→                      {seg.type === 'tool' && seg.toolResult ? (
   371→                        <InlineQueryResult result={seg.toolResult!} />
   372→                      ) : seg.chart_spec ? (
   373→                        <ChartWidget data={seg.chart_spec.data} layout={seg.chart_spec.layout} frames={seg.chart_spec.frames} />
   374→                      ) : null}
   375→                    </div>
   376→                  );
   377→                }
   378→                // Text segment (answer or subagent_end without chart_spec)
   379→                return (
   380→                  <div key={`answer-${i}`} className="message-bubble__segment-content">
   381→                    <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(seg.text!)}</ReactMarkdown>
   382→                  </div>
   383→                );
   384→              })}
   385→              {/* Render streaming remainder inside the same answer block to avoid duplicate headers */}
   386→              {isInAnswerPhase && streamingRemainder?.trim() && (() => {
   387→                const displayText = hasCharts ? stripChartSpecBlocks(streamingRemainder) : streamingRemainder;
   388→                return displayText?.trim() ? (
   389→                  <div className="message-bubble__segment-content">
   390→                    <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(displayText)}</ReactMarkdown>
   391→                  </div>
   392→                ) : null;
   393→              })()}
   394→            </div>
   395→          )}
   396→          {/* Only show standalone streaming answer block when no answer segments exist yet */}
   397→          {answerBlockSegments.length === 0 && isInAnswerPhase && streamingRemainder?.trim() && (() => {
   398→            const displayText = hasCharts ? stripChartSpecBlocks(streamingRemainder) : streamingRemainder;
   399→            return displayText?.trim() ? (
   400→              <div className="message-bubble__segment message-bubble__segment--answer">
   401→                <div className="message-bubble__segment-label message-bubble__segment-label--answer">{t('answer')}</div>
   402→                <div className="message-bubble__segment-content">
   403→                  <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(displayText)}</ReactMarkdown>
   404→                </div>
   405→              </div>
   406→            ) : null;
   407→          })()}
   408→          {message.segments!.filter((s) => s.type === 'error').map((seg, i) => {
   409→            const lastUserMsg = messages.slice().reverse().find((m) => m.role === 'user');
   410→            const handleRetry = lastUserMsg ? () => sendMessage(lastUserMsg.content) : undefined;
   411→            return <ErrorBlock key={`error-${i}`} errorMessage={seg.errorMessage || 'Unknown error'} onRetry={!isStreaming ? handleRetry : undefined} />;
   412→          })}
   413→          {message.isStreaming && !message.content && questionSegments.length === 0 && (
   414→            <span className="message-bubble__typing">{t('thinking')}</span>
   415→          )}
   416→        </div>
   417→      ) : (
   418→        <>
   419→          <div className="message-bubble__content">
   420→            {message.content ? (
   421→              <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(message.content)}</ReactMarkdown>
   422→            ) : message.isStreaming ? (
   423→              <span className="message-bubble__typing">{t('thinking')}</span>
   424→            ) : null}
   425→          </div>
   426→          {message.toolCalls && message.toolCalls.length > 0 && (
   427→            <div className="message-bubble__tools">
   428→              {message.toolCalls.map((tc) => (
   429→                <InlineQueryResult key={tc.toolCallId} result={tc} />
   430→              ))}
   431→            </div>
   432→          )}
   433→        </>
   434→      )}
   435→    </div>
   436→  );
   437→}
   438→

> AGENT

Found the issue. When you switch back to a conversation that's still streaming, `loadMessages` always sets `setIsStreaming(false)` — so the ChatInput shows the normal "ask about your data" placeholder instead of a streaming indicator. The background stream's callbacks are all guarded by the generation counter and become no-ops, so the conversation is stuck showing partial data with no way to get updates.

The root cause is that `loadMessages` lets the background SSE stream continue but resets all frontend state. This creates a zombie stream that can't update the UI.

The fix: **abort the outgoing stream in `loadMessages`** (same as `clearMessages` already does), and mark cached messages as `isStreaming: false` so the state is consistent.

> AGENT

Build succeeds. Let me summarize the changes:

**Root cause:** `loadMessages` (conversation switching) intentionally kept the background SSE stream running and cached messages with `isStreaming: true`. But the stream's callbacks were all invalidated by the generation counter, creating a zombie state — the cached messages showed a loading indicator while nothing was actually updating them. The global `isStreaming` was set to `false`, so the ChatInput showed the normal "ask about your data" placeholder.

**Fix (two changes in `loadMessages`):**

1. **Abort the stream on switch** (line ~672-678): Added `abortRef.current.abort()` so the SSE stream is cleanly terminated when switching conversations. The backend's `finally` block persists whatever text was accumulated, and the sidecar container stays alive for potential session resume.

2. **Cache messages with `isStreaming: false`** (line ~720-731): The cached messages now have `isStreaming: false` and `currentPhase: undefined`, consistent with the global state. This prevents frozen spinners when switching back.

This makes `loadMessages` consistent with `clearMessages` — both abort streams and finalize state. The difference is `clearMessages` empties messages (for "+") while `loadMessages` swaps them (for sidebar clicks).

> AGENT

The logs reveal the root cause. Let me trace exactly what happened:

```
sendMessage proceeding {generation: 1, conversationId: 'a9029aa8...'}  ← stream starts
clearMessages called {outgoingConversationId: 'a9029aa8...'}           ← user clicked "+"
ChatInput sendMessage returned                                         ← stream was ABORTED
// *** NO onDone for generation 1! ***

selectConversation response {messageCount: 1}                          ← backend has ONLY the user message!
```

**Root cause: `clearMessages` calls `abortRef.current.abort()`, which kills the agent stream.** The backend's `finally` block runs but has no text to persist (the agent hadn't generated anything yet). When you switch back, the response is gone — only your question remains.

The fix: **Don't abort the stream.** Let the agent continue in the background. The generation counter already prevents stale callbacks from corrupting state. When the agent finishes, the backend persists the full response.

I also need to revert my earlier wrong fix (adding abort to `loadMessages`) and fix the cache-vs-backend preference logic.

   690→    [userSessionId]
   691→  );
   692→
   693→  const loadMessages = useCallback((msgs: ChatMessage[], outgoingConversationId?: string | null, incomingConversationId?: string | null) => {
   694→    console.log('[CONV-DEBUG] loadMessages called', {
   695→      incomingConversationId,
   696→      outgoingConversationId,
   697→      msgsFromBackend: msgs.length,
   698→      streamConvRef: streamConversationIdRef.current,
   699→      cacheKeys: [...messagesCacheRef.current.keys()],
   700→      cachedForIncoming: messagesCacheRef.current.has(incomingConversationId || ''),
   701→      cachedLen: messagesCacheRef.current.get(incomingConversationId || '')?.length,
   702→      isStreaming,
   703→      abortActive: !!abortRef.current,
   704→    });
   705→    // Invalidate any in-flight stream so its callbacks become no-ops.
   706→    ++streamGenerationRef.current;
   707→
   708→    // Fall back to the stream's conversation ID when React state is stale
   709→    // (e.g. user switches before re-render after createConversation).
   710→    const outgoing = outgoingConversationId || streamConversationIdRef.current;
   711→    activeConversationIdRef.current = incomingConversationId || null;
   712→
   713→    // Abort any in-flight SSE stream so its callbacks don't fire after
   714→    // we switch conversations.  The backend's finally block will persist
   715→    // whatever text the agent accumulated so far.  The sidecar container
   716→    // stays alive for potential session resume on the next message.
   717→    if (abortRef.current) {
   718→      abortRef.current.abort();
   719→      abortRef.current = null;
   720→    }
   721→    if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   722→      clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   723→      flushTimerRef.current = null;
   724→    }
   725→
   726→    // Prefer cached messages when the cache is at least as complete as
   727→    // the backend response (cache keeps rich segments / streaming data).
   728→    let finalMsgs = msgs;
   729→    if (incomingConversationId) {
   730→      const cached = messagesCacheRef.current.get(incomingConversationId);
   731→      if (cached && cached.length > 0 && cached.length >= msgs.length) {
   732→        finalMsgs = cached;
   733→      }
   734→      // Clear used cache entry so stale data doesn't persist forever
   735→      messagesCacheRef.current.delete(incomingConversationId);
   736→    }
   737→
   738→    // Capture ref values NOW — React 18 batching may defer the updater
   739→    // function below until after the ref resets at the bottom of this
   740→    // function, so reading refs inside the updater would see empty values.
   741→    const capturedTextBuffer = textBufferRef.current;
   742→    const capturedAssistantId = assistantIdRef.current;
   743→    const capturedSegments = [...segmentsRef.current];
   744→    const capturedCurrentText = currentTextRef.current;
   745→    const capturedPhase = phaseRef.current;
   746→
   747→    // Save current messages to cache before replacing them.
   748→    setMessages(prev => {
   749→      console.log('[CONV-DEBUG] loadMessages setMessages updater', { outgoing, prevLen: prev.length, finalMsgsLen: finalMsgs.length, prevMsgs: prev.map(m => ({ id: m.id.slice(0,8), role: m.role, isStreaming: m.isStreaming, contentLen: m.content.length })) });
   750→      if (outgoing && prev.length > 0) {
   751→        const pendingText = capturedTextBuffer;
   752→        const aId = capturedAssistantId;
   753→
   754→        // Build finalized segments including any unflushed text
   755→        let finalSegments = [...capturedSegments];
   756→        const pendingSegmentText = capturedCurrentText + pendingText;
   757→        if (pendingSegmentText.trim()) {
   758→          const segType = capturedPhase === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   759→          finalSegments.push({ type: segType as ContentSegment['type'], text: pendingSegmentText });
   760→        }
   761→
   762→        const cleaned = prev.map(m => {
   763→          if (m.id === aId && m.isStreaming) {
   764→            return {
   765→              ...m,
   766→              content: m.content + pendingText,
   767→              isStreaming: false,
   768→              currentPhase: undefined,
   769→              segments: finalSegments.length > 0 ? finalSegments : m.segments,
   770→            };
   771→          }
   772→          return m.isStreaming ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined } : m;
   773→        });
   774→        // Filter out empty assistant messages (no content and no segments).
   775→        const toCache = cleaned.filter(m => {
   776→          if (m.role === 'assistant' && !m.content?.trim() && (!m.segments || m.segments.length === 0)) {
   777→            return false;
   778→          }
   779→          return true;
   780→        });
   781→        messagesCacheRef.current.set(outgoing, toCache);
   782→      }
   783→      return finalMsgs;
   784→    });
   785→
   786→    setIsStreaming(false);
   787→    textBufferRef.current = '';

Same problem. The evidence is clear now:

```
clearMessages called          ← stream NOT aborted (good!)
// ... time passes ...
selectConversation response {messageCount: 1}  ← backend STILL has only 1 message!
```

**No `onDone` for generation 1** — the first stream hasn't finished yet when you switched back. The backend hasn't persisted the response. And the cache filtered out the empty assistant message, so there's nothing to show.

Two issues to fix:

1. **Cache keeps empty streaming assistant** — so user sees "thinking" indicator when switching back
2. **When background stream completes, update the UI** — so the response appears automatically

Let me fix the caching in both `clearMessages` and `loadMessages`:

> AGENT

I understand — you can see the final result only after clicking again, but not the streaming progress. That's because the background stream's callbacks are all no-ops (generation counter guard), so no live updates.

The fix I'm implementing now will:
1. Show "waiting for response" in the ChatInput when viewing a conversation with an active stream
2. Auto-update the UI when the background stream finishes (re-fetch from backend in stale `onDone`)

This means you'll see "thinking..." while the agent works, then the full response appears automatically — no need to click again.

Let me implement this. First, I'll create a shared utility to build ChatMessages from backend data:

> TOOL

tool_use Write
id: toolu_01FyVRQ75xpP9K3ahDZQeTTE
```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/implement-memory/frontend/src/utils/buildChatMessages.ts",
  "content": "import type { ChatMessage, ContentSegment } from '../types';\n\ninterface BackendMessage {\n  id: string;\n  role: string;\n  content: string;\n  metadata: string | null;\n}\n\n/**\n * Convert backend message objects into ChatMessage[] for the UI.\n * Shared by ConversationContext.selectConversation and AgentContext stale-onDone refresh.\n */\nexport function buildChatMessages(messages: BackendMessage[]): ChatMessage[] {\n  return messages.map((msg) => {\n    if (msg.role !== 'assistant') {\n      return { id: msg.id, role: 'user' as const, content: msg.content };\n    }\n\n    // Try to restore segments from metadata\n    let segments: ContentSegment[] | undefined;\n    if (msg.metadata) {\n      try {\n        const meta = typeof msg.metadata === 'string' ? JSON.parse(msg.metadata) : msg.metadata;\n        if (Array.isArray(meta?.segments)) {\n          segments = (meta.segments as Array<Record<string, unknown>>).map((seg) => {\n            const s: ContentSegment = { type: seg.type as ContentSegment['type'] };\n            if (seg.text) s.text = seg.text as string;\n            if (seg.toolCallId || seg.toolName) {\n              s.toolResult = {\n                toolCallId: (seg.toolCallId as string) || '',\n                toolName: seg.toolName as string,\n                sql: (seg.sql as string) || '',\n                columns: [],\n                rows: [],\n                rowCount: (seg.rowCount as number) || 0,\n                error: seg.error as string | undefined,\n                chart_spec: seg.chart_spec as ContentSegment['chart_spec'],\n              };\n            }\n            if (seg.subagentId) s.subagentId = seg.subagentId as string;\n            if (seg.subagentName) s.subagentName = seg.subagentName as string;\n            if (seg.sqlResults) s.sqlResults = seg.sqlResults as ContentSegment['sqlResults'];\n            if (seg.chart_spec && seg.type === 'subagent_end') {\n              s.chart_spec = seg.chart_spec as ContentSegment['chart_spec'];\n            }\n            return s;\n          });\n        }\n      } catch {\n        // metadata parse failed, fall through to default\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Fallback: single answer segment from content\n    if (!segments) {\n      segments = [{ type: 'answer' as const, text: msg.content }];\n    }\n\n    return {\n      id: msg.id,\n      role: 'assistant' as const,\n      content: msg.content,\n      segments,\n    };\n  });\n}\n"
}
```

}

  "new_string": "          onDone: (newSessionId) => {\n            console.log('[CONV-DEBUG] onDone fired', { conversationId, generation, currentGen: streamGenerationRef.current, stale: streamGenerationRef.current !== generation, newSessionId });\n            // If this stream's generation is stale (user switched conversations\n            // or started a new stream), don't touch shared state — just clear\n            // the stale cache so the next switch loads the full backend response.\n            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) {\n              if (conversationId) messagesCacheRef.current.delete(conversationId);\n              // If user is currently viewing this conversation, re-fetch from\n              // backend so the completed response appears automatically.\n              if (conversationId && activeConversationIdRef.current === conversationId) {\n                // Small delay to let the backend's finally block persist the response.\n                setTimeout(() => {\n                  if (activeConversationIdRef.current !== conversationId) return;\n                  console.log('[CONV-DEBUG] stale onDone re-fetching', { conversationId });\n                  fetch(`/api/conversations/${conversationId}`)\n                    .then(res => res.ok ? res.json() : null)\n                    .then(conv => {\n                      if (!conv?.messages || activeConversationIdRef.current !== conversationId) return;\n                      setMessages(buildChatMessages(conv.messages));\n                      setIsStreaming(false);\n                    })\n                    .catch(() => {});\n                }, 300);\n              }\n              return;\n            }"
}

    19→export function AgentProvider({

    43→  // Track which conversation is currently displayed so background streams can detect they're stale

    52→
    53→  const flushText = useCallback(() => {

    62→    );
    63→    textBufferRef.current = '';

    66→  const sendMessage = useCallback(

    69→      if (isStreaming) { console.log('[CONV-DEBUG] sendMessage BLOCKED by isStreaming'); return; }

    73→
    74→      const userMsg: ChatMessage = {

    97→      // Build conversation history from current messages for resume fallback.

   100→      const history = messagesRef.current

   104→      // If there's pending history from a delete, start a new Langfuse session with that context
   105→      const pendingHistory = pendingHistoryRef.current;

   112→      // Extract all /skill-name references from message

   116→      if (slashMatches.length > 0) {

   119→      }

   149→              currentTextRef.current = '';

   151→            phaseRef.current = 'answer';
   152→            setMessages((prev) =>
   153→              prev.map((m) =>
   154→                m.id === assistantId
   155→                  ? { ...m, currentPhase: 'answer', segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }

   578→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   579→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   580→            }
   581→            flushText();
   582→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   583→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'answer', text: currentTextRef.current });
   584→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   585→            }
   586→            setMessages((prev) =>
   587→              prev.map((m) =>
   588→                m.id === assistantId
   589→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   590→                  : m
   591→              )
   592→            );
   593→            setIsStreaming(false);
   594→            abortRef.current = null;
   595→          },
   596→          onError: (error) => {
   597→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   598→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   599→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   600→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   601→            }
   602→            flushText();
   603→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   604→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   605→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   606→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   607→            }
   608→            segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'error', errorMessage: error });
   609→            setMessages((prev) =>
   610→              prev.map((m) =>
   611→                m.id === assistantId
   612→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   613→                  : m
   614→              )
   615→            );
   616→            setIsStreaming(false);
   617→            abortRef.current = null;
   618→          },
   619→          onUserQuestion: (data) => {
   620→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   621→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   622→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   623→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   624→            }
   625→            flushText();
   626→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   627→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   628→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   629→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   630→            }
   631→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   632→              type: 'user_question',
   633→              questionData: data,
   634→            });
   635→            setMessages((prev) =>
   636→              prev.map((m) =>
   637→                m.id === assistantId
   638→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   639→                  : m
   640→              )
   641→            );
   642→          },
   643→        },
   644→        controller.signal,
   645→        userSessionId,
   646→        convId,
   647→      );
   648→    },
   649→    [isStreaming, messages, flushText, refreshTables, userSessionId]
   650→  );
   651→
   652→  const deleteMessage = useCallback(
   653→    (messageIndex: number) => {
   654→      if (isStreaming) return;
   655→
   656→      // Store remaining messages as history for Langfuse context on next send
   657→      setMessages((prev) => {
   658→        const remaining = prev.slice(0, messageIndex);
   659→        pendingHistoryRef.current = remaining
   660→          .filter((m) => m.role === 'user' || m.role === 'assistant')
   661→          .map((m) => ({ role: m.role, content: m.content }));
   662→        return remaining;
   663→      });
   664→
   665→      // Persist the truncation to the backend
   666→      const convId = activeConversationIdRef.current;
   667→      if (convId) {
   668→        fetch(`/api/conversations/${convId}/messages?from_sort_order=${messageIndex}`, {
   669→          method: 'DELETE',
   670→        }).catch(() => {
   671→          // Best-effort: if this fails the UI is already updated
   672→        });
   673→        // Invalidate cache so switching back loads from backend
   674→        messagesCacheRef.current.delete(convId);
   675→      }
   676→
   677→      // Clear session so next message starts fresh
   678→      sessionIdRef.current = null;
   679→    },
   680→    [isStreaming]
   681→  );
   682→
   683→  const respondToQuestion = useCallback(
   684→    async (questionId: string, answers: string[], freeText?: string) => {
   685→      // Update the segment to show the user's answer
   686→      const segIdx = segmentsRef.current.findIndex(
   687→        (s) => s.type === 'user_question' && s.questionData?.questionId === questionId
   688→      );
   689→      if (segIdx !== -1) {
   690→        segmentsRef.current[segIdx] = {
   691→          ...segmentsRef.current[segIdx],
   692→          userAnswer: answers,
   693→          userFreeText: freeText,
   694→        };
   695→        setMessages((prev) =>
   696→          prev.map((m) =>
   697→            m.id === assistantIdRef.current
   698→              ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   699→              : m
   700→          )
   701→        );
   702→      }
   703→
   704→      // POST to backend
   705→      try {
   706→        await fetch('/api/chat/respond', {
   707→          method: 'POST',
   708→          headers: {
   709→            'Content-Type': 'application/json',

   711→          },
   712→          body: JSON.stringify({

   714→            answers,

   717→        });
   718→      } catch (e) {
   719→        console.error('Failed to respond to question:', e);
   720→      }
   721→    },
   722→    [userSessionId]
   723→  );
   724→
   725→  const loadMessages = useCallback((msgs: ChatMessage[], outgoingConversationId?: string | null, incomingConversationId?: string | null) => {
   726→    console.log('[CONV-DEBUG] loadMessages called', {
   727→      incomingConversationId,
   728→      outgoingConversationId,
   729→      msgsFromBackend: msgs.length,
   730→      streamConvRef: streamConversationIdRef.current,
   731→      cacheKeys: [...messagesCacheRef.current.keys()],
   732→      cachedForIncoming: messagesCacheRef.current.has(incomingConversationId || ''),
   733→      cachedLen: messagesCacheRef.current.get(incomingConversationId || '')?.length,
   734→      isStreaming,
   735→      abortActive: !!abortRef.current,
   736→    });
   737→    // Invalidate any in-flight stream so its callbacks become no-ops.
   738→    ++streamGenerationRef.current;
   739→
   740→    // Fall back to the stream's conversation ID when React state is stale
   741→    // (e.g. user switches before re-render after createConversation).
   742→    const outgoing = outgoingConversationId || streamConversationIdRef.current;
   743→    activeConversationIdRef.current = incomingConversationId || null;
   744→
   745→    // Do NOT abort the SSE stream — let the agent finish in the background.
   746→    // The generation counter ensures stale callbacks are no-ops.
   747→    // The backend's finally block will persist the full response.
   748→    // When the user switches back, the backend has the complete data.
   749→    if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   750→      clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   751→      flushTimerRef.current = null;
   752→    }
   753→
   754→    // Use cache only when it has MORE messages than backend (meaning the
   755→    // backend hasn't persisted the assistant response yet).  When counts
   756→    // are equal, prefer backend — it has the final persisted data which
   757→    // may be more complete than the frozen cache snapshot.
   758→    let finalMsgs = msgs;
   759→    if (incomingConversationId) {
   760→      const cached = messagesCacheRef.current.get(incomingConversationId);
   761→      if (cached && cached.length > 0 && cached.length > msgs.length) {
   762→        finalMsgs = cached;
   763→      }
   764→      // Clear used cache entry so stale data doesn't persist forever
   765→      messagesCacheRef.current.delete(incomingConversationId);
   766→    }
   767→
   768→    // Capture ref values NOW — React 18 batching may defer the updater
   769→    // function below until after the ref resets at the bottom of this
   770→    // function, so reading refs inside the updater would see empty values.
   771→    const capturedTextBuffer = textBufferRef.current;
   772→    const capturedAssistantId = assistantIdRef.current;
   773→    const capturedSegments = [...segmentsRef.current];
   774→    const capturedCurrentText = currentTextRef.current;
   775→    const capturedPhase = phaseRef.current;
   776→
   777→    // Save current messages to cache before replacing them.
   778→    setMessages(prev => {
   779→      console.log('[CONV-DEBUG] loadMessages setMessages updater', { outgoing, prevLen: prev.length, finalMsgsLen: finalMsgs.length, prevMsgs: prev.map(m => ({ id: m.id.slice(0,8), role: m.role, isStreaming: m.isStreaming, contentLen: m.content.length })) });
   780→      if (outgoing && prev.length > 0) {
   781→        const pendingText = capturedTextBuffer;
   782→        const aId = capturedAssistantId;
   783→
   784→        // Build finalized segments including any unflushed text
   785→        let finalSegments = [...capturedSegments];
   786→        const pendingSegmentText = capturedCurrentText + pendingText;
   787→        if (pendingSegmentText.trim()) {
   788→          const segType = capturedPhase === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   789→          finalSegments.push({ type: segType as ContentSegment['type'], text: pendingSegmentText });
   790→        }
   791→
   792→        const cleaned = prev.map(m => {
   793→          if (m.id === aId && m.isStreaming) {
   794→            return {
   795→              ...m,
   796→              content: m.content + pendingText,
   797→              // Keep isStreaming flag — the background stream continues.
   798→              segments: finalSegments.length > 0 ? finalSegments : m.segments,
   799→            };
   800→          }
   801→          return m;
   802→        });
   803→        // Filter out empty NON-streaming assistant messages.
   804→        // Keep streaming ones even if empty — they show a "thinking" indicator.
   805→        const toCache = cleaned.filter(m => {
   806→          if (m.role === 'assistant' && !m.isStreaming && !m.content?.trim() && (!m.segments || m.segments.length === 0)) {
   807→            return false;
   808→          }
   809→          return true;
   810→        });
   811→        messagesCacheRef.current.set(outgoing, toCache);
   812→      }
   813→      return finalMsgs;
   814→    });
   815→
   816→    // If the loaded messages include a streaming assistant (background stream
   817→    // still running), keep isStreaming = true so the ChatInput shows "waiting".
   818→    // The stale onDone will set isStreaming = false when the stream completes.
   819→    const hasStreamingMsg = finalMsgs.some(m => m.isStreaming);
   820→    setIsStreaming(hasStreamingMsg);
   821→    textBufferRef.current = '';
   822→    currentTextRef.current = '';
   823→    segmentsRef.current = [];
   824→    assistantIdRef.current = '';
   825→    phaseRef.current = 'thinking';
   826→    sessionIdRef.current = null;
   827→    pendingHistoryRef.current = null;
   828→  }, []);
   829→
   830→  const clearMessages = useCallback((outgoingConversationId?: string | null) => {
   831→    console.log('[CONV-DEBUG] clearMessages called', { outgoingConversationId, streamConvRef: streamConversationIdRef.current, cacheKeys: [...messagesCacheRef.current.keys()], isStreaming });
   832→    ++streamGenerationRef.current;
   833→    const outgoing = outgoingConversationId || streamConversationIdRef.current;
   834→    activeConversationIdRef.current = null;
   835→    // Do NOT abort the SSE stream — let the agent finish in the background.
   836→    // The generation counter ensures stale callbacks are no-ops.
   837→    // The backend's finally block will persist the full response.
   838→    // When the user switches back, the backend has the complete data.
   839→    if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   840→      clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   841→      flushTimerRef.current = null;
   842→    }
   843→
   844→    // Capture refs before setMessages (React 18 batching may defer the updater)
   845→    const capturedTextBuffer = textBufferRef.current;
   846→    const capturedAssistantId = assistantIdRef.current;
   847→    const capturedSegments = [...segmentsRef.current];
   848→    const capturedCurrentText = currentTextRef.current;
   849→    const capturedPhase = phaseRef.current;
   850→
   851→    setMessages(prev => {
   852→      if (outgoing && prev.length > 0) {
   853→        const pendingText = capturedTextBuffer;
   854→        const aId = capturedAssistantId;
   855→        let finalSegments = [...capturedSegments];
   856→        const pendingSegmentText = capturedCurrentText + pendingText;
   857→        if (pendingSegmentText.trim()) {
   858→          const segType = capturedPhase === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   859→          finalSegments.push({ type: segType as ContentSegment['type'], text: pendingSegmentText });
   860→        }
   861→        const cleaned = prev.map(m => {
   862→          if (m.id === aId && m.isStreaming) {
   863→            return {
   864→              ...m,
   865→              content: m.content + pendingText,
   866→              // Keep isStreaming flag — the background stream continues.
   867→              segments: finalSegments.length > 0 ? finalSegments : m.segments,
   868→            };
   869→          }
   870→          return m;
   871→        });
   872→        // Filter out empty NON-streaming assistant messages.
   873→        // Keep streaming ones even if empty — they show a "thinking" indicator.
   874→        const toCache = cleaned.filter(m => {
   875→          if (m.role === 'assistant' && !m.isStreaming && !m.content?.trim() && (!m.segments || m.segments.length === 0)) {
   876→            return false;
   877→          }
   878→          return true;
   879→        });
   880→        messagesCacheRef.current.set(outgoing, toCache);
   881→      }
   882→      return [];
   883→    });
   884→    setIsStreaming(false);
   885→    textBufferRef.current = '';
   886→    currentTextRef.current = '';
   887→    segmentsRef.current = [];
   888→    assistantIdRef.current = '';
   889→    phaseRef.current = 'thinking';
   890→    sessionIdRef.current = null;
   891→    pendingHistoryRef.current = null;
   892→  }, []);
   893→
   894→  return (
   895→    <AgentContext.Provider
   896→      value={{ messages, isStreaming, sendMessage, editMessage, deleteMessage, clearMessages, loadMessages, respondToQuestion }}
   897→    >
   898→      {children}
   899→    </AgentContext.Provider>
   900→  );
   901→}
   902→

> AGENT

Build compiles cleanly. Here's a summary of where we are:

**Fix attempt 4 is ready to test.** Key changes from the previous attempt:

1. **Cached streaming messages keep `isStreaming: true`** - so when you switch to a background-streaming conversation, the assistant message still shows the thinking indicator
2. **Global `isStreaming` is set from cached messages** - `setIsStreaming(finalMsgs.some(m => m.isStreaming))` means ChatInput shows "waiting for response" instead of "ask about your data"
3. **Stale `onDone` auto-refreshes** - when the background stream completes and you're viewing that conversation, it re-fetches from the backend after 300ms and shows the final result automatically

**Expected behavior when you test:**
- Send message in conv1 → switch to new conv → send message in conv2
- Switch to conv1: should see the user question + thinking indicator + "waiting for response" in chat input
- When conv1's stream finishes: the final response should automatically appear (no need to click again)
- Same behavior for conv2

**Known limitation**: While viewing a background-streaming conversation, you'll see a frozen "thinking" state rather than live text appearing character-by-character. The final result pops in once the stream completes. This is because the SSE callbacks are tied to one active stream, and supporting live cross-conversation updates would require a significant architectural refactor.

Please test and let me know how it goes.

     1→import type { ToolCallResult, UserQuestionData, UserQuestionOption } from '../types';
     2→
     3→interface AgentCallbacks {
     4→  onTextChunk: (text: string) => void;
     5→  onThinkingDone: () => void;
     6→  onToolCall: (pending: ToolCallResult) => void;
     7→  onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => void;
     8→  onSubagentStart?: (data: { id: string; name: string; prompt: string }) => void;
     9→  onSubagentEnd?: (data: { id: string; name: string; result?: string; sql_results?: Array<{ sql: string; columns?: string[]; rows?: Record<string, unknown>[]; rowCount?: number; error?: string }>; chart_spec?: { data: unknown[]; layout?: Record<string, unknown> } }) => void;
    10→  onDone: (sessionId: string | null) => void;
    11→  onError: (error: string) => void;
    12→  onUserQuestion?: (data: UserQuestionData) => void;
    13→}
    14→
    15→export type { AgentCallbacks };
    16→
    17→async function streamSSE(
    18→  response: Response,
    19→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
    20→  signal?: AbortSignal,
    21→): Promise<void> {
    22→  const reader = response.body?.getReader();
    23→  if (!reader) {
    24→    callbacks.onError('No response stream');
    25→    return;
    26→  }
    27→
    28→  let doneReceived = false;
    29→  const wrappedCallbacks: AgentCallbacks = {
    30→    ...callbacks,
    31→    onDone: (sessionId) => {
    32→      doneReceived = true;
    33→      callbacks.onDone(sessionId);
    34→    },
    35→    onError: (error) => {
    36→      doneReceived = true;
    37→      callbacks.onError(error);
    38→    },
    39→  };
    40→
    41→  try {
    42→    const decoder = new TextDecoder();
    43→    let buffer = '';
    44→    let eventType = '';
    45→
    46→    while (true) {
    47→      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
    48→      if (done) break;
    49→
    50→      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
    51→      const lines = buffer.split('\n');
    52→      buffer = lines.pop() ?? '';
    53→      for (const line of lines) {
    54→        if (line.startsWith('event: ')) {
    55→          eventType = line.slice(7).trim();
    56→        } else if (line.startsWith('data: ')) {
    57→          const data = line.slice(6);
    58→          try {
    59→            const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
    60→            handleSSEEvent(eventType, parsed, wrappedCallbacks);
    61→          } catch {
    62→            // Skip malformed JSON
    63→          }
    64→          eventType = '';
    65→        }
    66→      }
    67→    }
    68→
    69→    // Process any remaining data left in buffer after stream ends
    70→    if (buffer.trim()) {
    71→      const remainingLines = buffer.split('\n');
    72→      for (const line of remainingLines) {
    73→        if (line.startsWith('event: ')) {
    74→          eventType = line.slice(7).trim();
    75→        } else if (line.startsWith('data: ')) {
    76→          const data = line.slice(6);
    77→          try {
    78→            const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
    79→            handleSSEEvent(eventType, parsed, wrappedCallbacks);
    80→          } catch {
    81→            // Skip malformed JSON
    82→          }
    83→          eventType = '';
    84→        }
    85→      }
    86→    }
    87→
    88→    // Safety net: if stream ended without done/error, force done
    89→    if (!doneReceived) {
    90→      callbacks.onDone(null);
    91→    }
    92→  } catch (e: unknown) {
    93→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
    94→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
    95→    callbacks.onError(msg);
    96→  }
    97→}
    98→
    99→export async function runAgentLoop(
   100→  message: string,
   101→  agentSessionId: string | null,
   102→  langfuseSessionId: string | null,
   103→  conversationHistory: { role: string; content: string }[] | null,
   104→  conversationId?: string | null,
   105→  callbacks?: AgentCallbacks,
   106→  signal?: AbortSignal,
   107→  userSessionId?: string,
   108→  skills?: string[],
   109→): Promise<void> {
   110→  if (!callbacks) return;
   111→  try {
   112→    const response = await fetch('/api/chat', {
   113→      method: 'POST',
   114→      headers: {
   115→        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   116→        ...(userSessionId ? { 'X-Session-ID': userSessionId } : {}),
   117→      },
   118→      body: JSON.stringify({
   119→        message,
   120→        session_id: agentSessionId,
   121→        conversation_id: conversationId,
   122→        langfuse_session_id: langfuseSessionId,
   123→        conversation_history: conversationHistory ?? [],
   124→        ...(skills?.length ? { skills } : {}),
   125→      }),
   126→      signal,
   127→    });
   128→
   129→    if (!response.ok) {
   130→      const errorText = await response.text();
   131→      callbacks.onError(`Server error: ${errorText}`);
   132→      return;
   133→    }
   134→
   135→    await streamSSE(response, callbacks, signal);
   136→  } catch (e: unknown) {
   137→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
   138→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
   139→    callbacks.onError(msg);
   140→  }
   141→}
   142→
   143→export async function runAgentEditLoop(
   144→  newMessage: string,
   145→  conversationHistory: { role: string; content: string }[],
   146→  langfuseSessionId: string | null,
   147→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,

   149→  userSessionId?: string,

   151→): Promise<void> {

   153→    const response = await fetch('/api/chat/edit', {

   156→        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   157→        ...(userSessionId ? { 'X-Session-ID': userSessionId } : {}),
   158→      },
   159→      body: JSON.stringify({
   160→        new_message: newMessage,

   168→    if (!response.ok) {
   169→      const errorText = await response.text();

   174→    await streamSSE(response, callbacks, signal);
   175→  } catch (e: unknown) {
   176→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
   177→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
   178→    callbacks.onError(msg);
   179→  }
   180→}
   181→
   182→function handleSSEEvent(
   183→  eventType: string,
   184→  data: Record<string, unknown>,
   185→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
   186→) {
   187→  switch (eventType) {
   188→    case 'thinking':
   189→    case 'answer':
   190→      callbacks.onTextChunk(data.text as string);
   191→      break;
   192→    case 'thinking_done':
   193→      callbacks.onThinkingDone();
   194→      break;
   195→    case 'tool_call':
   196→      callbacks.onToolCall({
   197→        toolCallId: (data.id as string) ?? '',
   198→        toolName: (data.name as string) ?? undefined,
   199→        sql: (data.sql as string) ?? '',
   200→        command: (data.command as string) ?? undefined,
   201→        toolInput: (data.input as Record<string, unknown>) ?? undefined,
   202→        columns: [],
   203→        rows: [],
   204→        rowCount: 0,
   205→      });
   206→      break;
   207→    case 'tool_result': {
   208→      const result: ToolCallResult = {
   209→        toolCallId: (data.id as string) ?? '',
   210→        toolName: (data.name as string) ?? undefined,
   211→        sql: (data.sql as string) ?? '',
   212→        columns: (data.columns as string[]) ?? [],
   213→        rows: (data.rows as Record<string, unknown>[]) ?? [],
   214→        rowCount: (data.rowCount as number) ?? 0,
   215→        error: (data.error as string) ?? undefined,
   216→        output: (data.output as string) ?? undefined,
   217→        rawContent: (data.content as string) ?? undefined,
   218→        chart_spec: (data.chart_spec as { data: unknown[]; layout?: Record<string, unknown> }) ?? undefined,
   219→      };
   220→      callbacks.onToolResult(result);
   221→      break;
   222→    }
   223→    case 'subagent_start':
   224→      callbacks.onSubagentStart?.({
   225→        id: data.id as string,
   226→        name: data.name as string,
   227→        prompt: (data.prompt as string) ?? '',
   228→      });
   229→      break;
   230→    case 'subagent_end': {
   231→      type ChartSpec = { data: unknown[]; layout?: Record<string, unknown>; frames?: unknown[] };
   232→      // Backend sends chart_specs (array) for multi-chart support.
   233→      // Fall back to legacy chart_spec (single object) for backwards compat.
   234→      const rawSpecs = data.chart_specs as ChartSpec[] | undefined;
   235→      const legacySpec = data.chart_spec as ChartSpec | undefined;
   236→      const chartSpecs: ChartSpec[] = rawSpecs ?? (legacySpec ? [legacySpec] : []);
   237→
   238→      if (chartSpecs.length > 0) {
   239→        // Emit one callback per chart so each gets its own segment
   240→        for (const spec of chartSpecs) {
   241→          callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
   242→            id: data.id as string,
   243→            name: data.name as string,
   244→            chart_spec: spec,
   245→          });
   246→        }
   247→        // Emit text result separately (if any) so it doesn't get lost
   248→        if (data.result) {
   249→          callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
   250→            id: data.id as string,
   251→            name: data.name as string,
   252→            result: data.result as string,
   253→          });
   254→        }
   255→      } else {
   256→        callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
   257→          id: data.id as string,
   258→          name: data.name as string,
   259→          result: (data.result as string) ?? undefined,
   260→          sql_results: (data.sql_results as Array<{ sql: string; columns?: string[]; rows?: Record<string, unknown>[]; rowCount?: number; error?: string }>) ?? undefined,
   261→        });
   262→      }
   263→      break;
   264→    }
   265→    case 'user_question': {
   266→      // Normalize options: the agent may send plain strings or {label, description} objects
   267→      const rawOptions = (data.options as unknown[]) ?? [];
   268→      const normalizedOptions: UserQuestionOption[] = (Array.isArray(rawOptions) ? rawOptions : []).map(
   269→        (opt: unknown) => {
   270→          if (typeof opt === 'string') return { label: opt };
   271→          if (typeof opt === 'object' && opt !== null && 'label' in opt) return opt as UserQuestionOption;
   272→          return { label: String(opt) };
   273→        }
   274→      );
   275→      callbacks.onUserQuestion?.({
   276→        questionId: data.question_id as string,
   277→        question: data.question as string,
   278→        options: normalizedOptions,
   279→        multiSelect: (data.multi_select as boolean) ?? false,
   280→      });
   281→      break;
   282→    }
   283→    case 'done':
   284→      callbacks.onDone((data.session_id as string) ?? null);
   285→      break;
   286→    case 'error':
   287→      callbacks.onError((data.message as string) ?? 'Unknown error');
   288→      break;
   289→  }
   290→}
   291→
```

Perfect! Now I can provide you with the exact findings:

## Race Condition Analysis: Done Event vs Database Persistence

### The Problem: YES, There IS a Race Condition

**The "done" event is yielded BEFORE the finally block runs**, creating a critical race condition:

#### Timeline of Events:

1. **Line 675**: `done_sent = True` + yield `"event: done"` happens when `msg_type == "result"` arrives from the sidecar
   ```python
   elif msg_type == "result":
       actual_session_id = msg.get("session_id") or actual_session_id
       if msg.get("is_error"):
           errors = msg.get("errors", [])
           error_text = msg.get("result") or "; ".join(errors) or "Unknown error"
           yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': error_text})}\n\n"
       yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
       done_sent = True
   ```

2. **Lines 693-696**: Fallback done event (if sidecar closes without a result message)
   ```python
   if not done_sent:
       logger.warning("Sidecar stream ended without result message; sending done event")
       yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': 'Connection to agent was lost. Please try again.'})}\n\n"
       yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   ```

3. **Finally Block (Lines 701-729)**: Database persistence happens AFTER the try block completes
   ```python
   finally:
       # Persist assistant message even when the client disconnects...
       text_to_persist = assistant_text_parts or all_text_parts
       if conversation_id and text_to_persist:
           try:
               # ... segment finalization ...
               memory_store.add_message(
                   conversation_id, "assistant", "".join(text_to_persist),
                   metadata=meta if meta else None,
               )
           except Exception:
               logger.warning("Failed to persist assistant message", exc_info=True)
   ```

### The Race Condition Details:

**The client receives the `"done"` event (line 675 or 696) while the generator is still active.** The SSE stream yields the done event, which is buffered and flushed to the client, but the finally block doesn't execute until the generator exits. This means:

- Client sees "done" event
- Client may immediately start querying for the conversation
- But `memory_store.add_message()` (line 724) hasn't executed yet
- The assistant message isn't in the database when the client looks for it

### Why This Happens:

The `stream_chat()` function is an `AsyncIterator` that uses `yield`. The finally block is guaranteed to run when the generator exits, which happens AFTER all yields are consumed. But yields flush to the client immediately via HTTP streaming, creating a window where the client thinks the response is complete but it hasn't been persisted.

### The Flow Diagram:

```
Client                Backend Generator
                      |
                      | (streaming...)
                      |
                      | yield "event: done" (line 675)
                      |──────────────────────────> Client receives done
                      |
Client thinks         | (generator still running)
done, may query       |
for message           | (client request may arrive here)
                      |
                      | Generator exits
                      | finally block runs (line 701)
                      | memory_store.add_message() (line 724)
                      |
                      v (now in database)
```

### Mitigation Strategies:

To fix this, you'd need to:
1. **Move persistence inside the try block** before yielding the done event
2. **Add a post-done event** after the finally block completes (requires restructuring the generator)
3. **Use a synchronous persist-then-yield pattern** where you guarantee the database write before sending done
